Motivation: 视觉ICL通过支持图像-标签对演示输入输出映射，适合标注稀缺的医学影像，但模型可能过度依赖单个配对而非整体任务语义，这种依赖在临床标注错配或错配准场景下会导致性能崩溃，因此需要诊断并规制该配对依赖。

Method: 提出测试时“错排(derangement)”扰动：保持查询、支持图像与标签多重集不变，仅将每个支持标签重新分配给另一支持图像，由此定义配对间隙(pairing gap = 打乱后性能−匹配后性能)。进一步提出LUC课程：先进行匹配训练，再施加随机解除配对(将支持标签替换为同一episode中其他支持的标签)。并设计反向课程对照以验证顺序效应。

Result: 四个公开模型均表现出配对依赖但程度差异悬殊。配对训练的模型存在支持相关伪区域与病灶尺寸偏置，并对错配准标签敏感。LUC在两个主干上几乎消除配对间隙，在所有任务类型上维持或提升匹配性能，并泛化到留出任务与跨数据集episode。BraTS全肿瘤分割中，匹配支持DSC由0.733升至0.857，间隙由−0.184缩至−0.008。反向课程则残留大间隙。

Conclusion: 视觉ICL的配对依赖由训练顺序而非解除配对训练量决定，LUC通过“先匹配后解除配对”的课程有效规制该依赖并缓解相关失败模式。

Abstract: Visual in-context learning (ICL), well suited to label-scarce medical imaging, uses support image-label pairs to demonstrate input-output mappings, while the labels collectively indicate the requested task. We diagnose dependence on individual pairings with a test-time derangement that reassigns every support label to another support image while preserving the query, support images, and label multiset. The resulting pairing gap, defined as shuffled-minus-matched performance, shows that all four released models depend on the pairing, to widely varying degrees. Further analysis of a paired-trained model reveals support-associated spurious regions and lesion-size biases even with real, unaltered supports, alongside sensitivity to mis-registered support labels. To regulate this dependence, we introduce a late unpairing curriculum (LUC), which starts with matched training and then applies random unpairing, replacing each support label with that of another support in the same episode. LUC nearly closes the pairing gap on two backbones while maintaining or improving matched-support performance across all evaluated task types, with gains extending to held-out tasks and cross-dataset episodes. It also mitigates these failure modes. On BraTS whole-tumor segmentation, matched-support DSC rises from 0.733 to 0.857 while the gap shrinks from -0.184 to -0.008. In a released model, brief fine-tuning with random unpairing reduces the gap. A reversed curriculum that places the same number of unpairing epochs at the start of training leaves a large gap. This shows that pairing dependence is shaped by the order of training and not only by the amount of unpaired training.

</details>


### [137] [Position Forcing: Self-Conditioning 3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10342)
*Ziheng Ouyang,Zeqiang Lai,Jiarui Chen,Jiangshan Wang,Yuhao Wan,Jingbo Gong,Xiangyu Yue,Hengshuang Zhao,Qibin Hou,Chunchao Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Position Forcing：从VecSet潜变量中恢复token位置并按去噪阶段由粗到细量化回注，为单阶段3D生成提供位置自条件，无需独立位置生成阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单阶段3D生成模型多采用VecSet表示，将形状编码为无序潜token集合。与提供显式位置引导的两阶段方法相比，这类模型必须在整个去噪过程中隐式推断token位置，限制生成质量。作者观察到即使没有显式位置条件，VecSet token仍保留可恢复的空间对应关系，因此希望利用这一性质弥补位置引导的缺失。

Method: 提出Position Forcing，一种基于位置的自条件框架。去噪时从当前干净潜变量估计中恢复token位置，依据去噪阶段在渐进更细的分辨率上进行量化，并将所得位置编码回注到扩散Transformer中，形成渐进细化的位置反馈，沿由粗到细轨迹引导形状生成。

Result: 实验表明，Position Forcing在单阶段3D生成方法中取得强性能，并优于若干有竞争力的多阶段方法。

Conclusion: 隐式位置信息的显式化回注是提升单阶段3D生成质量的有效途径，可在不增加独立位置生成阶段的前提下获得接近或超过多阶段方法的生成效果。

Abstract: Recent single-stage 3D generative models commonly adopt VecSet representations, encoding 3D shapes as unordered sets of latent tokens. However, compared with two-stage methods that provide explicit positional guidance, these models must implicitly infer token positions throughout denoising, limiting their generation quality. We observe that, despite the absence of explicit positional conditioning, VecSet tokens retain recoverable spatial correspondences. Building on this observation, we propose Position Forcing, a position-based self-conditioning framework. During denoising, Position Forcing recovers token positions from the current clean latent estimate, quantizes them at progressively finer resolutions according to the denoising stage, and feeds the resulting positional encodings back into the diffusion Transformer. This progressively refined positional feedback provides spatial guidance at a granularity appropriate to each denoising stage, guiding shape generation along a coarse-to-fine trajectory and substantially improving generation quality without a separate position generation stage. Experiments demonstrate that Position Forcing achieves strong performance among single-stage 3D generative methods and outperforms several competitive multi-stage approaches.

</details>


### [138] [Real-Time Joint Audio-Video Generation by Parallel Adapter Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10343)
*Jingyu Li,Xiaoxiao Xiang,Yiwen Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出并行组合（parallel composition）方案：在冻结的音频-视频扩散骨干上分别外挂因果适配器与少步适配器，二者权重更新近乎正交，可在推理时直接相加，得到实时联合音视频流式生成系统（480×832 约26 fps，30秒稳定生成）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时交互式联合音视频生成需同时具备块自回归注意力（提早出帧）与少步采样（每块开销低）。传统流式视频方法采用链式蒸馏（双向教师→因果学生→少步学生，或反序），后一阶段微调会破坏前一阶段已获得的能力。

Method: 借鉴模型合并思路：在打包的音频-视频骨干上并行获取两种能力。因果适配器针对冻结骨干训练，少步能力由现成少步适配器提供。由于二者作用于不同功能轴，预测并验证其权重更新方向近似正交（训练中无显式正交约束），故可做直和式的并行组合，推理时直接相加，无需联合训练。

Result: 验证了权重更新的近正交性；组合模型图像质量接近双向教师，在多数指标上持平或优于链式基线；流式系统实时生成联合音视频，480×832约26 fps（无量化），可连续生成30秒且图像质量稳定。

Conclusion: 并行组合（适配器直接相加）是替代链式蒸馏的实用方案，可在不联合训练的情况下同时获得少步与流式能力，实现实时联合音视频生成。

Abstract: Deploying a joint audio-video diffusion transformer for real-time, interactive generation normally requires two essential modifications: block-autoregressive attention, so frames can be emitted before the whole clip is finished, and few-step sampling, so each block is cheap. Conventionally, the streaming video literature obtains both capabilities from a chained pipeline. It first distills a bidirectional teacher into a causal student, then into a few-step one, or proceeds in reverse order. Each stage of such a chain fine-tunes the weights the previous one produced, so a later objective can undo an earlier capability. Following the idea of model merging, we show that on a packed audio-video backbone the two capabilities can be acquired in parallel. A causal adapter is trained against the frozen backbone, and an off-the-shelf few-step adapter provides the few-step capability. As the two edit different functional axes, we predict, and then verify, that their weight-update directions are near-orthogonal, without any explicit orthogonality constraint during training. Orthogonal updates should combine without interfering, so parallel composition is a direct sum. The two adapters are simply added at inference, with no joint training, yielding few-step, streaming audio-video whose image quality tracks the bidirectional teacher. Compared to the chained baselines, the composed model matches or beats them on most metrics, making parallel composition a practical approach. The resulting streaming system generates joint audio-video in real time, $\approx$26 fps at $480\times832$ without quantization, and sustains 30 s of continuous generation with stable image quality.

</details>


### [139] [Explicit Geometric Chain-of-Thought for Vision-Language-Action in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10390)
*Xingtai Gui,Yucheng Zhou,Dongqian Guo,Jiahao Gong,Feiyang Tan,Jianbing Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoCoTDrive提出显式几何链式思考框架，将2D决策关键区域的定位与3D几何先验检索交织到自回归轨迹生成中，并构建PlanningGrounding数据集以监督规划相关的区域级定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA自动驾驶模型存在根本性错配：驾驶动作需要精确的3D几何线索，而视觉-语言理解与推理主要在2D语义空间中进行。

Method: 遵循“先用2D思考，用专用3D先验驾驶”范式：先定位与决策关键线索对应的2D区域，再从几何基础模型中采样特征获取局部3D先验；这些局部几何特征被交织进自回归上下文以支持轨迹生成。提出规划相关定位（planning-relevant grounding）这一区域级定位任务，并构建PlanningGrounding数据集。

Result: 在多个端到端自动驾驶基准上，GeoCoTDrive持续提升安全关键场景的规划性能，验证了显式几何链式思考对VLA规划的有效性。

Conclusion: 显式地理几何链式思考可有效弥合VLA的2D语义与3D几何错配，提升基于VLA的规划表现，但摘要未给出量化指标、消融细节与开源情况。

Abstract: Vision-language-action~(VLA) models have emerged as a promising paradigm for autonomous driving. However, existing VLA models still suffer from a fundamental mismatch: driving actions require precise 3D geometric cues, while visual-language understanding and reasoning are largely conducted in a 2D semantic space. In this paper, we propose GeoCoTDrive, an explicit geometric chain-of-thought framework that grounds geometry in a planning-oriented manner. GeoCoTDrive follows a think with 2D first, drive with dedicated 3D priors paradigm. It first grounds 2D regions corresponding to decision-critical cues, and then retrieves localized 3D priors by sampling features from a geometric foundation model within the grounded regions. These localized geometric features are interleaved into the autoregressive context to support the trajectory generation. To supervise this process, we introduce planning-relevant grounding, a new region-level grounding task that focuses on local spatial cues directly affecting ego planning decisions, and construct the PlanningGrounding dataset to endow VLAs with planning-oriented grounding capability. Experiments across multiple end-to-end autonomous driving benchmarks show that GeoCoTDrive consistently improves safety-critical planning performance, demonstrating the effectiveness of the explicit geometric chain-of-thought process for VLA-based planning.

</details>


### [140] [MOTIP2: Spatial Priors for End-to-End Multi-Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10391)
*Benoît Roussel,Damien Bouet,Liming Chen,Pierre Perrault*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MOTIP2 通过在数据、损失和表示三个阶段显式编码空间先验（Spatial ID Switches、Spatial ID Loss、Spatial Anchor），在不引入额外数据的前提下改进端到端多目标跟踪，抑制空间上不合理的 ID 切换，并在 DanceTrack、SportsMOT、PersonPath22 上取得新的 SOTA，同时提供速度-精度可调的模型族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端多目标跟踪器在关联困难的基准上已接近经典 tracking-by-detection 方法，但仍会产生经典方法不会出现的空间上不合理的错误（如把画面两端的目标赋予同一 ID）。由于跟踪标注稀缺，模型难以自行学到这些约束，因此作者选择显式编码空间先验，同时保持推理完全端到端、无后处理关联。

Method: 提出三种空间先验，分别作用于数据、损失与表示阶段：(1) Spatial ID Switches 将轨迹置换偏向空间重叠目标，缩小训练与推理阶段的混淆分布差异；(2) Spatial ID Loss 按框间距离缩放各身份惩罚，使远距离切换代价高于邻近切换；(3) Spatial Anchor 为每个轨迹 token 提供其帧内位置，作为注意力机制的显式空间线索。三者实例化于 MOTIP2，由 MOTIP 改造而来并基于实时 DEIM 检测 Transformer。

Result: 在无额外数据训练下：MOTIP2-L 达到 DanceTrack 73.4 HOTA、SportsMOT 76.0、PersonPath22 71.1 IDF1，均为新 SOTA；MOTIP2-X 在 DanceTrack 达 74.8 HOTA；轻量版 MOTIP2-S 以超过 3 倍速度匹配原始 MOTIP 精度，整体形成覆盖速度-精度权衡的模型族。

Conclusion: 显式空间先验是替代稀缺跟踪标注、抑制端到端跟踪器空间不合理 ID 切换的有效手段；MOTIP2 在不增加数据且保持端到端推理的前提下刷新多项基准，并通过模型族兼顾实时性与精度。

Abstract: End-to-end multi-object trackers have narrowed the gap with classical tracking-by-detection on association-difficult benchmarks. Yet they still make spatially implausible errors no classical tracker would, such as assigning one identity to objects on opposite sides of the frame. A model could learn to avoid them, but tracking annotations are scarce, so we encode spatial priors explicitly instead, while keeping inference fully end-to-end with no post-hoc association. We propose three spatial priors, at the data, loss, and representation stages. Spatial ID Switches bias trajectory permutations toward spatially overlapping objects, reducing the mismatch between training and inference confusions. Spatial ID Loss scales each identity's penalty by its box distance, so a distant switch costs more than a nearby one. Spatial Anchor gives each track token its frame position, an explicit spatial cue for attention. We instantiate the three priors in MOTIP2, a tracker adapted from MOTIP and built on the real-time DEIM detection transformer. Trained without extra data, its main model, MOTIP2-L, sets a new state of the art: 73.4 HOTA on DanceTrack, 76.0 on SportsMOT, and 71.1 IDF1 on PersonPath22. MOTIP2 is a family of models spanning the speed-accuracy trade-off: a lighter model, MOTIP2-S, matches the original MOTIP at over 3x the speed, and MOTIP2-X reaches 74.8 HOTA on DanceTrack.

</details>


### [141] [Gaussian Density Splatting Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10396)
*Miao Shang,Yabin Wang,Xiaopeng Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GDSNet 用连续 2D 高斯原语叠加表示人群，通过控制点特征池化回归高斯参数，并借助可微高斯泼溅以空间匹配方式端到端训练，在四个基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人群计数依赖网格化密度图，对空间分辨率敏感，且密度图与计数目标之间存在表征与监督的错配。作者希望用连续、分辨率无关的高斯原语表达人群分布，并让局部密度监督与全局计数优化统一在一个可端到端训练的框架中。

Method: 方法有两点核心：一是控制点引导的拟合机制，为局部区域分配一组控制点，通过特征池化回归每个高斯原语的参数，从而结构化预测；二是将可微高斯泼溅适配到计数任务，为每个原语参数化几何参数与标量密度质量，经可微渲染得到密度图，并以空间匹配损失进行端到端训练，同时提供局部密度监督和全局计数优化。

Result: 在四个标准人群计数基准上的大量评测表明，GDSNet 一致优于当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: 该工作说明连续高斯原语表达加可微泼溅渲染可作为网格密度图的有力替代，为人群计数提供了分辨率鲁棒、监督统一的建模范式；其控制点-原语结构思想也可迁移到其他点集/密度估计任务。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel crowd counting approach, the Gaussian Density Splatting Network (GDSNet). Unlike methods that rely on conventional, grid-based density maps and are sensitive to spatial resolution, GDSNet represents a crowd as a superposition of continuous 2D Gaussian primitives. Our approach is built upon two key contributions. First, we introduce a control-point-based fitting mechanism to structure the prediction of the Gaussian parameters. We design a method to allocate a set of control points that define local regions, from which features are pooled to regress each primitive's parameters. Second, we adapt a differentiable Gaussian Splatting framework to the counting task by parameterizing each primitive with geometric parameters and a scalar density mass. This formulation allows the network to be trained end-to-end via spatial matching of differentiably rendered density maps, naturally providing both local density supervision and global count optimization. Extensive evaluations on four standard benchmarks show GDSNet consistently outperforms the state of the art.

</details>


### [142] [Self-correction Optimization for Interleaved Multimodal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10400)
*Xin You,Zhiwei Ning,Zukai Chen,Minghui Zhang,Xuanke Shi,Hanxiao Zhang,Jingsong Liu,Jie Yang,Quan Wang,Yun Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的自纠正优化（SCO）方法，将无分类器引导更新视为参考，通过"新事件约束"与"状态保持约束"进行最小自纠正，实现时序一致的交错图文生成，并可扩展至视频生成与物理过程建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型生成交错图文内容时依赖昂贵的数据增强式额外训练，且难以保持视觉主体一致性、时序连贯性与物理合理性，因此需要一种免训练的一致交错生成方案。

Method: SCO：以无分类器引导（classifier-free guidance）更新作为参考轨迹，施加两项互补约束做最小幅度自纠正——新事件约束（new-event constraint）促进图像-文本序列的时序一致性；状态保持约束（state-preserving constraint）在后续生成步骤中维持视觉主体的连贯性。方法为 training-free，无需额外训练或增强数据。

Result: 在具有挑战性的交错多模态生成基准上，时序连贯性与视觉主体保持均取得显著提升；方法可扩展至视频生成，并改善机器人操作、长时程手工制作等物理过程建模。

Conclusion: 免训练的引导自纠正是一种有效且通用的交错图文生成一致性提升手段，并可迁移至视频与物理过程生成等更广泛场景。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have made significant progress in visual understanding and generation. However, generating interleaved image--text content remains challenging, as it requires tightly integrated multimodal understanding and generation capabilities. Although existing MLLMs provide promising solutions, most rely on additional training with augmented data, which is computationally expensive and remains limited in preserving visual subjects, temporal consistency, and physical plausibility. In this work, we propose self-correction optimization (SCO), an effective training-free method for consistent interleaved generation. SCO treats the classifier-free guidance update as a reference and performs minimal self-correction under two complementary constraints, including new-event and state-preserving constraints. Specifically, the new-event constraint promotes temporal consistency across image--text sequences, while the state-preserving constraint maintains the coherence of visual subjects throughout subsequent generation steps. Experiments on challenging interleaved multimodal generation benchmarks demonstrate significant improvements in temporal coherence and visual-subject preservation. Furthermore, SCO can be extended to video generation and improves the modeling of physically grounded processes, including robot manipulation and long-horizon handcrafting.

</details>


### [143] [Rubix: Global Correspondence-Free Point Set Alignment through Assignment Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10408)
*Subhransu S. Bhattacharjee,Dylan Campbell,Rahul Shome*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Rubix 将等权、平方欧氏损失下的平面 Procrustes-Wasserstein 对齐问题转化为「置换多边形」的几何问题，给出全局最优解，并证明其顶点数上界恰为 n(n-1)，从而解决了 Rote 的旋转-分配开放问题；方法可扩展到 3D 旋转与部分匹配，在 MPEG-7 形状匹配上比旋转网格快 50 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Procrustes-Wasserstein 对齐需同时估计匹配与旋转且无对应关系监督，交替最小化易陷入次优解。平面等权情形缺乏全局最优算法，且 Rote 提出的旋转-分配问题中置换多边形顶点数的精确界长期未解决。作者希望用凸几何刻画所有匹配的可行域，从而获得可证明最优、可计算的全局解。

Method: 对两个中心化 n 点集，每个匹配 σ 定义复相关 z_σ = Σ_i x̄_i y_{σ(i)}，其凸包即「置换多边形」。固定旋转下支撑顶点给出最优匹配，最远顶点给出全局对齐。作者证明 n≥2 时顶点数的紧界为 n(n-1)；在精确算术下用分配查询以 O(n^5) 恢复该多边形。基于分配的上界被用于分支定界，扩展到三维旋转与给定平移下的部分匹配。

Result: 证明置换多边形顶点数的紧界 n(n-1)（n≥2），回答了 Rote 的旋转-分配开放问题；精确算术下 O(n^5) 恢复多边形；在计时 MPEG-7 形状对上，Rubix 平均 12 ms 达到所有数值参考值，在相同精度下比旋转网格快 50 倍；其距离在真实 3D 扫描的重力对齐匹配、形状检索与含噪晶体分类上优于交替最小化。

Conclusion: Rubix 以置换多边形的凸几何给出平面等权 Procrustes-Wasserstein 的全局最优算法，并给出新的组合几何界，同时通过分支定界推广到 3D 与部分匹配；在形状匹配与真实 3D 任务上兼顾精度与效率。

Abstract: Procrustes-Wasserstein alignment jointly estimates a matching and rotation without supplied correspondences, but alternating minimization can stop at suboptimal solutions. Rubix solves the equally weighted planar problem globally under squared Euclidean loss. Each matching $\sigma$ of two centered $n$-point sets defines a complex correlation $z_\sigma=\sum_i\bar x_i y_{\sigma(i)}$. Their convex hull is the permutation polygon: supporting vertices give optimal matchings at fixed rotations, and the farthest vertex gives the global alignment. We prove the sharp bound of $n(n-1)$ vertices for $n\ge2$, answering Rote's rotation-assignment open problem. In exact arithmetic, assignment queries recover the polygon in $\mathcal O(n^5)$ operations. Assignment-based bounds extend the approach to three-dimensional rotations and partial matching at a supplied translation through branch-and-bound. On timed MPEG-7 shape pairs, Rubix attains every numerical reference value in 12 ms on average, 50 times faster than a rotation grid at the same accuracy. Its distances improve gravity-aligned matching of real 3D scans, shape retrieval and noisy crystal classification over alternating minimization.

</details>


### [144] [GraphRectify: Graph-Based Transfer of Adversarial Example Detectors Across Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10423)
*Arash Vashagh,Roozbeh Razavi-Far*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GraphRectify：图结构表示迁移框架，将对抗样本检测器从原分类器骨干迁移到新骨干，在多数据集/架构/攻击下平均ROC-AUC优于从零训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗样本检测器通常与训练时的分类器骨干耦合，当被保护模型替换或升级时检测器失效，需重新训练。直接迁移困难：不同网络的中间表示不兼容。

Method: GraphRectify 将分类器中间特征建模为结构化图表示，并学习适配映射，把新骨干的表示对齐到原模型上训练好的检测器，实现检测器复用，无需在新骨干上重新训练。

Result: 在多个数据集、骨干架构与对抗攻击（含同时攻击分类器与检测器的自适应攻击）上评估，GraphRectify 的整体ROC-AUC 高于在新骨干上从零训练以及所评测的迁移消融；跨骨干家族迁移、数据充足时增益最明显；数据极度受限时从零训练仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: 对抗检测知识可跨异构分类器架构有效迁移，无需每次骨干变化就重新学习，为检测器复用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Adversarial example detectors are often tied to the classifier backbone they were trained on, limiting reuse when the protected model is replaced or upgraded. Directly transferring such detectors across backbones is challenging because different networks generally produce incompatible internal representations. We propose GraphRectify, a graph-based framework for transferring adversarial image detectors across classifier backbones. GraphRectify learns a structured representation of intermediate classifier features and adapts representations from a new backbone to the detector learned on the original model, enabling detector reuse. We evaluate GraphRectify across multiple datasets, backbone architectures, and adversarial attacks, including detector-aware adaptive attacks that jointly target the classifier and detector. Across the complete evaluation matrix, GraphRectify achieves higher aggregate ROC-AUC than training a detector from scratch on the new backbone and the evaluated transfer ablations. The gains are particularly strong for transfers between different backbone families and when sufficient data are available. In contrast, training from scratch remains competitive in the most data-limited settings. These results show that adversarial detection knowledge can transfer effectively across heterogeneous classifier architectures rather than being relearned whenever the protected backbone changes.

</details>


### [145] [SGF+: Decoupling Gradient Flows for Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10429)
*Zihan Su,Junhao Zhuang,Yaowei Li,Siwen Lu,Haoran Li,Lingen Li,Haoyu Wu,Weiyang Jin,Songchun Zhang,Haoyang Huang,Chun Yuan,Zeyue Xue,Nan Duan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SGF+将自回归视频生成中"上下文写入"与"去噪"两个角色解耦为独立参数，通过因果注意力保留交互，仅用原始生成目标联合优化，显著提升画质与长时一致性，可在5秒片段训练下连续生成24小时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频生成中，当前帧既要去噪又要写入KV表示供未来预测，两个角色共享参数。作者发现二者梯度模式不同且存在系统性负对齐，导致视觉质量与时间一致性的联合优化受阻。

Method: 提出Self Gradient Forcing Plus（SGF+）：为上下文写入与去噪分配独立参数，同时通过因果注意力保持二者交互；不使用辅助损失，仅以原始生成目标联合优化，上下文写入通过其对未来预测的贡献获得监督。

Result: 在逐帧与分块生成设置下，相比评估基线在视觉质量和长时程一致性上均有提升，且无需额外视频训练数据或更长训练周期；仅用5秒rollout训练即可支持长达24小时的连续生成，无需长视频微调。

Conclusion: 角色特定参数化是高质量自回归视频生成与原生长时程外推的有效设计原则，梯度冲突分析为架构解耦提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Autoregressive video generation requires denoising the current frames while writing their key-value representations as context for future predictions. However, these two roles typically share parameters, and we find that their gradients exhibit distinct patterns and systematic negative alignment, hindering the joint optimization of visual quality and temporal consistency. We introduce Self Gradient Forcing Plus (SGF+), which assigns separate parameters to context writing and denoising while preserving their interaction through causal attention. Both roles are jointly optimized using the original generation objective without auxiliary losses, with context writing supervised through its contribution to future predictions. This simple change improves visual quality and long-horizon consistency over the evaluated baselines in both framewise and chunkwise generation, without additional video training data or a longer training horizon. Trained on only 5s rollouts, SGF+ supports continuous generation for up to 24 hours without long-video fine-tuning. These results highlight role-specific parameterization as an effective design principle for high-quality autoregressive video generation and native long-horizon extrapolation.

</details>


### [146] [Detecting Adversarial Images through Response Profiles of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10436)
*Arash Vashagh,Roozbeh Razavi-Far*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 基于冻结视觉-语言模型对一组通用语义提示的响应模式（类别统计、提示间关系、与干净参考分布的偏差及弱变换稳定性）构建紧凑响应画像，用轻量分类器检测对抗图像，在多种数据集、CLIP风格骨干和多类攻击下取得强判别性能，并对未见攻击与自适应攻击具有较好鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗扰动可改变冻结VLM的预测，却仍保持置信度与图文相似度模式看似合理，使单纯依赖置信度或相似度的检测失效；作者希望利用图像与一整套通用语义提示交互的"更宏观"响应模式来识别对抗输入。

Method: 保持VLM参数冻结，对单张图像在一组通用语义提示上获取响应；将其汇总为四类特征：类别级统计量、提示之间的相互关系、相对干净参考分布的偏差、以及在弱图像变换下的稳定性；形成紧凑响应画像，交由轻量分类器判别是否为对抗样本。

Result: 在多个公开图像数据集、若干CLIP风格视觉骨干以及梯度、优化、自动化和空间类攻击上评估：攻击特化设置下判别性能强，对训练未见攻击仍保留显著性能；在受控的检测器专属协议下，响应画像表示优于所评估的嵌入几何基线；特征组提供互补信息，且对提示配置变化保持有效；同时报告了推理开销与针对检测器感知自适应攻击的表现。

Conclusion: 跨语义提示的响应模式为冻结VLM中的对抗图像检测提供了有用的互补信号；但该方法属对抗鲁棒性/安全检测方向，与具身智能、VLA、世界模型、3D重建等核心主线无直接关系，仅可作相关背景参考。

Abstract: Adversarial perturbations can alter the predictions of frozen vision-language models (VLMs) while leaving their confidence and image--text similarity patterns seemingly plausible. We investigate whether we can identify adversarial inputs based on the broader way an image interacts with a collection of general semantic prompts. Our detector summarizes these responses using category-level statistics, relationships among prompts, deviations from clean reference distributions, and stability under weak image transformations, producing a compact response profile that is classified by a lightweight model while the VLM remains fixed. We evaluate the approach on multiple public image datasets, several CLIP-style visual backbones, and a range of gradient-based, optimization-based, automated, and spatial attacks. The detector achieves strong discrimination in attack-specific settings and retains substantial performance when evaluated on attacks not seen during training. Under a controlled detector-specific protocol, the response-profile representation outperforms the evaluated embedding-geometry baselines. Additional analyses show that the feature groups provide complementary information and that the method remains effective under variations in the prompt configuration. We also examine inference cost and performance against detector-aware adaptive attacks. Overall, the results indicate that response patterns across semantic prompts provide a useful complementary signal for adversarial image detection in frozen VLMs.

</details>


### [147] [ECHO: Embodied Camera Observations of Human Object Carrying](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10438)
*Xuefei Sun,Lorin Achey,Kali Hamilton,Alberto Speranzon,Gregory Grebe,Yonatan Bisk,Christoffer Heckman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"情境化物体放置"基准任务，并发布ECHO大规模合成数据集：将HM3D室内RGB-D重建场景与具身人类搬运物体的记录配对，含3805条标注episode、6-DoF轨迹、动作描述与暗示目的地的情境句；基线与消融表明单一模态不足，需联合推理场景结构、人类活动与情境知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身与辅助智能体不仅需识别物体，还需依据环境布局与住户习惯推断物体应放置的位置。该问题进展有限，原因之一是缺少专门的数据集与评测基准：现有RGB-D扫描数据集只重建静态房间、无人类活动；人-物交互数据集只有动作、缺少可导航的完整重建场景与"自然目的地"的真值定义。

Method: 定义"情境化物体放置"任务——在被观测的搬运episode中预测物体目的地；构建ECHO数据集（合成的具身相机人类搬物观测）：密集RGB-D室内扫描（159个楼层、115个HM3D场景）＋人类搬运日常物体的记录，含198类物体、每层完整RGB-D扫描与人工房间标签及表面列表；每episode提供同步RGB-D片段、6-DoF相机/人/物体轨迹、起点与目的地表面、动作caption、以及暗示目的地但不直接点名的情境句。评测采用输入掩码探针（input-masked probes）与端到端基线。

Result: ECHO是首个公开的、同时结合重建场景、人类活动、自然语言与情境放置标注的数据集，共3805条人工标注episode。评测与掩码探针结果显示，没有任何单一输入模态足以完成任务，须联合推理场景结构、人类活动与情境知识。

Conclusion: 情境化物体放置是一个被明确提出但此前缺乏定义与评测的具身任务，ECHO为该任务提供了首个多模态基准；结果说明场景结构、人类行为与情境常识的融合是必要方向，但论文以基准与数据集构建及基线评测为主，尚未给出可推广的强方法。

Abstract: Embodied and assistive agents must do more than recognize objects: they must reason about where an object belongs given the layout of an environment and the habits of the people who live in it. Progress on this problem has been limited, in part because no dedicated benchmark or dataset exists to define and evaluate it. Existing RGB-D scan datasets reconstruct static rooms without human activity, while human-object-interaction datasets capture motion without a navigable, fully reconstructed scene or a ground-truth notion of an object's natural destination. We introduce contextual object placement as a benchmark task: predicting an object's destination during an observed object-carrying episode. To support this task, we present Embodied Camera observations of Human Object carrying (ECHO), a large-scale synthetic dataset that pairs dense RGB-D scans of indoor scenes with recordings of an embodied human carrying everyday objects to context-appropriate destinations. ECHO is the first publicly available dataset to combine reconstructed scenes, human activity, natural language, and contextual-placement annotations. It comprises 3,805 human-annotated episodes across 159 floors of 115 HM3D scenes, involving 198 distinct objects. Each floor includes a complete RGB-D scan with human-annotated room labels and a surface list. Each episode provides synchronized RGB-D encounter clips; 6-DoF camera, human, and object trajectories; start and destination surfaces; an action caption; and a human-written context: a single sentence describing the inhabitant's routine that implies the destination without naming it. We evaluate contextual object placement using input-masked probes and an end-to-end baseline. Results show that no single input modality is sufficient, highlighting the need to jointly reason over scene structure, human activity, and contextual knowledge.

</details>


### [148] [MORCA: Offline-to-Online Reinforcement Learning for Adaptive Cache Reuse in Video Diffusion Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10457)
*Yuxiang Xiong,Ruiyan Wang,Wenqiang Wang,Teng Hu,Songhang Shen,Bohao Feng,Hongqian Deng,Ran Yi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MORCA：用离线到在线强化学习训练缓存调度框架，在用户指定加速比下做潜空间感知的重用/重算决策，提升视频扩散Transformer生成保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Diffusion Transformer 视频合成质量高但迭代去噪推理延迟大。现有动态缓存方法以"步误差"（该步重用缓存引入的误差）指导决策，作者指出步误差与最终视频质量损失（终端误差）并不直接对应；且现有阈值法无法精确控制加速比，难以满足用户指定加速目标。

Method: 提出 MORCA 缓存调度框架：论证终端误差与步误差的差异，并说明潜空间信息有助于刻画二者关系，从而指导缓存决策；将重用/重算决策建模为在用户指定加速目标约束下的序贯决策问题，用离线到在线强化学习训练调度策略。

Result: 在多种视频生成模型、多个目标加速比下，与最先进缓存方法在相近计算预算下相比，MORCA 取得更好的生成保真度。

Conclusion: 以终端误差为目标、潜空间感知并以强化学习实现精确加速比控制的缓存调度，优于传统步误差阈值式缓存加速方法。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) achieve remarkable performance in video synthesis, but their iterative denoising process suffers from high inference latency. To address this, caching has emerged as an effective acceleration strategy by capitalizing on inter-step redundancy during denoising. Existing dynamic caching methods typically estimate the error that cache reuse would introduce at each denoising step (step error) to guide cache decisions, whereas our concern is how much quality loss cache reuse would cause in the final generated video (terminal error). We show that step error does not directly correspond to terminal error and that latent information helps capture their relationship, thereby informing cache decisions. Moreover, existing threshold-based methods cannot provide precise speedup control, making it difficult to meet practical requirements for user-specified acceleration targets. To address these limitations, we introduce MORCA, a cache scheduling framework trained through offline-to-online reinforcement learning to make latent-aware reuse/recompute decisions under user-specified acceleration targets. Extensive experiments on different video generation models across multiple target acceleration ratios demonstrate that MORCA achieves better generation fidelity than state-of-the-art caching methods under comparable computational budgets. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [149] [Label-free cell counting and viability prediction with brightfield imaging and deep learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10473)
*Amir Reza Vazifeh,Christian Zeigler,Sornanathan Meyyappan,Richard Jeske,Jason W. Fleischer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ViabiLens：基于明场成像的无标记细胞活力分析AI软件，结合细胞检测模型与CNN分类器，在CHO细胞上对无染色样本的活力预测平均绝对误差2.68%，并公开基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统细胞活力评估依赖膜不透性染料染色，存在化学试剂干扰细胞正常生理过程、部分膜损伤细胞活力判定模糊、荧光染料光漂白导致精度下降、无法原位实时测量等局限，因此需要一种无标记、非侵入式的活力分析方案。

Method: 先验证两个假设：染色细胞在明场下的图像已包含区分活/死细胞的充分信息；无染色明场图像与染色图像具有相似特征，从而使染色数据上训练的模型可泛化至无染色数据。据此构建ViabiLens：细胞检测模型定位单个细胞 + CNN进行活/死分类，并配以基于UMAP的交互式可视化界面。

Result: 在覆盖较宽活力区间的CHO细胞上评估，ViabiLens对无染色样本的活力估计相对荧光参考测量的平均绝对误差为2.68%。同时发布无标记细胞活力分析基准数据集。

Conclusion: ViabiLens表明无标记明场成像结合AI可实现接近荧光参考精度的细胞活力评估，规避染色带来的干扰与实时性问题，适用于生物制药制造与药物开发，并为该方向提供公开基准。

Abstract: Cell viability assessment is a core requirement in cell culture systems, with critical applications in biopharmaceutical manufacturing and drug development. Conventionally, it is measured by adding membrane-impermeable dyes to a sample (a process called staining), which allows compromised cell membranes to be distinguished from intact ones. However, staining has several limitations: (a) chemical agents can perturb normal cellular processes of the cells being measured, (b) it is often ambiguous to assign viability to individual cells whose membrane integrity is only partially compromised. (c) photobleaching can undermine measurement accuracy over time when using fluorescent stains, and (d) staining cannot be performed in situ or in real time. Here, we show that (1) stained cells captured under brightfield imaging contain sufficient information to distinguish live and dead cells, and (2) cells captured under unstained brightfield imaging exhibit similar image features to their stained counterparts, enabling models trained on stained cells to generalize to unstained ones. We then report the development and validation of ViabiLens, an AI-assisted software for label-free cell viability analysis. The ViabiLens combines a cell detection model for localizing individual cells with a convolutional neural network (CNN) classifier for live/dead prediction, paired with an interactive UMAP-based viewer for visualizing and exploring individual cells across the sample. Evaluated on Chinese Hamster Ovary (CHO) cells spanning a wide range of viability conditions, ViabiLens achieves a mean absolute error of 2.68\% on unstained samples against fluorescence-based reference measurements. We also release a benchmark dataset for label-free cell viability analysis to facilitate future research, available at this https URL .

</details>


### [150] [Insights from Autoresearch for Solar Panel Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10491)
*Justinas Lekavicius,Kursat Komurcu,Valentas Gruzauskas,Linas Petkevicius*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文考察 AutoResearch 协议：编码语言模型在 1 小时 GPU 预算内自动编辑训练程序，仅当验证 IoU 提升时保留该修改；在冻结的真实图像划分上做光伏板分割（DeepLabV3-ResNet-50 固定）。三次各 24 次实验的搜索（Gemma 4 12B、Qwen3-8B）均超越其 1 小时基线，但保留的修改无法跨硬件迁移；Qwen3-8B 纯真实图像配置测试 IoU 0.836，略优于 GAN 增广参考的 0.833。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索语言模型能否以"编辑训练程序—验证指标择优保留"的闭环自动改进训练流程，并检验由此获得的技术方案是否具备跨硬件可迁移性。以光伏板分割为具体落地场景，同时质疑 GAN 类合成增广在该任务上的必要性。

Method: AutoResearch 协议：固定 DeepLabV3-ResNet-50 与真实图像数据划分，编码 LM 在每次 1 小时 GPU 预算内修改训练程序，仅保留验证 IoU 提升的改动。共 3 个 campaign、每个 24 次实验，覆盖 Gemma 4 12B 与 Qwen3-8B 两种模型；将最优配置与 GAN 增广参考 schedule 在测试 IoU 上对比，并在不同硬件上复现验证迁移性。

Result: 所有 3 个 campaign 的搜索均比其 1 小时基线有所提升；但保留的修改无法跨硬件迁移。Qwen3-8B 纯真实图像配置测试 IoU 0.836，对比 GAN 增广参考的 0.833（+0.003，差距较小，需注意统计显著性）。

Conclusion: 语言模型驱动的自动训练程序搜索可在固定预算下小幅提升特定分割任务性能，并提示合成增广未必必要；但收益对硬件环境敏感、不可迁移，说明该协议目前更适合作为单环境内的调优工具，而非可泛化的方法学结论。

Abstract: This paper investigates AutoResearch, a protocol in which a coding language model edits a training program under a one-hour GPU budget and retains a change only if validation IoU improves. The protocol is applied to photovoltaic panel segmentation on a frozen real-image split, with DeepLabV3--ResNet-50 held fixed. Three campaigns of 24 experiments, using Gemma~4 12B, Qwen3-8B all improve their one-hour baselines, but retained modifications do not transfer across hardware. The Qwen3-8B configuration, trained on real images only, reaches a test IoU of 0.836 versus 0.833 for the reference GAN-augmented schedule. Research repository this https URL .

</details>


### [151] [QuadTok: Quadtree Visual Tokenizer for Autoregressive Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10497)
*Yucheng Mao,Zeyuan Chen,Xiaojun Shan,Xiang Zhang,Divyansh Srivastava,Bingnan Li,Zhuowen Tu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: QuadTok 提出层次化四叉树视觉分词结构，动态分配 token 容量，节省约 10% token 并支持自回归图像生成（ImageNet 256×256 gFID 2.08）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 2D 网格或 1D token 序列存在空间绑定与序列灵活性的矛盾；均匀网格在均质区域浪费表征容量。

Method: 四叉树分词器按视觉复杂度自适应细分，均质区域保持粗分辨率；以四叉树拓扑为条件，用 947M GPT 式模型做自回归生成，树结构天然提供因果顺序。

Result: ImageNet 上省约 10% token，零样本迁移 COCO 省约 9%，重建保真度相当；947M 生成模型在 ImageNet 256×256 达 2.08 gFID，并具备零样本空间可控生成能力。

Conclusion: 层次化四叉树分词在效率与生成质量间取得良好平衡，且天然支持自回归与空间可控生成，为视觉 tokenization 提供新范式。

Abstract: We introduce QuadTok, a novel framework for visual tokenization and autoregressive image generation. Compared to traditional approaches using 2D grids or 1D token sequences, we propose a hierarchical quadtree structure, bridging the gap between 2D spatial binding and 1D sequence-level flexibility. The QuadTok tokenizer dynamically allocates representational capacity to visually intricate areas while leaving homogeneous regions at a coarse resolution. Compared with a fixed 256-token grid, our ImageNet-trained tokenizer saves approximately 10% of tokens on ImageNet and 9% when transferred zero-shot to the COCO dataset, while maintaining comparable reconstruction fidelity. Furthermore, the natural causality introduced by the tree structure seamlessly enables autoregressive image generation. Conditioned on a quadtree topology supplied before generation, our 947M GPT-style generative model achieves a 2.08 gFID on the ImageNet $256 \times 256$ benchmark. Additionally, leveraging the strong spatial correlation preserved by the quadtree structure, the QuadTok generator enables zero-shot spatially controlled image generation capabilities. Code: this https URL .

</details>


### [152] [Video-Conditioned Generative Joint 2D-3D Hand Motion Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10512)
*Chen Xu,Yunqi Li,Binbin Huang,Brent Yi,Shenghua Gao,Yi Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 JoHan，一个从零训练的生成式框架，直接从视频序列联合生成对齐的 2D 与 3D 局部手部姿态序列，利用学习到的 2D 引导与运动先验实现时序一致的手部运动及相机空间全局位姿重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频中手部运动重建受遮挡与观测不完整影响，逐帧姿态估计不可靠且时序不一致，亟需避免中间逐帧预测的生成式方法。

Method: 统一生成框架，无中间逐帧姿态预测；联合学习 2D/3D 手部姿态序列的时序动态与跨表示对应关系；2D 轨迹提供图像空间时空线索引导 3D 生成，运动先验保证时序一致性，并借由学到的 2D-3D 对应恢复相机坐标系下全局位置与朝向。

Result: 在挑战性基准上局部手部姿态与相机空间重建的精度和速度显著提升，运动动态更自然、时序更平滑，同时保持较高逐帧精度。

Conclusion: 联合生成 2D-3D 序列的范式可有效缓解遮挡与时序不一致问题，兼顾精度与平滑度，为视频手部运动重建提供新思路。

Abstract: Recovering faithful 3D hand motion from video remains challenging due to frequent occlusions and incomplete visual observations, which make frame-wise pose estimates unreliable and temporally inconsistent. To address this problem, we propose JoHan, a unified generative framework that recovers hand motion directly from video sequences without relying on intermediate per-frame pose predictions. Trained from scratch, our model jointly generates aligned 2D and 3D local hand pose sequences by learning their temporal dynamics and cross-representation correspondence. The generated 2D trajectories exploit direct spatial and temporal cues from the 2D images to guide the following generative 3D motion reconstruction, while the learned motion prior promotes temporal consistency. Their learned 2D-3D correspondence further enables recovery of the hand's global position and orientation relative to the camera. Extensive experiments on challenging benchmarks demonstrate significantly improved accuracy and speed in local hand-pose and camera-space reconstruction. Notably, our method captures much better hand-motion dynamics, producing significantly smoother motion than previous methods while maintaining high per-frame pose accuracy.

</details>


### [153] [GRACE: Generation-aware latent compression for efficient video generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10524)
*Jiyoung Kim,Paul Hyunbin Cho,Jisu Nam,Donghoon Lee,Hyunsung Go,Yeonkyeong Lee,Hansaem Kim,Seungryong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GRACE：两阶段框架，压缩预训练视频自编码器以加速视频扩散模型（DiT），保持与预训练DiT的兼容性。冻结基础潜变量、学习残差潜变量，并在冻结DiT特征空间对齐生成导向，再以轻量微调与非对称去噪适配DiT。Wan2.1-I2V-14B上token减8倍、延迟降11.1倍，VBench质量持平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散模型DiT在token较多时推理代价高，需要高度压缩的视频自编码器提速；但压缩比升高会退化重建质量，需增加通道数，拖慢DiT收敛；压缩潜变量分布偏移，导致预训练DiT必须重训或高成本适配，而仅以重建为目标优化自编码器仍会使潜变量偏离DiT已学分布。

Method: 提出GRACE两阶段框架：阶段一压缩预训练视频自编码器，保留冻结的基础潜变量（来自预训练编码器），为强压缩下丢失的信息学习残差潜变量，并在冻结DiT的特征空间中将压缩潜变量与预训练潜变量对齐，使自编码器以生成为目标优化；阶段二以轻量微调与非对称去噪适配DiT，其中基础部分先于残差部分去噪。

Result: 在Wan2.1-I2V-14B上，480x832x81分辨率下token数减少8倍、延迟降低11.1倍，VBench生成质量与压缩前预训练管线相当。

Conclusion: 通过生成感知的潜空间压缩与DiT兼容性对齐，可在不牺牲生成质量的前提下显著加速视频扩散模型，为高效视频生成提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Highly compressed video autoencoders offer an effective way to accelerate video diffusion models, as the Diffusion Transformer (DiT) operates on far fewer tokens. However, such autoencoders are challenging to train, since a higher compression ratio degrades reconstruction quality and recovering it requires more channels, which is known to slow the convergence of the DiT. The compressed latent also differs from the one the DiT was trained on, so the pretrained DiT must be either retrained from scratch or adapted at considerable cost. Compressing the autoencoder the DiT was trained with appears to preserve compatibility, yet optimizing it for reconstruction alone still shifts the latent away from the distribution the DiT has learned. To address this, we propose Generation-Aware Latent Compression for Efficient Video Generation (GRACE), a two-stage framework that compresses a pretrained video autoencoder while keeping it compatible with the pretrained DiT. Specifically, we keep a frozen base latent from the pretrained encoder and learn a residual latent for the information lost under stronger compression, while aligning the compressed latent with the pretrained latent in the feature space of the frozen DiT so that the autoencoder is optimized for generation. We then adapt the DiT with lightweight fine-tuning and asymmetric denoising, where the base is denoised ahead of the residual. GRACE reduces the token count of Wan2.1-I2V-14B by 8x and its latency by 11.1x at 480x832x81, while matching the generation quality of the pretrained pipeline before compression on VBench.

</details>


### [154] [Never Look Back: Understanding Persistence in 3D Object Memory from Egocentric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10538)
*Shravan Chaudhari,William Paul,Suchi Saria,Rama Chellappa,Homanga Bharadhwaj*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Ledger，一种从自我中心视频构建的持久 3D 物体记忆，记录物体位置、移动历史与上下文描述，用于空间问答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身助手需要像人一样记住"东西放哪了、里面装的是什么"，即使当时并未意识到以后会用到，也即使物体已离开视野或从未被触碰。现有方法多为在线感知或依赖原始视频检索，缺乏跨时间持久、抗定位噪声的物体记忆。

Method: Ledger 将物体观测按其静止位置聚类，仅在出现重复证据后才记录一次移动，以抑制定位噪声；同时为每个物体保存简短上下文描述（如容器内容物、支撑面）。记录持久化存储，可脱离原始图像/视频直接回答空间问题。并构造 100 条多场景拼接流进行检索与构建失效分析。

Result: HD-EPIC 准确率 29.7%→42.6%，UCS-Bench 33.8%→38.5%，Ego4D 物体定位返回预测中位误差 0.99 m（原文 0.99 m）。消融显示时间持久性、上下文描述与检索三者互补；多场景流中检索与构建均出现失效，逐场景构建可部分恢复跨场景切换造成的性能损失。

Conclusion: 持久的 3D 物体记忆（位置+历史+描述）显著提升自我中心空间问答与物体定位，且不依赖原始视频回查；跨场景构建仍是主要瓶颈，逐场景构建是有前景的缓解方向。

Abstract: As we move through the world and carry out everyday tasks, we encounter objects that may become relevant only later. We are capable of recalling where we left something or what was inside a container, even without knowing we would need it later. Here, we study how an embodied assistant can build a similar memory from egocentric videos, by observing a person's day-to-day activities. We present Ledger, a persistent 3D object memory that combines object locations, their histories, and contextual descriptions. It associates observations across the recording and retains objects after they leave the view, including those the person never touches. It clusters each object's observations by resting locations and records a move only after repeated evidence, reducing the effect of localization noise. Short descriptions preserve details such as an object's contents or supporting surface. It saves these records to later answer spatial questions without having to access the original images or video. Our memory raises HD-EPIC accuracy from 29.7% to 42.6%, UCS-Bench accuracy from 33.8% to 38.5% and localizes Ego4D objects with a 0.99 m median error on returned predictions. Our analyses identify complementary roles for temporal persistence, contextual descriptions, and retrieval. Our study on 100 stitched streams of multiple scenes each further exposes failures in both retrieval and construction. Per-scene construction partially recovers the performance lost across scene changes compared to that of single scene streams.

</details>


### [155] [Tetris3D: 3D Scene Generation With Objects That Fit Together](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10539)
*Jaeyeong Kim,Jinhyuk Jang,Jongmin Lee,Kyehong Park,Seungryong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Tetris3D：单图3D场景重建的生成框架，将每个物体生成显式地以周围物体几何及物理关系为条件，并配套发布1.2M规模物理仿真数据集ComOb（含逐物体mesh与成对物理关系标注），在遮挡交互区域仍能恢复几何/物理一致的形状与位姿，达到SOTA生成质量与物理稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单图3D场景重建方法多各自独立生成物体或以隐式方式耦合，缺乏对相邻物体间细粒度空间兼容性的显式引导，导致交互区域被遮挡时物体形状与位姿容易违反几何与物理约束。

Method: 以「场景中相邻物体几何 + 成对物理关系」显式条件化每个物体的生成，联合约束其形状与位姿在场景中几何与物理合理；并构建ComOb——基于物理仿真的1.2M场景数据集，覆盖多类物体的物理交互，提供逐物体mesh与成对物理关系标注。

Result: 在合成与真实场景上的实验表明，Tetris3D在交互区域被遮挡时仍能恢复连贯的物体形状与位姿，并在生成质量与物理稳定性两方面均取得SOTA。

Conclusion: 显式以物体间几何与物理关系为条件的生成范式，配合大规模物理标注数据，是提升单图3D场景重建几何/物理一致性的有效路径，为接触丰富场景的可交互3D资产恢复提供了方法论与数据基础。

Abstract: We propose Tetris3D, a generative framework for single-image 3D scene reconstruction that recovers objects which are physically and geometrically coherent as a scene. Existing methods often generate objects independently or couple them implicitly, providing limited guidance for ensuring fine-grained spatial compatibility between neighboring objects that interact with one another. To address this, we explicitly condition the generation of each object on the geometry of surrounding objects and their physical relationships, guiding its shape and pose to remain geometrically and physically plausible within the scene. Moreover, we introduce ComOb, a physics simulation-based dataset of 1.2M scenes featuring physical interactions across diverse object categories, with per-object meshes and pairwise physical relation annotations. Comprehensive experiments on synthetic and realworld scenes show that Tetris3D recovers coherent object shapes and poses even when interacting regions are occluded, and achieves state-of-the-art performance in both generation quality and physical stability.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [156] [A Review Of Robotic World Models For Dynamic Environments Based On Factor And Scene Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08800)
*Marco Giberna,Miguel Fernandez-Cortizas,Jose Luis Sanchez Lopez,Holger Voos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 这篇综述系统梳理了动态环境下基于因子图与场景图的机器人世界模型，围绕表示、构建/更新流程与下游任务三个维度进行分类，并强调混合模型、开放挑战与评测缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人环境本质上是动态的，而早期因子图（不确定性感知的几何估计）与场景图（结构化语义抽象）多假设静态环境，静态世界表示难以支撑长期自主运行，因此需要系统性地审视动态性该如何被纳入图式世界模型。

Method: 综述性方法：按(I)合适表示、(II)构建与更新流程、(III)下游任务利用三个维度组织评估；分别回顾因子图与场景图方法，并重点分析将两者结合的混合模型；对不同动态类型如何被建模进行分析，归纳常见架构模式。

Result: 归纳出动态图式世界模型的常见架构模式与建模动态性的方式，并识别出若干新兴趋势与开放问题：学习式感知到表示层的不确定性传播、最小传感条件下动态实体运动与尺度的可观测性、可扩展的终身维护、以及缺乏将世界模型质量与动态下游任务性能挂钩的数据集与评测协议。

Conclusion: 图式世界模型（尤其因子图-场景图混合模型）是应对动态机器人环境的有前景方向，但不确定性传播、可观测性、终身维护与评测基准仍是关键未解问题。

Abstract: Models based on graphs have emerged in robotics as a powerful foundation for internal world representations, where factor and scene graphs are among the most prominent model types found in the related literature and in successful robotic solutions. Initially, many of these models were assuming static environments as a simplification. Herein, factor graphs mainly provide uncertainty-aware geometric estimations while scene graphs enable a structured semantic abstraction. However, real-world robotic environments are often dynamic, posing severe challenges for purely static world representations. Therefore, this review presents a comprehensive view on how dynamic aspects of real-world environments can be addressed in such graph-based world models. We organize our assessments around three main aspects: (I) suitable representations, (II) pipelines to construct and update the representations, and (III) their exploitation for downstream tasks. We review approaches that are either based on factor or scene graphs, but put special emphasis on novel approaches that combine both types to form hybrid models. We mainly analyze how different types of dynamics can be modeled herein, and categorize common architectural patterns. Finally, emerging trends and open challenges are identified, including uncertainty propagation from learned perception through the representation layers, the observability of dynamic-entity motion and scale under minimal sensing, scalable lifelong maintenance, and the lack of datasets and evaluation protocols that ground world-model quality in downstream task performance under dynamics.

</details>


### [157] [SAFE: Unified Slip and Fracture Detection with Low-Cost Acoustic Sensing in Robotic Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08802)
*Zerun Wang,Vivek Kamat,Shekhar Bhansali*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 SAFE：用两个低成本 PVDF 声学传感器配合电机本体感知，在 Fin Ray 柔性夹爪上实时检测滑动与破碎状态，并用 HistGradientBoosting 分类器驱动自适应抓取控制，无需视觉与先验材质知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作易碎物体时需感知滑动、破碎等状态才能恰当响应，但物体材质未知、视觉难以捕捉接触瞬间的力学事件，低成本通用方案缺失。

Method: 两个被动 PVDF 声学传感器安装于顺应性 Fin Ray 夹爪，结合电机本体感知构建 79 维特征向量，用统一的 HistGradientBoosting 分类器输出正常/滑动/破碎三态；在该检测层上构建自适应抓取控制器，于 Jetson Orin Nano 上以 104 Hz 运行。

Result: 留一抓取交叉验证下 Alert-F1 达 0.884，滑动与破碎几乎无混淆，消融显示声学传感不可替代；46 次闭环真机试验成功率 91.3%，固定力策略在条件不匹配时降至 0%；未见物体上成功率 82.4%。

Conclusion: 单一低成本声学+本体感知方案即可实现与物体无关的失效感知抓取控制，无需物体专用标定，具备实时性与跨物体泛化能力。

Abstract: Manipulating fragile objects remains challenging as robots must understand the state of what they grasp, such as slip or fracture, to respond appropriately, especially when material properties are unknown. In this paper, we present SAFE: a low-cost, general-purpose sensing approach that detects both slip and fracture in real time using two passive polyvinylidene fluoride (PVDF) acoustic sensors and motor proprioception, without relying on vision or prior material knowledge. The sensors are mounted on a compliant Fin Ray gripper, and a unified HistGradientBoosting classifier reports the state (normal, slip, or fracture) from a 79-dimensional feature vector. Under leave-one-grasp-out cross-validation, SAFE achieves an Alert-F1 of 0.884 with near-zero slip-fracture confusion, and ablations confirm that acoustic sensing is indispensable. An adaptive grasp controller built on this detection layer runs at 104 Hz on a Jetson Orin Nano, achieving 91.3% success across 46 closed-loop robot trials spanning diverse object categories, while each fixed-force strategy drops to 0% on object conditions that mismatch its preset. It further reaches 82.4% success on novel objects unseen during training, demonstrating robust, failure-aware grasp control without object-specific calibration.

</details>


### [158] [Context-Aware Adaptive Pesticide Spraying for Agricultural Robots under Changing Weather and Terrain Using Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08807)
*Cong-Thanh Vu,Yen-Chen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的情境感知自适应喷洒框架，融合作物类型、农药类型与天气数据进行空间推理决策，并用MPPI轨迹跟踪控制器实现精准导航与喷洒，作物行检测精度提升≥30%，并能动态调节喷洒量与行进速度、减少农药漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精准喷洒对农业效率至关重要，但现有方法多聚焦作物检测并依赖预设喷洒参数，而人类操作者会综合环境条件、区域作物特性与农药类型动态调整策略，机器人缺乏这种情境自适应的决策能力。

Method: 以VLM为核心构建情境感知自适应喷洒框架，整合多源信息（作物类型、农药类型、天气数据）进行空间推理并输出自适应喷洒决策；下层采用基于模型预测路径积分（MPPI）的轨迹跟踪控制器，实现精确导航与在作物位置的准确喷洒。

Result: 对比结果显示所提方法在作物行检测上精度提升至少30%；在两种环境中的实验评估表明机器人能灵活调节喷洒量与行进速度，同时减少农药漂移。

Conclusion: VLM驱动的情境感知决策与MPPI控制的结合可有效提升农业喷洒的自主性、精度与环境适应性，减少农药浪费与漂移。

Abstract: Precision pesticide spraying is essential for optimizing application efficiency and ensuring uniform chemical distribution. Spraying performance is influenced by multiple factors, including environmental conditions such as temperature and wind speed, pesticide type, and the robot's capability to accurately perceive crops and target spray locations. Existing approaches predominantly emphasize crop detection and rely on predefined spraying parameters, whereas human operators dynamically adjust their spraying strategies by considering environmental conditions, region-specific crop characteristics, and the type of pesticide being applied. In this study, we propose a context-aware adaptive spraying framework based on Vision-Language Models (VLMs), which enables robots to leverage spatial reasoning and integrate information from multiple sources, including crop type, pesticide type, and weather data, to make adaptive and optimized spraying decisions. Subsequently, a trajectory-tracking controller based on Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control is employed to ensure precise navigation and accurate spraying at crop locations. The comparative results demonstrate that the proposed method improves accuracy by at least 30% in detecting crop rows. In addition, the experimental evaluations conducted in two environments further demonstrate the robot's ability to flexibly adjust spraying volume and travel speed, while reducing pesticide drift.

</details>


### [159] [Taming an End-to-End Autonomous Driving Policy for Urban Navigation of Quadruped Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08812)
*Joochan Kim,Chanuk Yang,Tackgeun You,Ziran Wang,Hwasup Lim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Go2-DrivoR将端到端自动驾驶轨迹规划框架DrivoR适配到四足机器人城市导航：通过goal token将轨迹生成条件化于局部坐标系子目标，改造以车辆为中心的打分公式，并重定义可行驶区域合规项与目标条件化ego progress项；仅用TartanGround仿真数据训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DrivoR面向车辆端到端规划，其打分层与可行驶区域定义不适配四足机器人在人行道场景下的短时域目标条件局部规划；作者希望在不重新设计核心解码器的前提下，把该框架扩展到足式机器人导航。

Method: 目标条件化改造：(1) 引入goal token，将轨迹生成以局部坐标系子目标为条件；(2) 重定义可行驶区域合规项以适配人行道导航；(3) 将原始ego progress项重写为goal-conditioned ego progress；保留原解码器结构，只改条件输入与打分公式。

Result: 仅在TartanGround仿真数据上训练，在未见过的仿真环境中waypoint条件规划性能提升，并可零样本迁移到真实世界开环轨迹预测。

Conclusion: 说明以轻量目标条件化+打分重构的方式，可将车辆端到端规划框架迁移至四足机器人导航，并具备一定仿真到真实的泛化能力；但仍限于短时域局部规划与开环预测验证。

Abstract: We present Go2-DrivoR, a goal-conditioned adaptation of the end-to-end autonomous driving trajectory planning framework DrivoR for urban navigation with quadrupedal robots. By conditioning trajectory generation on a local-frame subgoal through a goal token and adapting the vehicle-centric scoring formulation, the method extends DrivoR to short-horizon goal-conditioned local planning without redesigning its core decoders. Specifically, we redefine drivable-area compliance for sidewalk-oriented navigation and reformulate the original ego progress term as goal-conditioned ego progress. Trained exclusively on TartanGround simulation data, Go2-DrivoR improves waypoint-conditioned planning performance on unseen simulation environments and transfers zero-shot to open-loop real-world trajectory prediction.

</details>


### [160] [Autonomous Droplet Navigation via Model-Based Reinforcement Learning: Zero-Shot Transfer and Emergent Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08852)
*Rajneesh Anand,Mayuresh V. Kothare*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 首个用于开放无约束表面上液滴自主导航的机器人平台，采用无模型的基于模型的强化学习（MBRL），在仅50–150次物理回合内训成策略，具备零样本迁移、自主发现振荡去钉扎策略等能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自驱动实验室（SDL）依赖闭环自动化，但针对柔软可变形物质的物理操控基础设施仍缺失。开放表面上的液滴输运是一个典型难题：接触角滞后、毛细钉扎和表面异质性导致动力学部分可观测，经典基于模型的控制方法难以应对。

Method: 搭建两轴倾斜平台，表面覆薄硅油膜以驱动液滴，顶部相机提供实时视觉反馈；采用基于模型的强化学习训练控制策略，不使用仿真或解析模型，仅依赖50–150个物理回合（视几何复杂度而定），实现闭环自主液滴导航。

Result: 策略可零样本迁移至未见几何构型；液滴卡滞时自主发现振荡去钉扎（depinning）策略将其释放；完整训练流程在90分钟内完成。

Conclusion: 该工作将强化学习操控从刚性微机器人拓展到可变形软物质系统，为下一代自驱动实验室提供了可落地的物理操控基础设施。

Abstract: Self-driving laboratories (SDLs) are transforming chemical and materials discovery through closed-loop automation, yet automated infrastructure for physical manipulation of soft, deformable matter remains beyond current robotic platforms. A critical instance is autonomous droplet transport on an open surface, where contact-angle hysteresis, capillary pinning, and surface heterogeneity produce partially observable dynamics that pose significant challenges for classical model-based controllers. We introduce the first robotic platform for closed-loop autonomous liquid droplet navigation on an open, unconfined surface using model-based reinforcement learning. A two-axis tilting board coated with a thin silicone oil film drives the droplet, while an overhead camera provides real-time feedback. A learned policy was trained on just 50 to 150 physical episodes depending on geometric complexity, without simulation or analytical models. Beyond performance alone, the platform demonstrates three capabilities of interest to the SDL community: it robustly transfers zero-shot to unseen geometries; it autonomously discovers an oscillatory depinning strategy to free the droplet when it sticks; and it completes its full training pipeline in under 90 minutes. These results extend reinforcement-learning manipulation from rigid microrobots to deformable soft-matter systems for next-generation SDLs.

</details>


### [161] [CIRRA: Dual-Level Continual Instruction Reconciliation with Ongoing Execution for Embodied Robot Agents in Interactive Household Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08862)
*Ci Zhang,Enfu Nan,Arman Akbari,Lin Zhao,Li Wang,Chen Wang,Weiwei Chen,Yanzhi Wang,Geng Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CIRRA双层框架，用LLM语义推理加规则约束的结构化整合来处理机器人持续指令调和问题，避免整体重规划带来的歧义与冗余，并发布CHIRP基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家用机器人在执行任务时经常收到新指令，现有智能体倾向于重新生成或大幅改写剩余任务序列，导致计划歧义、逻辑不一致和冗余执行。作者希望在不破坏正在进行的执行主干的前提下，局部整合新指令。

Method: 将"持续指令调和"形式化；CIRRA先将来指令落地到唯一可执行技能并消解欠指定动作与位置；保留进行中的子任务序列作为执行主干，把新子任务插入位置匹配的片段生成整合候选；语义推理器仅评估被修改的片段以识别依赖与冲突，选出逻辑最连贯的局部整合；同时提出CHIRP文本基准（8个家庭环境、6类日常活动、120个episode）。

Result: CHIRP上决策一致率74.2%，超最强重规划基线30个百分点，每个正确融合决策均产生位置正确且无冲突的调度；在Unitree G1人形机器人上，CIRRA每次都能在正确时机打断进行中的技能，各项指标显著优于所有基线。

Conclusion: 结构保持的局部整合可缓解歧义与不一致，并复用共享子任务以减少冗余执行；该方法在文本基准与真机人形平台上均验证有效，为持续指令调和提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Household robots must accommodate new user instructions while executing ongoing tasks. Existing agents often regenerate or extensively revise the remaining task sequence, introducing plan ambiguity, logical inconsistency, and redundant execution. We formulate continual instruction reconciliation and propose CIRRA (Continual Instruction Reconciliation for Robot Agents), a dual-level framework combining LLM-based semantic reasoning with rule-constrained structural integration. CIRRA first grounds incoming instructions to unique executable skills and resolves underspecified actions and execution locations. It then preserves the ongoing subtask sequence as an execution backbone and generates integration candidates by inserting incoming subtasks into location-matched segments. The semantic reasoner evaluates only modified segments to identify dependencies and conflicts and select the most logically coherent local integration. This structure-preserving process maintains alignment with ongoing execution, mitigates ambiguity and inconsistency, and reuses shared subtasks to reduce redundant execution. We also introduce CHIRP (Continual Household Instruction Reconciliation and Planning), a text-based benchmark of 120 episodes across eight household environments and six categories of everyday activities. On CHIRP, CIRRA achieves 74.2% decision agreement, exceeding the strongest replanning baseline by 30 percentage points; every correct fusion decision yields a correctly placed, conflict-free schedule. On a Unitree G1 humanoid, CIRRA interrupts ongoing skills at the correct moment in every trial and significantly outperforms all baselines on every metric.

</details>


### [162] [Trajectory Planning without Trajectory Data: A Manifold-Guided Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08863)
*Silong Yong,Anji Liu,Cunxi Dai,Carl Busart,Guanya Shi,Yilun Du,Katia Sycara,Yaqi Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Ariadne：仅用状态观测学习状态空间流形，利用其几何结构进行轨迹规划，无需专家轨迹监督，可泛化到未见过的起止点约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹规划方法依赖生成模型在大量专家轨迹上训练，存在监督成本高、随序列长度扩展性差、对未见约束（如新起止点对）泛化差等问题。

Method: 不直接建模轨迹分布，而是学习状态空间的内在流形，并在学习到的流形上构建可行轨迹，从而在推理时以任务目标约束为条件生成轨迹。

Result: 在Maze2D与机器人运动规划基准上，Ariadne仅用状态监督即可构造可行路径，并泛化到未见起止点组合；在高维双臂规划任务中，性能与轨迹监督方法及经典规划器相当，且训练不需轨迹数据。

Conclusion: 以流形几何替代轨迹分布建模，可显著降低监督成本并提升约束泛化能力，为无轨迹数据的规划提供可行替代方案。

Abstract: A common way for trajectory planning is to leverage generative models trained on large collections of expert trajectories. At inference time, the model generates executable trajectories by conditioning on task goal constraints. However, trajectory-based methods rely on costly supervision, scale poorly with sequence length, and often generalize poorly to unseen constraints such as novel start-goal pairs. We propose an alternative to learn the underlying state-space manifold and use the geometry of the manifold for trajectory planning. This approach requires only state observations and enables generalization to unseen constraints by con- structing trajectories on the learned manifold of the state space. Experiments on Maze2D and robotic motion-planning benchmarks show that Ariadne constructs feasible paths from state-only supervision and generalizes to unseen start-goal combinations. On high-dimensional dual-arm planning, it remains competitive with trajectory-supervised and classical planners, while requiring no trajectory data for training.

</details>


### [163] [From Wearable Interfaces to Dexterous Policies: Contact Shifts and Tactile Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08870)
*Ruitong Tian,Fang Xu,Noah B. Wilson,Xianyao Li,Eric Jing Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究可穿戴数据采集外骨骼（DexUMI族）的手部几何设计如何影响所采集触觉数据及最终策略性能，证明采集硬件是机器人学习数据生成过程的一部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴接口让操作者用自己的手直接与物体交互采集灵巧演示，减少对目标机器人的依赖，但硬件本身也进入演示生成过程：接口几何会同时影响任务执行方式与可记录的触觉观测。作者希望回答"采集硬件本身是否应作为学习流程的变量被评估"。

Method: 对比同族两个DexUMI外骨骼版本（相同的机器人指令定义、映射流程与触觉模块类型，仅手侧几何不同）。在四项任务（盖子拧开、鸡蛋盒开启、USB插入、焊接工具取放）上比较界面差异带来的接触位置/出现与否变化，并分别用二值聚合输入与"空间+力"输入训练配对策略，进行跨界面、跨输入形式的对照评估。

Result: 改进界面降低主观体力需求、提升部分设备评分，并在基线被机械阻挡的精细抓取中实现触觉可及；盖子拧开主要体现接触位置变化，鸡蛋盒开启主要体现接触发生与否的变化。使用改进界面演示训练的策略在两项任务上成功率更高，存在显著的界面汇总效应；"空间+力"输入在全部四项任务上均优于二值聚合。

Conclusion: 可穿戴采集硬件是机器人学习数据生成过程的组成部分，应同时依据操作者使用体验、其使何种触觉交互可记录、以及所生成演示支撑的策略性能来评估。

Abstract: Unlike conventional teleoperation, wearable interfaces allow operators to collect dexterous demonstrations through their own hand motions while directly interacting with task objects. This direct interaction reduces dependence on the target robot during collection, but it also makes the collection hardware part of the physical process that generates each demonstration. Interface geometry can influence both how a task is performed and what tactile observations are recorded for learning. We study two versions of a DexUMI-family exoskeleton that share the same robot command definition, mapping procedure, and tactile module type but differ in hand-side geometry. The revised interface reduces reported physical demand, improves selected device ratings, enables tactile access in a precision grasp that is mechanically blocked by the baseline, and produces task-dependent changes in recorded contact. Lid twisting primarily exhibits a change in contact location, whereas egg carton opening primarily exhibits a change in contact occurrence. We then train matched policies using either a binary aggregate input or a spatial-plus-force input. On lid twisting and egg carton opening, policies trained on revised-interface demonstrations achieve higher success than those trained on baseline demonstrations, with a significant pooled interface effect. Across these and two additional tasks, USB insertion and soldering tool pick and place, the spatial-plus-force input likewise outperforms binary aggregation. These results show that wearable collection hardware is part of the data-generation process for robot learning. Such interfaces should therefore be evaluated not only through operator experience, but also through the tactile interactions they make recordable and the policy performance their demonstrations support.

</details>


### [164] [Toward Evidence-Driven Human-Agent-Robot Teaming for Earth-Independent Anomaly Triage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08933)
*Ignacio G Lopez-Francos,Alexis Gallagher,Samira Shalal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向深空自主异常分诊的人-智能体-机器人协同架构：AI 作为有界、可审查服务的状态协调器，通过分诊状态管理、船员具身智能体与移动机器人组成闭环证据链，并在硬件在环原型（Reachy Mini + Innate MARS）上以两个场景验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深空任务无法依赖地面实时支持；初始告警信息不足以确定故障根因，而判别性证据散布在乘员观察或乘员因安全、代价、可达性限制无法检查的位置。因此需要一种地球独立的异常分诊机制，让 AI 在有界授权下协调人类与机器人获取针对性证据。

Method: 以证据驱动的体系架构而非全自主控制器为设计原则：Agentic AI 作为状态协调器，管理有界、可审查的服务。核心组件包括：分诊状态管理器（维护假设、证据来源、不确定性、操作上下文、工具状态）、面向船员的具身智能体（引导观察、解释评估变更）、移动机器人（获取局部定向证据）。采用类型化接口隔离对话与编排同监测、机器人指令、上下文检索、安全关键控制。

Result: 用两个场景进行说明：一是基于真实 ISS 氨气误报的载人深空任务，疑似污染限制船员进入；二是载人月球基地的电源接口异常，机器人巡检可区分局部连接器故障与远程遥测中其他模糊原因。以 Reachy Mini 与 Innate MARS 移动机器人搭建了硬件在环集成原型并进行了演练。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是提出"权限受限的闭环证据回路"架构，并给出硬件在环集成原型验证，展示了人-智能体-机器人在无地面支持条件下协同分诊异常时的可行性与接口设计原则。

Abstract: Deep-space crews cannot rely on real-time ground support for urgent off-nominal events. Initial alerts may underdetermine cause, while discriminating evidence may reside in crew observations or at locations that are unsafe, costly, or unavailable for crew inspection. We present an evidence-driven architecture for human-agent-robot teaming in Earth-independent anomaly triage. Agentic AI is treated as a stateful coordinator over bounded, inspectable services rather than as a fully autonomous vehicle controller. A triage state manager maintains hypotheses, evidence provenance, uncertainty, operational context, and tool status; a crew-facing embodied agent elicits observations and explains assessment changes; and a mobile robot acquires targeted, localized evidence. Typed interfaces separate dialogue and orchestration from monitoring, robot command, context retrieval, and safety-critical control. Two scenarios illustrate the architecture: a crewed deep-space mission based on an actual ISS ammonia false alarm, where suspected contamination restricts crew access, and a power-interface anomaly at a crewed lunar base, where robotic inspection distinguishes a local connector fault from other causes ambiguous in remote telemetry. Our main contribution is an authority-bounded closed evidence-loop architecture, exercised in a hardware-in-the-loop integration prototype using Reachy Mini and an Innate MARS mobile robot.

</details>


### [165] [HULK: Learning Whole-Body Forceful Loco-Manipulation for Humanoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08970)
*An Dang,Arturo Flores Alvarez,Yu-Ming Chen,Conor Mc Gartoll,Helen Sun,Aaron Ames,Nima Fazeli,Manikantan Nambi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HULK 是一个用于大幅重物全身 loco-manipulation 的全身控制框架：用 MPC 引导强化学习（预测负载动力学），训练两个教师（腕部受力下的手臂跟踪、抱持大物体的行走）并蒸馏为单一策略，加入捕获点控制障碍函数(CBF)提升负载下平衡；在仿真和 Unitree G1 上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型重物会改变人形机器人质心并对上半身施加持续负载，导致平衡困难与指令跟踪退化。现有全身控制方法在强外力交互下缺乏对负载动力学的显式预测与平衡约束，因此需要面向"大力交互"的 loco-manipulation 框架。

Method: 1) 用 MPC 生成负载动力学预测以引导 RL（MPC-guided RL）；2) 训练两个教师策略：腕力作用下的手臂运动跟踪、将大物体贴抱于身前的行走；3) 训练时用 capture-point 控制障碍函数 (CBF) 增强腕力教师，提升负载下平衡；4) 将两教师蒸馏进单一策略；5) 在仿真与 Unitree G1 实机上评估。

Result: 仿真中，带障碍函数的教师在每臂 10 kg 负载下取得最低的前向与侧向速度跟踪误差，并将聚合 DCM 偏移幅度相对纯 MPC-guided RL 降低 35.7%；腕力教师可承受高达 130 N 的躯干推力扰动；在 Unitree G1 上完成评估。

Conclusion: 显式负载动力学预测 + 平衡约束（CBF）可显著改善人形机器人重载下的 loco-manipulation 平衡与跟踪性能，双教师蒸馏可统一腕力交互与负重行走能力，具备实机可行性。

Abstract: Humanoid loco-manipulation of large, heavy objects demands forceful interaction across the entire body. However, such payloads shift a humanoid's center of mass and impose sustained loads across the upper body, challenging balance and command tracking. We present HULK, a whole-body control framework for forceful loco-manipulation. Using model predictive control (MPC) to guide reinforcement learning with predictions of the loaded dynamics, we train two teachers: one tracks arm motions under wrist forces, and the other locomotes while holding large objects against the body. A capture-point control barrier function augments the wrist-force teacher during training to improve balance under load. We distill both teachers into a single policy. Evaluation spans simulation and the Unitree G1. In simulation, the teacher with the barrier function achieves the lowest forward and lateral velocity tracking errors at 10 kg per arm among evaluated controllers and reduces aggregate divergent component of motion (DCM) excursion magnitude by 35.7% relative to MPC-guided reinforcement learning alone. Our wrist-force teacher withstands torso push disturbances of up to 130 N.

</details>


### [166] [PhysEvo: Astra Can Act, Let It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08995)
*Wenqing Tian,Zeyu Zhang,Zhaocheng Liu,Fengwei Liu,Qiang Liu,Liang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhysEvo 提出面向冻结模型的"物理递归自我改进"框架：任务智能体执行机器人操作，元智能体依据轨迹诊断失败、修订工具与技能并自我改进诊断工具，实现无权重更新的持续能力积累，并在仿真与真机上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能体（如 Astra）能力受限于其观察与控制世界的接口（工具、技能、控制原语、观测策略），而提升通常依赖模型权重更新或单独训练动作策略，成本高且难以持续。作者希望在不更新冻结模型的前提下，通过"递归自我改进"让智能体把行动后果转化为对自身行动方式的可持久化、可验证的改进。

Method: PhysEvo 采用双角色单模型架构：任务智能体（task agent）执行机器人任务；元智能体（meta-agent）利用产生的轨迹进行失败诊断，修订工具与技能并验证修正；元智能体还可改进自身的诊断工具，使保留下来的修订同时服务于后续行动与后续自我改进。整个过程不更新模型权重，也不单独训练动作策略，从而发展出关节级控制、证据寻求式观测和可复用操作技能；并可将在仿真中演化出的 harness 部署到真机继续迭代。

Result: 在 42 项 RoboDojo 任务上，对保留的任务专用部署版本进行留出布局（held-out layout）评测，五维平均分 68.14/100、成功率 62.00%，显著高于对比中最强的公开参照 RoboDawn 一次性 Astra 智能体的 47.17%；在 8 项对直接 Astra 具有挑战性的操作任务上成功率达 55.00%，而直接 Astra 参照仅 1.25%；将仿真演化得到的 harness 部署到 AgileX PiPER 并继续技能修订，在五项真实任务 25 次试验中取得 90.60/100 平均分与 84.00% 成功率。

Conclusion: PhysEvo 表明，冻结模型也能通过将行动后果转化为持久、可测试的工具与技能修订，实现物理层面的递归自我改进，在留出布局仿真与真机部署中均大幅超越一次性智能体基线，为无需权重更新的具身智能体持续进化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Astra can act, yet reliable manipulation depends on the system through which it observes and controls the world. We introduce PhysEvo, a framework for physical recursive self-improvement (RSI) around a single frozen model. A task agent executes robot tasks; a meta-agent uses the resulting trajectories to diagnose failures, revise tools and skills, and test corrections. The meta-agent can also improve its own diagnostic tools, so retained revisions support both later action and later self-improvement. This process develops joint-level control, evidence-seeking observation, and reusable manipulation skills without model-weight updates or a separately trained action policy. Across 42 RoboDojo tasks, held-out-layout evaluation of retained task-specific deployment versions yields a five-dimension average score of 68.14/100 and 62.00% success, compared with 47.17% for RoboDawn's one-shot Astra agent, the strongest published reference in our comparison. On eight manipulation tasks challenging direct Astra, PhysEvo achieves 55.00% success, compared with 1.25% for the direct-Astra reference. Deploying the simulation-evolved harness on AgileX PiPER and continuing skill revision yields 90.60/100 average score and 84.00% success across 25 trials on five real-world tasks. PhysEvo turns the consequences of action into persistent, testable changes to how a frozen model acts and improves.

</details>


### [167] [MimicX: Policy-in-the-Loop Supervision Refinement for Video-Driven Humanoid Motion Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09055)
*Shuaijun Liu,Chenglong Zhang,Xuhao Liu,Feiyang You,Yifan Liao,Shuyang Hao,Chaozhe Zhang,Chengyu Wu,Zhen Sun,Ningxin Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MimicX：利用策略在执行中的反馈（policy-in-the-loop）来定位视频驱动人形运动跟踪中的困难过渡与受影响身体区域，联合调整跟踪目标与重置课程，从而提升跟踪精度与可执行时长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视频为人形机器人学习提供了丰富运动目标，但视觉上看似合理的参考动作在物理执行时仍会持续失败。这些失败点恰恰指示了监督信号应在何处改变，然而现有流程缺乏利用执行反馈反向修正监督的机制。

Method: MimicX 是一个策略在环框架。从重建并重定向后的运动出发，先定位困难过渡片段与受影响的身体区域；随后联合自适应调整跟踪目标（tracking objectives）与重置课程（reset curriculum）以支持策略续训（policy continuation）；通过重复 rollout 验证，在跟踪守卫（tracking guards）约束下筛选以执行为优先的改进。MimicX-HLoop 通过异构执行加速这一反馈回路。

Result: 在四个核心视频任务上，相对 Fixed Reference 基线一致提升跟踪精度与鲁棒执行时长（Robust Execution Horizon）。任务平均：身体跟踪误差降低 25.7%，执行时长增加 255.6%。额外的视频、运动参考与碰撞场景研究验证了方法在核心任务之外的泛化性。

Conclusion: MimicX 将策略执行失败转化为可操作的监督信号，回答“该改哪里”与“该保留哪种改进”两个问题，形成视频驱动人形运动跟踪的闭环精炼范式。

Abstract: Human videos provide rich motion targets for humanoid learning, yet visually plausible references can still produce persistent failures under physics-based execution. These failures reveal where training supervision should change. We present MimicX, a policy-in-the-loop framework that uses execution feedback to refine video-driven humanoid motion tracking. Starting from reconstructed and retargeted motion, MimicX localizes difficult transitions and affected body regions, then jointly adapts tracking objectives and the reset curriculum for policy continuation. Repeated rollout verification selects execution-priority improvements subject to tracking guards. Across four core video tasks, MimicX consistently improves tracking accuracy and Robust Execution Horizon relative to the Fixed Reference baseline. Task-averaged results show a 25.7% reduction in body-tracking error and a 255.6% increase in execution horizon. Additional video, motion-reference, and collision-scene studies evaluate the method beyond the core tasks, while MimicX-HLoop accelerates feedback through heterogeneous execution. Overall, MimicX turns policy failure into actionable supervision for deciding what to refine and which refinement to retain.

</details>


### [168] [Workhorse: Learning Robust Whole-Body Humanoid Loco-Manipulation from Human Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09117)
*Songbo Hu,Qiayuan Liao,Yufeng Chi,Kevin Zakka,Yakun Sophia Shao,Pieter Abbeel,Koushil Sreenath*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Workhorse：从无机器人的人类演示中学习人形机器人全身接触丰富操作，视觉规划器预测躯干/双腕/双脚五链目标，RL全身跟踪器在真机Unitree G1上执行；两策略独立在同一人类姿态数据上训练（无需retargeting），并通过互相模仿对方部署误差做数据增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人难以从自我中心RGB与本体感知中规划接触丰富的全身操作；依赖遥操作/动作重定向的数据采集成本高，且规划器与跟踪器之间的部署误差不匹配会破坏闭环执行。

Method: 两阶段解耦：①视觉规划器从自我中心RGB预测五链目标位姿（躯干、双腕、双脚）；②强化学习全身跟踪器在机器人上跟随该目标。两策略各自在同一录制人类姿态上独立训练，不做动作重定向。关键技巧是交叉误差增强：为每个策略的训invariant数据注入另一策略在部署时产生的误差，从而提升闭环鲁棒性。

Result: 真机Unitree G1上完成：用双手和踢击分拣箱子、接住抛来的箱子、弄倒并爬越手提箱；分拣中展示了被人推开或被拿走箱子后的恢复。仿真复刻房间中分拣成功率77%，在40 N·s推力下64%；用同一演示重训两策略后，第二台仿真人形在无推力下分拣成功率达83%。

Conclusion: 无需机器人数据与动作重定向的人类演示可直接迁移到人形全身接触丰富操作，规划器-跟踪器间的误差感知数据增强是闭环成功的关键；系统具备一定抗扰动与错误恢复能力。

Abstract: Humanoid robots still struggle to plan contact-rich whole-body manipulation from egocentric RGB and proprioception. Workhorse learns such manipulation from robot-free human demonstrations. A visual planner predicts five-link targets: the poses of the torso, both wrists, and both feet. A reinforcement-learning whole-body tracker follows them on the robot. Both policies train separately on the same recorded human poses, without retargeting. We augment the training data of each policy to imitate the errors that the other makes at deployment. On a real Unitree G1, Workhorse sorts boxes with its hands and a kick, catches a thrown box, and topples and climbs a suitcase. During box sorting, we show recoveries after a person pushes the robot or takes the box away. In a simulated copy of the demonstration room, the system completes box sorting in 77% of episodes, and in 64% under 40 N.s pushes. With both policies retrained from the same demonstrations, a simulated second humanoid completes box sorting in 83% of episodes without pushes.

</details>


### [169] [FlashNeRD: Performance-First Contact-Rich Neural Robot Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09130)
*Mohammadmehdi Ataei,Mohammad Amin Nabian,Jie Xu,Miles Macklin,Yashraj Narang,Eric Heiden,Octi Zhang,Yunrong Guo,Ruben D'Sa,Oliver Hennigh,Gavriel State,Sanjay Choudhry*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FlashNeRD 通过并行流式架构、任意位置接触编码器与双向耦合，将 NeRD 的学习型机器人动力学提速并提升精度，在五个机器人上实现更快动力学、策略学习与规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NeRD 以学习模型替代数值动力学、保留解析碰撞检测，但存在三大瓶颈：相对原模拟器加速有限、仅支持预定义接触点、与被操作物体无双向耦合。

Method: 提出并行流式架构使单步预测更快更准；设计可在任意接触位置接受接触信息的编码器；用解析求解器模拟物体，实现机器人与物体的双向耦合。

Result: 五个机器人实验中动力学更快更准；三个机器人上动力学模型比优化 NeRD 快达 55× 且长程滚动更准；PPO 训练 ANYmal 运动策略仅 34 秒，比解析模拟器快 3.8×、比优化 NeRD 快 2.7×；DIAL-MPC 下机器人攀爬全部六个测试平台（固定接触 NeRD 仅一个），规划时间约为解析模拟器一半；立方体重定向策略达成率在模拟器策略目标计数的 2% 以内。

Conclusion: 消除 NeRD 三大限制后，学习型机器人动力学在速度、精度与接触泛化上均显著提升，并可直接支撑策略学习与推理期规划。

Abstract: Compared with analytical physics, learned dynamics models promise robot simulation that is faster, inherently differentiable, and easily adaptable to real data. Neural Robot Dynamics (NeRD) pursues this by keeping collision detection analytical and replacing a simulator's numerical dynamics for the robot with a learned model. Three limitations remain. NeRD offers little speedup over the simulator it learned from, accepts contact only at predefined points, and has no two-way coupling with objects it manipulates. FlashNeRD removes all three with a parallel streaming architecture that makes each prediction faster and more accurate, an encoder that accepts contacts wherever they occur, and two-way coupling with objects simulated by analytical solvers. Experiments across five robots show faster and more accurate dynamics, faster policy learning, and faster inference-time planning. Across three robots, FlashNeRD's dynamics model is up to $55\times$ faster than an optimized NeRD and more accurate over long rollouts. This speedup extends to policy learning, where PPO trains an ANYmal locomotion policy in 34 seconds, $3.8\times$ faster than the analytical simulator and $2.7\times$ faster than an optimized NeRD. With DIAL-MPC, a sampling-based MPC method, the robot climbs all six test platforms where fixed-contact NeRD manages one, at approximately half the analytical simulator's planning time. Cube-reorientation policies trained with FlashNeRD complete within 2% of the simulator-trained policy's target count.

</details>


### [170] [World Models Dream of Success: Diagnosing and Repairing Failure Insensitivity in Robot World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09134)
*Jiuyi Xu,Xiao Hu,Meida Chen,Peng Gao,Yang Ye,Yangming Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CureWM 通过"执行验证的反事实重放"修复已发布机器人世界模型的乐观偏差：在严重度网格上构造替代动作、仿真/真机验证结果，并用失败与存活成功样本微调已发布 checkpoint，无需改架构或训练目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人世界模型用于策略评估、规划与合成数据生成时，必须能区分成功动作与失败动作；但现有已发布模型（两个架构家族、四个 checkpoint）对动作变化敏感性弱，且在已验证失败上仍给出"成功式"预测。现有工作在训练中引入失败数据，但"哪些数据能修复已发布 checkpoint（不改架构与训练目标）"尚不清楚。

Method: CureWM：从成功示范出发，在严重度网格上构造替代动作，通过仿真或真机执行验证其结果，再在所得失败、存活成功与名义示范上联合微调已发布模型。该构造在共享起始上下文下提供受控的动作对比；诊断上使用潜在距离指标衡量"失败被预测为成功"的乐观程度。

Result: 在 484 个留出的 LIBERO 失败反事实上，乐观率由官方数据微调后的 80% 降至 30–43%（四个独立微调 CureWM 模型，均值 38%）。真机机械臂两项评测中，被潜在距离诊断为"成功式"的失败预测由仅用成功示范微调的 90% 降至 33%。在每任务失败数、成功重放数据与训练预算匹配时，反事实失败带来的成功–失败价值间隔为 0.124，而新采集的 on-policy 失败仅 0.014。

Conclusion: 结果支持用"执行验证的反事实重放"进行事后修复，并表明降低乐观偏差必须与成功–失败判别能力一并评估。代码已开源。

Abstract: Robot world models support policy evaluation, planning, and synthetic data generation, but these applications require predictions that distinguish successful actions from failures. Across four released checkpoints from two architecture families, we observe weak sensitivity to action changes and success-like predictions on verified failures. Although recent work incorporates failures into model training, which data can repair released checkpoints without changing their architecture or training objective still remains underexplored. To this end, we introduce CureWM, which constructs alternative actions from successful demonstrations across a severity grid, verifies their outcomes through execution in simulation or on hardware, and fine-tunes released models on the resulting failures and surviving successes alongside nominal demonstrations. This construction provides controlled action contrasts from shared starting contexts. On 484 held-out LIBERO failure counterfactuals, optimism falls from 80\% after fine-tuning on the official data to 30--43\% across four independently fine-tuned CureWM models (38\% mean). In two separate evaluations on a physical robot arm, failure predictions scored as success-like by a latent-distance diagnostic decrease from 90\% after fine-tuning on successful demonstrations alone to 33\% with CureWM. With failure counts per task, successful replay data, and training budget matched, counterfactual failures yield a success--failure value gap of 0.124, compared with 0.014 for freshly collected on-policy failures. These findings support execution-verified counterfactual replay for post-hoc repair and show why reduced optimism must be evaluated alongside success--failure discrimination. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [171] [A Unified Kinematic Representation Enables Reusable Biological Joint Moment Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09150)
*Jinwoo Hwang,Ilseung Park,Changseob Song,Vu Phan,Eni Halilaj,Inseung Kang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出以关节运动学作为中间表示，解耦硬件相关感知与生物关节力矩估计，使仅在开源生物力学数据上训练的估计器可跨髋/膝外骨骼与IMU平台工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动的生理状态估计（尤其生物关节力矩）广泛用于外骨骼控制，但估计器与设备特定的传感器配置强耦合，导致控制器难以迁移，且无法利用开源生物力学数据集。

Method: 以关节角度与角速度为输入，训练关节力矩估计器，训练数据仅来自开源生物力学数据集；在髋外骨骼、膝外骨骼和IMU运动捕捉套件三种平台上，于平地行走、上坡与下坡条件下进行跨平台评估。

Result: 髋外骨骼运动学输入下RMSE 0.17 Nm/kg、R²=0.79；膝外骨骼下RMSE 0.19 Nm/kg、R²=0.62；IMU推导的双侧髋膝踝运动学下RMSE 0.15 Nm/kg、R²=0.85。

Conclusion: 关节运动学作为中间表示可使仅用开源数据训练的估计器跨不同可穿戴平台运行，有望减少目标设备数据采集成本，支持外骨骼控制与可穿戴生物力学中可迁移的关节力矩估计。

Abstract: Objective: Data-driven models that estimate physiological states, particularly biological joint moments, are widely used in exoskeleton control. However, these estimators are often coupled to device-specific sensor configurations, limiting controller transfer and the use of open-source biomechanics datasets.
