Methods: We proposed joint kinematics as an intermediate representation that decouples hardware-specific sensing from downstream biological joint moment estimation. A joint-moment estimator using joint angles and angular velocities was trained exclusively on open-source biomechanics data and evaluated using kinematics from a hip exoskeleton, knee exoskeleton, and inertial measurement unit (IMU) sensor suite during level-ground, ramp-ascent, and ramp-descent walking. Results: The estimator achieved an root mean square error (RMSE) of 0.17 Nm/kg and coefficient of determination (R2) of 0.79 using hip exoskeleton kinematics, 0.19 Nm/kg and 0.62 using knee exoskeleton kinematics, and 0.15 Nm/kg and 0.85 using kinematics derived from IMUs across bilateral hip, knee, and ankle joints. Conclusion: Joint kinematics enabled an estimator trained only on open-source data to operate across distinct wearable platforms. Significance: This framework may reduce target-device data collection and support transferable biological joint moment estimation for exoskeleton control and wearable biomechanics.

</details>


### [172] [geodex: A Library for Motion Planning on Riemannian Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09165)
*Phone Thiha Kyaw,Ben Wei,Sepehr Samavi,Miguel Angel Rogel Garcia,Jonathan Kelly*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: geodex：一个开源 C++20（含 Python 绑定）的基于采样的运动规划库，将流形、黎曼度量、retraction 和采样器解耦为可互换组件，在 R^n、T^n、S^n、SO(2)/SE(2)/SO(3)/SE(3) 及其乘积与铰接机器人构型空间上支持用户自定义黎曼度量下的避障规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人构型空间上规划时，若用环境平坦度量（如欧氏距离），会忽略构型空间的内蕴几何（曲率、以及构型相关的代价度量），导致轨迹偏长、耗能高、不自然。现有流形优化库几何原语丰富但不做避障；通用运动规划库虽支持多种状态空间与自定义距离函数，却未把构型相关的黎曼度量当作驱动距离、插值与测地线的几何结构。因此需要一个统一二者、把黎曼度量纳入采样规划距离与插值接口的库。

Method: 提出 geodex：开源 C++20 库并提供 Python 绑定。核心设计是把四类几何对象——流形、流形上的黎曼度量、retraction（回缩）、采样器——实现为独立且可互换的模块，通过单一基于采样的运动规划接口暴露。规划器本身与具体空间无关：同一规划器不加修改地运行在 R^n、T^n、S^n、矩阵李群 SO(2)/SE(2)/SO(3)/SE(3)、这些空间的乘积、以及铰接机器人构型空间上，每个空间均可配备用户自定义黎曼度量，从而以该度量驱动距离计算、插值与测地线，并在其上进行避障规划。库随附文档、测试与可复现基准套件。

Result: 实现了在多种规范空间与铰接机器人构型空间上以用户自定义黎曼度量运行的统一采样规划器；同一规划器无需修改即可跨空间复用；库公开可用，包含文档、测试和可复现基准套件（摘要未给出定量轨迹长度/能耗对比数值）。

Conclusion: geodex 填补了"流形优化库（无避障）"与"通用运动规划库（不把构型相关黎曼度量作为几何驱动）"之间的空白，以模块化、可互换的几何组件和统一采样规划接口，使内蕴几何驱动的规划在机器人与流形空间上开箱可用；开源发布配合文档、测试与基准，便于复用与扩展。

Abstract: Planning motions that respect the intrinsic geometry of a robot's configuration space, including its curvature and a configuration-dependent notion of cost, yields shorter, lower-energy, and more natural trajectories than planning under the ambient flat metric. Existing libraries for optimization on manifolds provide rich geometric primitives but do not plan around obstacles. While general-purpose motion planning libraries support many state spaces and custom distance functions, they do not yet treat a configuration-dependent Riemannian metric as the geometry that drives distance, interpolation, and geodesics. We present geodex, an open-source C++20 library with Python bindings. The library exposes the manifold, its Riemannian metric, the retraction, and the sampler as independent, interchangeable components through a single sampling-based motion planning interface. The same planner runs unchanged on canonical spaces $\mathbb{R}^n$, $\mathbb{T}^n$, $\mathbb{S}^n$, matrix Lie groups such as $SO(2)$, $SE(2)$, $SO(3)$, and $SE(3)$, products of these spaces, and articulated-robot configuration spaces, each equipped with a user-defined Riemannian metric. We make geodex publicly available with documentation, tests, and a reproducible benchmark suite.

</details>


### [173] [Beyond Reconstruction: What Matters in Action Tokenization for Robot Policies?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09170)
*Haoran Chen,Jingtian Ji,Samuel Wheeler,Kaylene Caswell Stocking,Matthew Walter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ProAct，一种策略无关的动作分词器训练方法，在重建目标之外显式优化下游可预测性与鲁棒性，在 Robomimic、LIBERO、RoboTwin 及真机上平均提升 rollout 成功率 11.3、21.8、36.7 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归 VLA 策略依赖动作分词器把离散 token 解码为连续控制动作。现有方法多基于重建目标学习 token-动作映射，但作者的分析表明：仅重建精度高并不足以保证下游策略成功；策略能否对新观测预测出正确 token、以及未见 token 能否解码为合理动作（可预测性、鲁棒性）同样关键，而这两点不被重建目标直接激励。

Method: ProAct：在重建损失基础上以策略无关方式加入针对性增广/正则，使分词器训练同时面向下游可预测性与鲁棒性；训练只用动作数据集，不依赖具体策略，可跨多种分词器架构与基准使用。

Result: 在 Robomimic、LIBERO、RoboTwin 三个基准和多种分词器架构上，rollout 成功率平均提升 11.3 个百分点；迁移到 VLA 策略平均提升 21.8 个百分点；真实机器人操作平均提升 36.7 个百分点。

Conclusion: 有效的动作分词不应仅视为重建问题，而应设计成兼顾保真度、可预测性与鲁棒性的“策略接口”，从而直接服务于下游机器人策略表现。

Abstract: Autoregressive action-token policies such as vision-language-action models require action tokenizers to translate discrete token sequences into precise control actions in continuous space. Many action tokenizers learn the mapping between tokens and actions via a reconstruction objective. However, as we show through extensive analysis, sufficiently accurate action reconstruction is only one part of what makes a downstream robot policy successful. It is also critical that the policy is able to predict the right tokens for new observations, and that unseen policy token predictions still decode into reasonable actions. These properties are downstream of tokenizer training and are not directly incentivized by a reconstruction objective alone. In this work, we introduce Predictable and Robust Action Tokenization (ProAct), a tokenizer training method that strategically augments reconstruction with the goal of improving downstream predictability and robustness. ProAct is policy-agnostic and uses only action datasets for training. Across the Robomimic, LIBERO, and RoboTwin benchmarks and a diverse set of tokenizer architectures, ProAct improves rollout success by an average of 11.3 percentage points. These improvements also translate to vision-language-action policies and real-world robotic manipulation, yielding average gains of 21.8 and 36.7 percentage points, respectively. These results suggest that effective action tokenization should be designed as a policy interface that balances fidelity, predictability, and robustness, rather than as a reconstruction problem alone.

</details>


### [174] [CAP: Codebook-Aligned Prediction for Tokenized Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09178)
*Haoran Chen,Jingtian Ji,Samuel Wheeler,Kaylene Caswell Stocking,Matthew Walter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Codebook-Aligned Prediction (CAP)：在动作 tokenizer + 自回归策略框架下，直接把 tokenizer 的 codebook 向量复用为策略的分类原型（class prototypes），而非把 token 当作无关类别索引重新学习分类头。在四种量化器家族、三个仿真基准与两个真机任务上，CAP 在冻结 tokenizer 的前提下持续提升任务成功率，并证明增益来源是对 latent code 结构的复用而非 token 准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 tokenizer 的机器人策略在 tokenization 后丢弃了 tokenizer 学到的 latent code 结构，把离散 token 当作互不相关的类索引，从零训练新分类器。作者认为这一被丢弃的结构本身携带动作感知信息，值得保留。

Method: CAP 不改动 tokenizer 与策略主干，仅将 tokenizer codebook 中的 code vector 直接用作策略输出的类原型，从而在策略头中显式复用 latent 空间结构；并在四种量化器家族、三个仿真基准、两个真机任务上做对照实验，同时隔离分析 token 准确率、策略头变化等因素。

Result: CAP 在全部设置下相对标准 token 分类头稳定提升任务成功率，且 tokenizer 及其重建质量保持不变。分析显示增益并非来自更高的 token 预测准确率或仅策略头的变化，而是来自 codebook 提供的跨 token latent 结构信息，使 token 预测错误在动作空间中更“温和”，并改善策略主干学到的表征。

Conclusion: 动作 tokenizer 除提供离散目标外，还学到对下游策略有用的动作感知 latent 结构；下游策略训练时应保留并复用该结构，而非将其当作普通类别标签丢弃。

Abstract: Action tokenization converts continuous robot actions into discrete symbols that can be modeled autoregressively. However, existing tokenizer-based policies typically ignore the tokenizer's learned latent code structure: after tokenization, the policy treats tokens as unrelated class indices and learns a new classifier from scratch. We show that this discarded structure is valuable. We introduce Codebook-Aligned Prediction (CAP), a method that directly reuses the tokenizer's code vectors as policy class prototypes while leaving the tokenizer and policy backbone otherwise unchanged. Across four quantizer families, three simulation benchmarks, and two real-robot tasks, CAP consistently improves task success over standard token classification heads while holding the tokenizer (and therefore its reconstruction quality) fixed. Our analysis further shows that these gains are not explained by higher token accuracy or changes in the policy head alone. Instead, reusing the tokenizer codebook provides the policy with valuable information about the tokenizer's learned latent structure across tokens, making token prediction errors more benign in action space and improving the representations learned by the policy backbone. These results suggest that action tokenizers learn useful action-aware latent structure beyond discrete targets that should be preserved when training downstream policies.

</details>


### [175] [Embedded Evaluation of Task Admission Coalescing in Decentralized Multi-Robot Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09187)
*James Lott,Vahraz Honary*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究多机器人任务分配中"任务准入合并(coalescing)"机制：延迟新任务准入以控制分配器算力开销，同时权衡目标服务时延。基于CBAA、ACBBA、PI、HIPC四种MRTA算法，开展AGX Orin(3000任务实测计算延迟)、零计算对照组及RP2040硬件在环(96任务)三套实验，比较Eager即时准入与阈值2/4/8及"4任务+10s等待界"策略在三种到达率下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态任务场景中分配器通常即时接纳新发布任务，导致每个新任务都触发一次分配计算，造成处理器负载浪费。作者希望量化"任务准入合并"作为控制分配器算力开销的手段，并系统评估其带来的目标服务时延代价，尤其是不同到达强度与不同计算平台下该权衡是否值得。

Method: 在三种到达率下对比Eager即时准入与批大小b=2/4/8及"b=4且10s等待上界"策略。实验分三层：(1) AGX Orin上3000任务真实计算延迟测量；(2) 3000任务零计算配对对照(隔离计算成本)；(3) Pololu 3pi+ RP2040上96任务硬件在环。代表算法为CBAA、ACBBA、PI、HIPC，指标含分配器调用次数、处理器工作量、在线目标平均时延(区分准入等待与服务时延)。

Result: AGX 48个评估条件中31个实现调用次数与算力双降，节省幅度依算法而异；稀疏到达下成本最显著，b=4使在线目标平均时延增加20.08–23.57s，主要来自准入等待。有界准入在12个算法-负载条件中10个降低平均工作量，高到达HIPC降低19.35%工作量、时延仅增1.40s。RP2040上权衡更有利：四个匹配的中到达HIPC场景中，Count b=4使平均工作量降59.85%、服务时延降39.47%，而对应AGX场景时延反增27.69%。

Conclusion: 任务准入合并的有效性取决于到达强度、具体分配器行为以及计算在执行平台上的相对代价；在算力受限平台上收益更明显，甚至可同时降低工作量与服务时延。

Abstract: Multi-robot task allocators in dynamic missions commonly admit newly released tasks immediately, potentially invoking allocation for each new arrival. We evaluate task-admission coalescing as a mechanism for controlling allocator processor work while accounting for target-service latency, using CBAA, ACBBA, PI, and HIPC as a representative MRTA suite. The study comprises a 3,000-mission AGX Orin campaign with measured computation delay, 3,000 paired zero-compute missions, and a 96-mission Pololu 3pi+ RP2040 allocator hardware-in-the-loop campaign. Immediate (Eager) admission is compared with thresholds of two, four, and eight tasks and a four-task policy with a 10-s waiting bound across three arrival rates. Across the AGX experiments, coalescing reduces both allocator calls and processor work in 31 of 48 evaluated conditions, although the magnitude of the saving varies by allocator. The principal cost appears under sparse arrivals, where four-task batching increases online-target mean latency by 20.08-23.57 s, predominantly through admission waiting. Bounded admission reduces mean work in ten of twelve allocator-load conditions, including a 19.35% reduction with a 1.40-s mean latency increase for high-arrival HIPC. RP2040 experiments show that this tradeoff can become more favorable as processor constraints tighten. Across four matched medium-arrival HIPC scenarios, Count b=4 reduces mean RP2040 work by 59.85% and service latency by 39.47%, while the corresponding AGX cases increase latency by 27.69%. These results show that the usefulness of task-admission coalescing depends on arrival intensity, allocator-specific behavior, and the relative cost of computation on the execution platform.

</details>


### [176] [Belief-Space Planning with Planner-Conditioned Estimator Error under Intermittent Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09207)
*Peter M. Donley,Joseph L. Moore*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种信念空间规划方法，对"规划器条件化"的估计器误差进行预测、传播与惩罚，用仿射误差动力学+矩递推保留随机修正事件带来的不确定性，并在倾转旋翼VTOL舰船甲板着陆仿真中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信念空间规划器通常使用独立设计或现成的估计器，但规划器可获得估计器观测不到的状态相关信息。因此即使估计器在自身信息下最小化均方误差，其在规划器信息条件下的误差均值也非零。当未来修正是间歇且随机的，接受/拒绝误差均值之差会引入零均值假设所忽略的额外不确定性项。

Method: 用规划器条件化的估计器误差模型，将仿射误差动力学与矩递推结合，保留随机性引起的修正不确定性；给出在指定运行区间内预测估计器误差动力学的方法，并把预测误差矩纳入任务加权二次风险目标；在递推时域（receding-horizon）规划中用于VTOL舰船甲板着陆仿真。

Result: 条件化于规划器信息后，命令盲EKF的误差预测损失相对规划器无知模型降低13.4%，预测能力对运行区间与时域强依赖。16组配对闭环试验中，规划器将终端任务指标中位数从1.60降至0.99；对2s以后的估计器误差预测误差在1.3倍以内，而仅用协方差的规划低估达5.7倍。

Conclusion: 将估计器误差的规划器条件化均值与随机修正不确定性显式建模并纳入规划代价，可显著改善信念空间规划的性能与误差预测可信度；该思路对需精确风险量化的规划系统具有参考价值，但当前证据仍限于仿真。

Abstract: Belief-space planners with separately designed or off-the-shelf estimators may have access to state-relevant information the estimator does not observe. Consequently, even an estimator that minimizes mean-squared error under its own information can have a nonzero error mean when conditioned on planner information. When future corrections are intermittent and stochastic, differences between accepted and rejected error means introduce additional uncertainty terms to correction events that zero-mean assumptions ignore. In this paper, we propose a belief-space planning approach that predicts, propagates, and penalizes planner-conditioned estimator error along evaluated trajectories. A planner-conditioned estimator-error model combines affine error dynamics with a moment recursion that preserves stochastically-induced correction uncertainty. We describe a method by which to predict estimator error dynamics within specified operating regimes and incorporate predicted error moments into a task-weighted quadratic risk objective. We evaluate the approach in simulation for a tilt-rotor VTOL landing on a ship deck using receding-horizon planning. We find that conditioning on planner information reduced error-prediction loss for a command-blind EKF by 13.4% relative to a planner-ignorant model, with strongly regime and horizon-dependent forecasting abilities. In a small set of 16 paired closed-loop trials, the planner lowered the median terminal task gauge from 1.60 to 0.99, and predicted estimator error beyond 2s within a factor of 1.3, versus a 5.7-fold underprediction by covariance-only planning.

</details>


### [177] [Fast Planning for Multi-object Multi-target Throwing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09224)
*Zhengming Zhu,Yang Liu,Xiao Gao,Aude Billard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出多物体多目标抛掷（MOMT）统一规划框架：离线用倒立飞行动力学与机器人运动/动力学构建可行集模型，在线通过快速解匹配与状态过滤在5 ms内生成可行抛掷序列，在7-DoF机械臂+多指手上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物流与仓储自动化中，机械臂抛掷可扩大工作空间并加速流程，但单次抛掷吞吐量有限。借助拟人手的高自由度，一次挥扫抛掷多个物体到多个目标（MOMT）可显著提升吞吐，关键挑战在于轨迹时长与规划时间之间的权衡。

Method: 两阶段框架。离线：结合物体倒立飞行动力学与机器人运动学/动力学，构建可行抛掷集模型。在线：通过快速解匹配，过滤物体有效脱离状态与机器人可行状态，组合成抛掷序列，规划时间小于5 ms。目标位置在执行中随机变化时可在线重规划。硬件为7-DoF机械臂配多指手。

Result: 仿真中双物体协同抛掷相比独立单物体规划缩短执行时间最多46%，扩展至三物体仍保持该增益；真实实验双物体缩短29%（与理论50%的差距归因于抛掷间过渡开销）；目标位置执行中随机改变时，系统在100 ms延迟内重规划并成功命中新目标且不停止机器人。

Conclusion: 建立了首个面向MOMT抛掷的统一规划框架，仿真验证多物体可扩展性，实物验证双物体可行性，推动了高吞吐机器人操作的前沿，并附有方法及硬件实验视频。

Abstract: Robot throwing has emerged as a promising technique for improving efficiency in logistics and warehouse automation, by enlarging the workspace and speeding up the process. To significantly increase the throwing system's throughput, we develop strategies for throwing multiple objects in one swipe. Such multi-object multi-target throwing (MOMT) leverages the large degrees of freedom of anthropomorphic hands. The key is to quickly generate fast and feasible throwing motions, which involves a complex trade-off between short trajectory duration and short planning time. We solve this problem in two stages. Offline, we build a model of the feasible set by combining object's inverted flying dynamics and the robot's kinematics and dynamics. Online, we generate feasible throws through fast solution matching and filtering of object's valid detach state and robot's feasible state that can compose sequences of throws in less than 5 ms. We validate the framework on a 7-DoF manipulator equipped with a multi-fingered hand. In simulation, coordinated two-object throwing reduces execution time by up to 46% compared to independent single-object planning, and this improvement is maintained when scaling to three objects. Real-world experiments with two objects confirm a 29% reduction; the remaining gap to the theoretical 50% is attributed to inter-throw transition overhead. When target positions are randomly changed mid-execution, the system re-plans and successfully reaches the new targets within 100 ms latency without stopping the robot. These results establish the first unified planning framework for MOMT throwing -- demonstrating scalability to multiple objects in simulation and real-world feasibility on two-object tasks -- advancing the frontier of high-throughput robotic manipulation. A video summarizing the method and the hardware experiments is available at this https URL

</details>


### [178] [Co-Evolving Robot Orchestrators and Policies through Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09228)
*Xilun Zhang,Maggie Wang,Erik Bauer,Hong-Xing Yu,Huang Huang,Jiajun Wu,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Robo-COP 让 VLM 编排器与 VLA 策略在部署中协同进化：自采集技能示范、按需微调策略、并在验证技能提升后才采纳新策略，在仿真与真机任务上均提升保留集成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 agentic 机器人系统以冻结的 VLA 策略为核心，编排器只能规避策略失败而无法克服它，策略成为系统瓶颈；而单独微调策略又会与为旧策略行为调好的编排器脱耦。

Method: 提出 Robo-COP：在部署过程中让编排器与策略共同进化。系统从自身执行轨迹中筛选技能示范数据，当数据能针对反复出现的失败时微调策略，并且只有在新策略在对应训练技能上确有提升时才采纳该策略。

Result: 在十个 RoboLab 仿真任务上，平均保留集成功率由冻结策略基线的 64.8% 提升到 73.8%，而固定计划微调且无验证仅达 65.8%；在三个真实世界任务上，保留集成功率由 38.3% 提升到 50.0%。

Conclusion: 部署可被转化为自我改进的飞轮：机器人边做边学，每次执行改进都产生更好的数据供下一轮学习，从而突破单一策略瓶颈并实现持续泛化提升。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies trained on large datasets are capable within their training domains, yet they still fail to generalize to the variety of situations a robot meets in real-world deployment. Agentic robot systems complement the policy with a vision-language model (VLM) orchestrator that learns when to call the policy, how to instruct it, and when to use scripted skills instead. However, because the harness is built around a frozen policy that has limited language steerability, the orchestrator can avoid the policy's failures but never overcome them. The policy becomes the bottleneck of the whole system. Fine-tuning the policy can remove this bottleneck, but updating it alone decouples it from an orchestrator tuned to its old behavior. We propose Robo-COP, in which the orchestrator and policy co-evolve during deployment. Robo-COP curates skill demonstrations from its own executions, fine-tunes the policy when this data can address recurring failures, and adopts each new policy only after it improves the skills it was trained for. Across ten simulated RoboLab tasks, Robo-COP raises mean held-out success from 64.8% to 73.8% over the same harness with a frozen policy, while fine-tuning on a fixed schedule without verification reaches only 65.8%. On three real-world tasks, Robo-COP raises held-out success from 38.3% to 50.0%. Robo-COP turns deployment into a self-improving flywheel in which robots learn by doing, with each improvement in execution producing better data for the next round of learning. Videos and code are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [179] [RoboRender: Robot-Oriented Video Generation for Visual Sim-to-Real Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09254)
*Huang Huang,Wensi Ai,Ziyu Chen,Youhui Wang,Zijian Du,Yang Liu,Jiaolong Yang,Li Fei-Fei,Jiajun Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboRender 将仿真轨迹（深度视频+语言指令+机器人RGB掩码）转成照片级RGB视频用于策略学习，实现零样本真机迁移，平均成功率71%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真可大规模低成本生成机器人数据，但存在视觉sim-to-real差异，导致策略迁移失败。现有方法依赖中间表示，会丢失语义信息或需要在部署时增加感知模块。

Method: RoboRender训练一个面向机器人的视频生成模型，以仿真深度视频、语言指令和机器人RGB掩码视频为条件，在保留仿真几何、机器人运动与动作标签的同时合成真实纹理、背景与干扰物；生成的RGB视频与仿真器提供的状态和动作配对用于训练策略。

Result: 在机器人视频测试集上生成质量优于深度条件基线；在抓放、铰接物体操作和移动操作的实机实验中，用RoboRender生成数据训练的策略平均成功率71%，约为原始仿真渲染的7.1倍、常规视觉域随机化的3.6倍；每条轨迹生成更多视频可进一步提升性能，开门任务成功率提升65个百分点。

Conclusion: 生成式视频渲染可缓解视觉sim-to-real gap，实现零样本策略迁移，且策略性能随生成数据量增加而提升。

Abstract: Simulation enables large-scale, low-cost robot data generation, but policies trained in simulation often fail to transfer to the real world due to the sim-to-real visual discrepancies. Existing approaches often rely on intermediate representations, which can discard rich semantic information or require additional perception modules at deployment. We address this visual sim-to-real gap with RoboRender, a framework that converts simulated trajectories into photorealistic RGB videos for policy learning. RoboRender trains a robot-oriented video generation model conditioned on simulated depth videos, language instructions, and robot RGB mask videos, preserving simulator geometry, robot motion, and action labels while synthesizing realistic textures, backgrounds, and distractors. The generated RGB videos are paired with simulator-provided states and actions to train policies for zero-shot real-world deployment. On robot video test sets, our video model outperforms depth-conditioned video generation baselines in generation quality. In real-world experiments across pick-and-place, articulated-object manipulation, and mobile manipulation tasks, policies trained on RoboRender-generated data achieve a 71% average success rate, outperforming raw simulation renderings and conventional visual domain randomization by approximately 7.1x and 3.6x, respectively. We further show that policy performance improves with more generated videos per simulation trajectory, increasing opening-task success by 65 percentage points. These results demonstrate that generative video rendering mitigates the visual sim-to-real gap for zero-shot policy transfer. Project website: this https URL .

</details>


### [180] [Evaluating the Transfer of Co-Evolved Communication from 2D to 3D Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09280)
*Fernando Montes-Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究考察两个机器人智能体在2D离散模拟器中协同进化出的通信机制，迁移到带真实物理的3D模拟器后是否保留其功能角色，并分析时间预算、社会线索与角色不对称性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同进化通信机制通常在简化2D模拟器中训练，但其在更真实物理环境中的可迁移性尚不明确；需验证所得通信行为是否为环境特异的假象，还是具备跨模拟器鲁棒的功能性意义。

Method: 将2D离散模拟器中两智能体协同进化的通信控制器直接迁移至3D物理模拟器；系统改变回合时间预算（2,000–6,000物理步）、社会线索可用性以及两协同进化角色的不对称性；对8次独立进化运行进行对比，并通过控制智能体处理顺序排除物理引擎伪影。

Result: 成功率随评估时间预算近似线性增长，2,000至6,000物理步间未见平台期，说明短回合评估会低估控制器性能；社会线索在两种模拟器中主要起"拥堵协助"作用而非导航引导，2D中效应更强；8次独立运行显示不对称方向一致但幅度差异明显；控制处理顺序排除了物理引擎伪影。

Conclusion: 协同进化通信机制迁移到3D物理环境后仍具功能角色，但其表现受时间预算与线索解读方式影响；迁移后的性能差距与评估时长、线索功能变化及角色不对称性有关。

Abstract: This work examines the transfer of a co-evolved communication mechanism between two robotic agents from a discrete two-dimensional (2D) simulator to a three-dimensional simulator with real physics (3D). The study focuses on whether a communication mechanism co-evolved in a 2D environment retains its functional role after transfer to a 3D physics-based simulator. To support this analysis, the effects of the episode time budget, the social cue, and the asymmetry between the two co-evolved roles were examined. The results indicate that the success rate increased approximately linearly with the evaluated time budgets, with no evidence of a plateau between 2,000 and 6,000 physics steps, suggesting that evaluations based on shorter episodes may underestimate the performance of the trained controllers. In both simulators, the social cue functioned primarily as a jam- assistance mechanism rather than as a navigation guide, although with a more pronounced effect in 2D. Analysis of eight independent evolutionary runs revealed a consistent direction of asymmetry, although its magnitude varied across runs. Controlling the processing order between agents allowed us to rule out an artifact of the physics engine. Finally, the results are discussed in terms of the factors that may contribute to the remaining performance gap observed after transfer.

</details>


### [181] [LACE-CRAFT: Robot Co-Design with Actor Inheritance and Blackboard Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09283)
*Yuhan Wen,Jiawei Wang,Qixuan Zhang,Yusen Qin,Lan Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 LACE-CRAFT 机器人协同设计框架：用「继续学习 vs 策略自适应」两条分支加冻结基线做公平对比，并以多智能体分工机制（F/M/R/I 角色）生成和交叉评审形态-奖励配对方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人形态搜索与策略学习通常耦合，但每个新设计都从零训练会丢弃已获得控制经验，导致样本效率低、评估成本高。作者希望复用现任设计的完整学习状态，让新形态候选不必重新学起，同时建立可比的评估口径来判断「保留旧策略」与「为新形态适配策略」孰优。

Method: LACE：从现任机器人的完整学习状态继续训练，并把其 actor 参数与观测统计量迁移给兼容的挑战者设计；由固定任务指标在「继续学习分支 / 自适应分支 / 冻结现任」三方之间做选择。CRAFT：通过共享实验记录与行为回放，协调 Feedback、Morphology、Reward、Integration 四类角色，生成并交叉评审成对提案。生成式扩展把生成网格转成可编辑的铰接模型（含关节配置、执行器接口与同步更新的仿真资产）。

Result: 在五个运动（locomotion）基准上，三个评估种子的平均得分比 D2C 高 6.4%–91.9%。两种方法在五轮内各训练 30 个新的形态-奖励配对，LACE 额外使用 4 个继续训练单元。做了五项任务级消融，考察策略继承与回放衍生反馈的作用。另制作实物原型，展示室内行走并说明从几何到硬件的流程。

Conclusion: 复用学习状态可显著降低新形态的适应成本，且「继续学习 vs 策略自适应」的分支式比较比单纯从零训练更高效；多角色协同生成加交叉评审能产出可用的形态-奖励配对，生成式几何到铰接模型再到硬件的管线具备可落地性。

Abstract: Robot co-design couples morphology search with policy learning, yet training every new design from scratch discards acquired control experience. We present LACE-CRAFT, which compares continued learning on the current robot with policy adaptation to new morphology-reward pairs. LACE resumes the incumbent's full learning state and initializes compatible challengers with its actor parameters and observation statistics. A fixed task metric selects among both branches and the frozen incumbent. CRAFT coordinates Feedback, Morphology, Reward, and Integration roles through shared experimental records and behavioral replays to generate and cross-review paired proposals. A generative extension converts generated meshes into editable articulated models with configured joints, actuator interfaces, and consistently updated simulation assets. Across five locomotion benchmarks, mean scores over three evaluation seeds are 6.4-91.9% higher than D2C. Both methods train 30 new morphology-reward pairs over five rounds; LACE additionally uses four continuation training units. Five-task ablations examine policy inheritance and replay-derived feedback. A fabricated prototype demonstrates indoor walking and illustrates the geometry-to-hardware workflow.

</details>


### [182] [Co${}^{2}$Skill: Whole-Body Control via Skill Composition for Long-Horizon Human-Environment Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09291)
*Jeonghwan Kim,Hyeonwoo Kim,Hanbyul Joo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出统一策略框架 Co²Skill，用预训练运动先验+任务/相位依赖观测掩码，结合目标条件 loco-manipulation 课程与跨任务课程，将全身场景交互与灵巧操作整合进同一控制公式，实现顺序任务执行与边交互边操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于物理的控制器虽能分别在"全身场景交互"（如坐、站、爬、上下楼梯）与"灵巧物体操作"上生成物理合理行为，但二者基本独立建模，缺乏统一策略来同时完成场景交互与物体操作，难以支撑复杂非结构化环境中的顺序任务。

Method: 构建在预训练运动先验之上的统一策略；采用任务与相位相关的观测掩码选取当前交互目标相关信息；设计目标条件 loco-manipulation 课程：以部分参考引导保证精度，同时从多样初始状态探索，并允许超出示范轨迹的目标导向执行；再引入跨任务课程，联合训练单项技能与选定任务序列，在任务边界保持物理状态、在后续场景交互中维持抓取。

Result: 在坐、站、攀爬、楼梯通行、目标导向操作，以及顺序执行和随机不同条件下进行了评估；并在室内环境中演示技能组合，表明这些技能可在同一控制公式下集成。

Conclusion: 通过统一策略公式与两级课程设计，可将场景交互与灵巧操作融合于单一控制器，支持顺序任务执行与同时的场景交互加物体操作，为复杂环境下的全身灵巧行为提供可行方案。

Abstract: Achieving human-level dexterity in complex, unstructured environments requires the seamless integration of whole-body scene interaction and dexterous object manipulation skills. While existing physics-based controllers generate physically plausible behaviors in each domain, they largely address these two capabilities independently. In this paper, we present Co${}^{2}$Skill that integrates scene interaction and dexterous manipulation through a unified policy formulation. Built on a pretrained motion prior, the policy uses task and phase dependent observation masks to select information relevant to the current interaction goals. We introduce a goal-conditioned loco-manipulation curriculum that combines partial reference guidance for precision with exploration from varied initial states while allowing goal-directed execution beyond the demonstrated trajectories. We further introduce a cross-task curriculum that jointly trains individual skills and selected task sequences, preserving physical states across task boundaries and maintaining grasps during subsequent scene interactions. Together, these support sequential task execution and simultaneous scene interaction with object manipulation. We evaluate sitting, standing, climbing, stair traversal, and goal-directed manipulation, together with sequential execution and with random different conditions. Additionally, we demonstrate skill compositions in indoor environments, illustrating their integration within the same control formulation.

</details>


### [183] [Adaptive Risk-Certified Event-Triggered Replanning for Dynamic Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09302)
*Richie R. Suganda,Bin Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CERT-Replan框架，用在线校准的障碍预测不确定性（保形预测）计算障碍风险，作为MPC重规划的提前预警触发信号，避免反复修正同一标称轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态环境中的安全导航依赖障碍物预测，但预测误差具有不确定性、非平稳性，可能引发罕见但安全关键的失效。现有CBF安全滤波器只能拒绝当前不安全控制，无法判断"当前有限时域规划模式"本身是否随不确定性演化而变得不安全。

Method: 用保形预测在线校准按时域索引的障碍预测残差，得到不确定性半径并评估动态障碍安全裕度。单步安全滤波器保护下一步控制；时域级风险监控器评估当前MPC rollout上预测障碍违反损失的上尾CVaR，超过预算时拒绝当前规划模式并切换到低风险备选（速度剖面、走廊、同伦类）。

Result: 非平稳基准下碰撞相比仅安全滤波基线降低83.3%，相比简单重规划触发降低77.8%，平均安全滤波介入减少32.4%；结合Trajectron++达到96%无碰撞运行。硬件实验与机载运行时分析证明计算可行。

Conclusion: 将校准后的障碍风险作为重规划的早期预警信号，优于被动安全滤波与朴素重规划触发，在保持计算可行的同时显著提升动态避障安全性。

Abstract: Safe navigation in dynamic environments requires robots to plan under obstacle predictions whose errors are uncertain, non-stationary, and can induce rare but safety-critical failures. Existing control-barrier-function safety filters can reject immediately unsafe controls, but they provide little guidance on when the current finite-horizon planning mode itself is becoming unsafe as prediction uncertainty evolves. We propose Conformal Event-Triggered Risk-Certified Replanning (\emph{CERT-Replan}), a framework that uses calibrated barrier risk as an early-warning signal for replanning. CERT-Replan calibrates horizon-indexed obstacle-prediction residuals online and uses the resulting uncertainty radii to evaluate dynamic-obstacle safety margins. A one-step safety filter protects the next applied control, while a horizon-level risk monitor evaluates the upper-tail CVaR of predicted barrier-violation losses along the current MPC rollout. When this risk exceeds an allocated budget, CERT-Replan rejects the current planning mode and selects a lower-risk alternative, such as a different speed profile, corridor, or homotopy class, rather than repeatedly correcting the same nominal plan. In a non-stationary benchmark, CERT-Replan achieves an \(83.3\%\) collision reduction relative to the safety-filter-only baseline \textcolor{black}{and a \(77.8\%\) reduction relative to simple replanning triggers}, while reducing average safety-filter intervention by \(32.4\%\). \textcolor{black}{With Trajectron++, CERT-Replan achieves \(96\%\) collision-free operation. Hardware experiments and onboard runtime profiling demonstrate computational feasibility.}

</details>


### [184] [Predicted Futures Are Not Enough: Learning Executable Goals for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09309)
*Tzu-Yu Chuang,Ching-Hsiang Chang,Yi-Hsiu Lee,Yi-Ting Chen,Min Sun,YuanFu Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出“实体级目标读出（Entity-Level Goal Readout）”，把3D trace世界模型的预测转化为可执行的SE(3)终端目标，实现预测到执行的显式接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式世界模型能预测操作场景的未来演化，但这些未来并不直接给出控制所需的紧凑任务变量；仅以未来预测为监督时，终端目标精度并非显式学习目标，导致预测与可执行控制之间存在接口缺口。

Method: 将物体中心位姿预测与基于观测深度的平移接地结合，输出紧凑的SE(3)终端目标；由共享的Pose-Native Executor结合在线物体位姿反馈执行闭环控制，无需重跑世界模型。通过目标诊断直接度量终端目标精度，并用受控平移扰动刻画目标误差下的执行退化。

Result: 五类操作任务平均成功率79.69%；Franka机械臂零样本部署在标称StackCube上73.33%，含干扰物66.67%，PickPlate（策略训练未见目标）75.00%。

Conclusion: 预测-执行接口应作为世界模型规划中显式的可学习组件，而非控制管线中附带的后期处理。

Abstract: Generative world models provide rich predictions of how manipulation scenes may evolve toward task objectives, yet those futures do not directly expose the compact task variables required by control. When training supervises future prediction alone, terminal goal accuracy is not an explicit learning objective, even when geometric recovery is available. We present Entity-Level Goal Readout, a learned prediction-to-execution interface that makes the executable terminal goal an explicit output of a 3D trace world model. It combines object-centric pose prediction with translation grounded in observed depth to produce a compact goal in SE(3). A shared Pose-Native Executor consumes this fixed goal with online object-pose feedback for closed-loop control without rerunning the world model. Across five manipulation tasks, the pipeline achieves a mean success rate of 79.69%. Goal diagnostics directly measure terminal goal accuracy, while controlled translation perturbations characterize how execution degrades under goal error. Zero-shot deployment on a Franka arm achieves 73.33% success on nominal StackCube, 66.67% with distractors, and 75.00% on PickPlate with a target unseen during policy training. These results support treating the prediction-to-execution interface as an explicit learned component of world-model planning rather than incidental post-processing in the control pipeline itself. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [185] [ClimbLab: MATLAB Simulation Platform for Legged Climbing Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09315)
*Kentaro Uno,Warley F. R. Ribeiro,Yusuke Koizumi,Keigo Haji,Koki Kurihara,William Jones,Kazuya Yoshida*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开源MATLAB仿真与分析平台，支持带浮动基座的任意多足攀爬机器人建模，可在任意环境中仿真行走与攀爬。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向腿式攀爬机器人研究，缺乏可灵活配置环境参数（倾角、重力、地面刚度、点云地形）并支持任意肢体构型多体建模的仿真分析工具。

Method: 基于刚体动力学引擎构建浮动基座铰接多体系统；可配置倾角、重力、地面刚度等环境变量，支持任意点云作为地形图；给出仿真器结构、计算流程，并展示四足机器人壁面抓附与陡坡攀爬的示例。

Result: 实现了通用腿式攀爬机器人仿真平台，并完成四足机器人壁面抓附与陡坡攀爬的代表性仿真示例。

Conclusion: 该开源MATLAB平台为腿式攀爬机器人的建模、运动仿真与环境适应性分析提供了通用工具，但仅给出仿真验证，未包含实机实验。

Abstract: This paper presents an open-sourced MATLAB simulation and analysis platform dedicated to legged climbing robots. This simulator enables the design of any limbed robotic system as an articulated multi-body with a floating base and simulates it walking and climbing in an arbitrary environment. The main variable environmental parameters are inclination, gravity, and ground stiffness, and any point cloud can be installed as the terrain map. Furthermore, the simulator employs a rigid body dynamics engine. This paper first describes the simulator structure, and the computational flow and next presents the representative simulation examples where quadrupedal robots assumed gripping on the wall or climbing on the steep slope.

</details>


### [186] [MCFR: A Mask-Guided Coarse-to-Fine Regression Framework for Robust Multi-Variant Board-to-Board Connector Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09327)
*Guanghui Shen,Song Wang,Dan Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 MCFR——掩膜引导的由粗到细回归框架，用于多型号板对板(BTB)连接器自动插装，通过目标掩膜先验与光度细化抑制背景伪相关，真实装配成功率99.25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3C制造中BTB连接器自动插装需高视觉精度与部署鲁棒性；多型号连接器形貌/外观差异大导致跨型号泛化困难，且固定视角检测下训练与部署失配引入背景伪相关，退化真实性能。

Method: MCFR框架：引入目标感知掩膜先验（object-aware mask prior）隔离前景、抑制背景干扰；采用由粗到细的位姿回归，并加入显式光度细化(photometric refinement)提升对齐精度与鲁棒性。

Result: 在自建多型号数据集、BTB批量插装试验台及真实手机装配任务上，一致优于代表性基线，真实插装平均成功率99.25%。

Conclusion: 验证了掩膜引导由粗到细回归对多型号BTB连接器自动装配的有效性与实际部署潜力，可作为3C精密装配的落地范式。

Abstract: Automated insertion of board-to-board (BTB) connectors in 3C manufacturing requires both high visual accuracy and strong deployment robustness. This problem remains challenging because multi-variant connectors exhibit significant morphological and appearance variations, making stable cross-variant generalization difficult, while the mismatch between training and deployment under fixed-view inspection settings induces background spurious correlation and degrades real-world performance. To address these issues, this paper proposes MCFR, a Mask-Guided Coarse-to-Fine Regression framework for multi-variant BTB connector assembly. By introducing an object-aware mask prior and explicit photometric refinement, the proposed method suppresses background interference and improves alignment accuracy and robustness in practical deployment. Experiments on a self-constructed multi-variant dataset, a BTB batch insertion testbed, and a real smartphone assembly task show that MCFR consistently outperforms representative baselines and achieves an average real-world insertion success rate of 99.25%. These results demonstrate the effectiveness and practical potential of MCFR for automated assembly of multi-variant BTB connectors.

</details>


### [187] [Learning Unknown Constraints without Unsafe Data via Optimality and Counterfactual Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09350)
*Zhouyu Zhang,Chih-Yuan Chiu,Glen Chou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Counterfactual KKT (CF-KKT) 约束学习框架：利用局部学习的可微动力学模型在专家演示上施加 KKT 一阶最优性条件，并通过反事实行为生成合成不可行数据，从而在无需已知动力学、无在线风险探索的条件下恢复未知约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从演示中学习约束存在两种范式：CIOC 依赖已知动力学与结构化约束表示，但数据高效、安全；ICRL 支持未知动力学与复杂约束，但需要大量在线探索且可能违反安全约束。作者希望结合二者优势，在离线演示条件下实现高效、安全且灵活的约束恢复。

Method: 1) 局部学习可微动力学模型，在演示轨迹上直接施加 KKT 启发的局部最优性条件；2) 利用学到的动力学在演示附近生成"提升奖励"的反事实行为，揭示在无未知约束时更优的行为，作为合成不可行样本；3) 当约束参数化已知时，同一框架退化为基于 CIOC 的直接参数恢复，并分析其对动力学建模误差的敏感性。

Result: 在高维机器人控制任务上，方法能够学习神经约束表示，相比最先进的离线 ICRL 基线在安全性与数据效率上均有提升。

Conclusion: CF-KKT 将 CIOC 的数据效率与安全性同 ICRL 对未知动力学和复杂约束的灵活性相结合，为离线约束学习提供了不依赖在线探索、不需已知动力学的可行路径。

Abstract: Learning from demonstrations (LfD) provides a framework for inferring unknown constraints from locally optimal, constraint-satisfying expert behavior. Existing approaches largely fall into two paradigms, constrained inverse optimal control (CIOC) and inverse constrained reinforcement learning (ICRL). CIOC exploits optimality conditions such as the Karush--Kuhn--Tucker (KKT) conditions but typically assumes known dynamics and structured constraint representations. Meanwhile, ICRL accommodates complex unknown constraints and unknown transition dynamics but often requires extensive online exploration, during which unsafe constraint violations may occur. In this work, we introduce Counterfactual KKT (CF-KKT), a constraint learning framework that leverages learned dynamics and locally optimal demonstrations to recover unknown constraints without requiring known dynamics or additional risky exploration, thereby combining the data efficiency and safety advantages of CIOC with the flexibility of ICRL. First, we use a locally learned differentiable dynamics model to impose KKT-inspired optimality conditions directly on the demonstrations. Second, we use the learned dynamics to generate reward-improving counterfactual behaviors near the demonstrations, revealing behaviors that would be preferable in the absence of the unknown constraint and thus providing synthetic infeasible data. When the constraint parameterization is known, the same learned-dynamics framework enables direct CIOC-based parameter recovery, and we characterize its sensitivity to dynamics misspecification. Across high-dimensional robotic control tasks, our approach learns neural constraint representations with improved safety and data efficiency relative to state-of-the-art offline ICRL baselines.

</details>


### [188] [COOL: Curiosity-Driven Object Ownership Learning for Personalized Robotic Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09358)
*Samira Huber,Ruben Hammele,Sören Pirk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: COOL：一种机器人框架，通过日常观察自主学习物体所有权关系，并用智能体驱动的好奇心引导数据采集来维持长期空间记忆，支持基于所有权的导航与任务执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化服务机器人需理解"我的背包在哪"这类含所有权语义的自然语言指令，但所有权很少被显式标注，只能从长期人-物交互行为中推断，现有系统缺乏这一能力与长期记忆维护机制。

Method: 提出COOL框架：从日常观察中自主推断物体所有权关系，并构建环境的长期空间记忆；采用基于智能体、由好奇心驱动的数据采集策略，引导机器人前往信息量最高的位置以获取新信息并刷新过时观测。

Result: 离线实验、消融研究与真实世界评估表明，COOL能从真实交互中推断所有权关系，并利用该知识完成以所有权为条件的导航与任务执行。

Conclusion: COOL证明机器人可从非显式标注的长期行为证据中学习物体所有权，并通过主动刷新记忆保持环境表征的时效性，为个性化服务机器人提供可行路径。

Abstract: Robots are increasingly expected to provide personalized services in everyday environments. To do so, they must ground natural-language commands such as "Where is my backpack?" or "Find my bottle" and execute them by reasoning about object instances, people, locations, and ownership. This is challenging because ownership is rarely labeled explicitly and must be inferred from long-term, behavioral evidence of human-object interactions. To address this, we present COOL, a novel robotic framework for autonomously learning object ownership from everyday observations and maintaining a long-term spatial memory of its environment. To keep its memory current, COOL uses an agent-based curiosity-driven data collection strategy that guides the robot toward the most promising locations to gain information and refresh stale observations. Offline experiments, ablation studies, and real-world evaluations show that COOL can infer ownership relations from real-world interactions and use this knowledge for ownership-conditioned navigation and task execution.

</details>


### [189] [Immiscible Diffusion Policy: Preserving Multimodal Robot Actions through Label-Free Noise Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09369)
*Xiao Zhang,Yuxin Chen,Zhixuan Liang,Guojian Zhan,Chenran Li,Chenfeng Xu,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Yiheng Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 扩散策略常因独立动作-噪声配对导致路径混叠而坍缩为单模态；作者提出免标签、仅训练期的"Immiscible Diffusion Policy"，通过动作-噪声分配保持较独立的噪声-动作路径，在5个仿真和2个真实人形操作任务上显著提升多模态保持能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散策略被期望恢复多模态动作分布，但即使数据集模态平衡且批内严格对称，仍会坍缩为单一模态。作者分析认为独立动作-噪声配对增加了扩散路径的mixing与crossing，产生平均化的去噪响应，抑制模态特有行为；该问题在低维稠密动作空间的机器人规划中尤为严重。

Method: 提出Immiscible Diffusion Policy：一种无需标签、仅作用于训练阶段的插件。核心机制是action-noise assignment（动作-噪声分配），使噪声到动作的路径尽可能保持相对分离（避免不可混溶），从而减少路径交叉。不修改策略网络架构，也不改变推理流程。

Result: 在5个仿真与2个真实世界人形操作任务（涵盖state、RGB、point-cloud观测）上验证：在三个双模态任务中将非主导模态占比提升6.0x–14.6x；在两个四模态任务中恢复了vanilla策略rollout中完全缺失的演示模态，同时保持强任务性能。

Conclusion: 扩散策略的多模态坍缩源于独立动作-噪声配对导致的路径混叠；Immiscible Diffusion Policy以极其简单的训练期修正有效保持动作多模态性，并在仿真与真实人形操作中均表现稳健，为通用机器人学习中的多模态保持提供了轻量可靠方案。

Abstract: When diffusion policies were first introduced, they were expected to recover multi-modal action distributions. However, we find this expectation does not always hold, as diffusion policies often collapse to a single modality even when we guarantee the balance of dataset modalities and exact within-batch symmetry. Our analysis indicates that independent action-noise pairing contributes to this failure by increasing mixing and crossing among diffusion paths, which can produce averaged denoising responses and suppress modality-specific behavior. This issue is especially severe in robot planning, where action spaces are dense and low-dimensional, significantly increasing such mixing and crossing. To alleviate this problem, we propose Immiscible Diffusion Policy, a label-free training-time add-on to diffusion policy that uses action-noise assignment to preserve relatively distinct noise-to-action routes without modifying the policy architecture or inference procedure. Across five simulated and two real-world humanoid manipulation tasks spanning state, RGB, and point-cloud observations, our method significantly improves the policy's preservation of action modalities while maintaining strong task performance. It increases the proportion of the non-dominant modality by 6.0x-14.6x across three two-modality tasks and recovers demonstrated modalities that are entirely absent from vanilla policy rollouts on both four-modality tasks. These results demonstrate that Immiscible Diffusion Policy provides a simple yet robust approach to preserving action multi-modality in general robot learning tasks.

</details>


### [190] [IVG-UAV: An Intelligent Voice-Guided UAV System for Autonomous Ripe Fruit Harvesting with Vision-Based Classification and Adaptive Path Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09398)
*Dinh Trung Duong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一套语音控制无人机采摘系统，集成Whisper语音识别+LLM、视觉成熟度分类与自适应路径规划，并在Gazebo中仿真验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热带地区（尤其大型农场）水果采摘高度依赖人工，导致劳动力成本高、物流调度复杂，需要自动化方案。

Method: 统一框架内整合Whisper语音识别与大语言模型进行指令理解、基于计算机视觉的果实成熟度分类、以及自适应路径规划；全部模块在Gazebo仿真环境中建模与验证。

Result: 摘要仅说明在受控农业仿真场景下完成了系统建模与性能评估，未给出具体量化指标、真机试验或对比结果。

Conclusion: 初步验证了语音驱动无人机采摘框架在仿真中的可行性，但尚缺实机部署与量化评估。

Abstract: In tropical regions, their agricultural sectors remain highly dependent on manual labor for fruit harvesting. On large-scale farms, this dependency often results in significant labor cost and logistic complexities. This project presents the development and simulation of a voice-controlled Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) system designed to automate harvesting tasks in extensive plantations. The proposed system integrates speech recognition using Whisper [1] and LLM, computer vision-based ripeness classification, and adaptive path planning within a unified framework. The entire system is modeled and validated in a Gazebo simulation environment, allowing performance evaluation under controlled agricultural scenarios

</details>


### [191] [TempoBridge: Language-Guided Tempo Control for Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09451)
*Yeonseo Lee,Hyosup Shin,Guebin Hwang,Sungho Jo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TempoBridge：一种轻量框架，利用冻结的VLA表征从指令中的节奏（tempo）线索调制机器人动作速度，无需额外节奏条件演示或基策略微调，在LIBERO上把节奏成功率从52.6%提升至89.7%，并在真机上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型擅长"做什么"，但缺乏对"怎么执行"（如快慢节奏）的控制；已有方法需要节奏条件化的机器人演示或微调基策略，数据成本高。

Method: 基于冻结的VLA表征，从上下文VLM表征中抽取节奏线索，通过因果相位路由器（causal phase router）将节奏线索与任务进度对齐，在任务各阶段执行时调制标称运动指令；不训练节奏专用基策略，也无需节奏条件演示。

Result: LIBERO任务上，典型节奏指令下节奏成功率由52.6%升至89.7%，同时保持高任务成功率；无节奏线索时性能接近基线；对未见节奏表达具备零训练泛化能力；真机实验展示了语言条件化的节奏调制。

Conclusion: TempoBridge证明可在不牺牲任务成功率的前提下，为VLA赋予细粒度、分阶段的速度控制，且低成本、可泛化到未见节奏表达与真实操作场景。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are effective at understanding what task to perform, but provide limited control over how it should be executed, such as moving quickly or slowly. We introduce TempoBridge, a lightweight framework that uses frozen VLA representations to modulate actions according to tempo cues in the instruction at each task phase, without additional tempo-conditioned robot demonstrations or tempo-specific base-policy fine-tuning. TempoBridge extracts tempo cues from contextual VLM representations, aligns them with task progress through a causal phase router, and modulates nominal motion commands during execution. Across LIBERO tasks, TempoBridge improves Tempo Success Rate from 52.6% to 89.7% under canonical tempo instructions while retaining high task success. It also preserves near-baseline performance when no tempo cue is present and generalizes to unseen tempo expressions without additional training. Experiments on a physical robot further demonstrate language-conditioned tempo modulation in real-world manipulation.

</details>


### [192] [RobotAPO: Adversarial Physics Preference Optimization for Robotic Manipulation Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09454)
*Kerui Li,Zhe Jing,Chenyi Huang,Xiaofeng Wang,Zheng Zhu,Haoming Cui,Huaibo Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AgiBot-PhysPref物理偏好数据集与RobotAPO对抗式物理偏好优化框架，在流匹配去噪空间中纠正生成视频的局部物理违规，显著提升真实机器人回放任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作视频常被用作具身智能体的视觉规划，但仅优化视觉合理性无法捕捉真实交互中脆弱的物理流形。交互边界处哪怕微小的物理违规（如穿模、物体过早移动）都会使下游执行所需的时序与位姿推断完全失效。标准监督微调缺乏惩罚这类局部失效的直接压力，因此需要引入物理一致性偏好信号。

Method: 1) 构建AgiBot-PhysPref：严格筛选的10,000样本偏好数据集，通过条件匹配隔离物理违规，将生成器自身失败分布转化为物理一致性信号；2) 提出RobotAPO：在连续流匹配去噪空间中运行的对抗式物理偏好优化框架；3) 采用轻量级对抗反事实提议器，在去噪空间中学习条件依赖、偏向物理失败的方向，避免仅记忆静态策展失败样本；4) 保持纯提示+参考的推理接口，无需外部结构条件。

Result: 在留出的AgiBot条件下，RobotAPO在物理一致性上超过最强受控内部基线6.8%硬分数和10.0%软分数；在真实机器人回放中，将物理一致性增益转化为任务成功率相对提升37.4%。

Conclusion: 显式纠正生成视频中的局部物理违规可有效改善下游机器人执行；将失败分布作为偏好优化信号、并在去噪空间做对抗式探索，是一种可行且无需外部结构条件的物理一致性提升路径。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation videos are increasingly used as visual plans for embodied agents, but optimizing purely for visual plausibility often fails to capture the fragile physical manifold of real-world interactions. Even minor physics-violating errors at the interaction boundary, such as interpenetration or premature object motion, can completely invalidate the inferred timing and pose needed for downstream execution. Because standard supervised fine-tuning lacks the direct pressure to penalize these localized failures, we introduce AgiBot-PhysPref. This rigorously curated 10,000-sample preference dataset isolates condition-matched physics violations, turning the generator's own failure distribution into a foundational signal for physical consistency. Building upon this, we propose RobotAPO, an adversarial physics preference optimization framework operating in the continuous flow-matching denoising space. To prevent the policy from merely memorizing static curated failures, RobotAPO employs a lightweight adversarial counterfactual proposer that learns a condition-dependent, physical-failure-biased direction in denoising space. This encourages the model to explore and better respect the physical interaction boundary, all while maintaining a pure prompt-and-reference inference interface without requiring external structural conditioning. Comprehensive evaluations demonstrate that explicitly correcting these localized physics violations improves downstream robot execution from generated videos. On held-out AgiBot conditions, RobotAPO outperforms the strongest controlled internal baseline in physical consistency by 6.8% hard score and 10.0% soft score. Crucially, in real-robot replay, it translates these physical-consistency gains into a 37.4% relative improvement in task success over the strongest controlled internal baseline.

</details>


### [193] [TMT: Runtime Backdoor Detection for Vision-Language-Action Policies on Unseen Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09462)
*Zirun Zhou,Jingfeng Zhang,HaoChuan Xu,Xizhe Zhang,Elliott Wen,Jing Sun,Hong Jia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TMT：基于Token流形与潜在层间转移建模的VLA策略运行时后门检测器，并探索自蒸馏策略净化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略被植入后门后，仍能保持正常任务性能，仅在触发条件下产生恶意动作；由于恶意动作本身可能逐个看似合理，且未见任务会自然带来观测与行为变化，使得运行时激活检测十分困难。

Method: TMT为双分支运行时检测器，仅在良性rollout上训练：一支建模输入token的流形结构，另一支在相邻层潜在动力学中评估预测误差。token流形分支发现可疑rollout后，由潜在偏差确认，并指导后续监控的转移选择。净化方面采用自蒸馏：用冻结的后门策略在良性输入上产生动作，监督学生在成对的良性/触发观测上学习，无需独立干净参考策略。评估上将传统后门检测器适配为VLA检测器，并把异常检测与失败检测方法改造复用为基线。

Result: 在与十种基线的后验对比中，TMT在三种VLA后门攻击下的未见任务上取得SOTA的后门检测性能。

Conclusion: 面向VLA的运行时后门检测与自蒸馏净化是可行且有效的，token流形与层间潜在转移的联合建模有助于在未见任务上泛化。

Abstract: Backdoored vision-language-action (VLA) policies can preserve benign task performance while producing malicious actions when a trigger appears. Detecting such activation is difficult because malicious behavior can comprise individually plausible actions, while unfamiliar tasks introduce legitimate changes in observations and behavior. We introduce TMT, a runtime backdoor detector based on Token Manifold and latent Transition modeling. Trained on benign rollouts, its two branches assess input-token structure and prediction errors in adjacent-layer latent dynamics. A suspicious rollout identified by the token manifold branch, once confirmed through latent deviations, guides transition selection for subsequent monitoring. We further explore policy purification through self-distillation: a frozen copy of the backdoored policy provides benign-input actions to supervise a student on paired benign and triggered observations, without requiring a separate clean reference policy. For evaluation, we adapt traditional backdoor detectors and repurpose anomaly and failure detection methods as VLA backdoor detectors. In a post-hoc comparison with ten baselines, TMT achieves state-of-the-art backdoor detection performance on unseen tasks across three VLA backdoor attacks. Our project page is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [194] [Precise SE(3) End-Effector Tracking in Whole-Body Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09479)
*Joohwan Seo,Xiaofeng Guo,Jinkun Cao,Roberto Horowitz,Rocky Duan,Guanya Shi,Koushil Sreenath*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ResGAC 将几何导纳控制(GAC)与残差强化学习结合，用于人形机器人全身运动中的末端位姿精确跟踪，并在 Unitree G1 真机上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人全身运动时，浮动基座振荡、重力、动态耦合与行走扰动使末端执行器精确跟踪困难。作者希望保留结构化任务空间反馈的稳定性，同时让学习策略补偿未建模动力学并协调行走与平衡。

Method: 提出 ResGAC：用几何导纳控制 GAC 在 SE(3) 任务空间提供结构化反馈并生成名义手臂关节位置目标；残差 RL 在共享关节位置动作空间中补偿未建模动力学并协调行走与平衡。利用左不变几何形式，使同一 GAC 律可跨操作参考系使用，并可采用地面固连航向坐标系，在保留平面行走的同时去除骨盆滚转、俯仰与升降对操作参考的干扰。

Result: 在真实 Unitree G1 人形机器人上验证。四个站立末端跟踪基准上均优于 SONIC 等基线，平移与旋转误差更低；地面固连航向坐标系减少了骨盆运动向期望末端位姿的传播。站立插孔任务成功率 90%，SONIC 为 50%；在下肢运动期间实现准确的世界坐标系 SE(3) 末端位姿跟踪。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将结构化几何导纳控制与残差强化学习结合，可在真机上实现人形机器人全身运动中的高精度末端位姿跟踪，且地面固连航向参考系能有效抑制基座振荡耦合，具有良好的实用价值。

Abstract: Precise end-effector tracking during humanoid whole-body motion is challenging due to floating-base oscillations, gravity, dynamic coupling, and locomotion-induced disturbances. We propose ResGAC, a whole-body humanoid controller for precise end-effector pose tracking that combines geometric admittance control (GAC) with residual reinforcement learning. GAC provides structured $\SE$ task-space feedback and generates nominal arm joint-position targets, while residual RL compensates for unmodeled dynamics and coordinates locomotion and balance in the shared joint-position action space. The left-invariant geometric formulation allows the same GAC law to be used across manipulation reference frames. This enables the use of a ground-attached heading frame that preserves planar locomotion while removing pelvis roll, pitch, and heave from the manipulation reference, thereby reducing reference-induced end-effector motion during locomotion. ResGAC is validated on a real Unitree G1 humanoid. Across four standing end-effector tracking benchmarks, ResGAC consistently outperforms representative baselines, including SONIC, achieving lower translational and rotational errors. Real-world experiments further demonstrate reduced propagation of pelvis motion to the desired end-effector pose using the proposed ground-attached heading frame. ResGAC achieves $90\%$ success in a standing peg-in-hole task compared with $50\%$ for SONIC, and accurate world-frame $\SE$ end-effector pose tracking during lower-body motion. Experimental videos are included in the supplementary material and are also available on the project website: this https URL .

</details>


### [195] [SiGNgapore - An Interactive Dataset for Sign-based Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09488)
*Nicky Zimmerman,Joel Loo,Zishuo Wang,David Hsu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 介绍一个面向无地图导航的指示牌中心数据集，采集自新加坡多个公共场所，包含多模态传感数据与任务标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人在人类环境中运行需摆脱预建地图依赖；人类设计的导航标识是低成本、语义丰富的导航线索，但缺乏针对标识驱动决策的公开多模态数据集。\n\n

Method: 用手持设备在新加坡公共场所采集数据，场景围绕导航标识及其复杂周边环境；记录RGB、稀疏深度、里程计与IMU；提供56个长时程导航任务、450余个标识中心场景，采用人类可读格式并附ROS 2转换脚本，另附场地地图与GPS对齐的场景图。

Result: 构建了一个多模态、多场景的标识中心无地图导航数据集，含长时程任务与场景图标注，并讨论了潜在使用场景。

Conclusion: 该数据集为标识驱动、无地图的机器人导航研究提供数据与工具基础，但论文以资源发布为主，未给出导航算法或实机验证结果。

Abstract: The future of autonomous robots in human-oriented environments depends on their ability to navigate in the absence of prebuilt maps; exploiting navigational aids, such as navigational signs, designed by humans for humans is critical for enabling autonomy. This manuscript introduces a unique dataset collected using a handheld device, at various public spaces across Singapore, representing environments that humans frequent daily, and focusing on sign-centric decision making for mapless navigation. The dataset includes RGB, sparse depth, odometry and IMU measurements of scenarios centered around navigational signs and the complex environments where they are placed. Additionally, we provide 56 long-horizon navigation missions and over 450 sign-centric scenarios. All data is provided in a human- readable format, as well as utility scripts for conversion to ROS 2. Lastly, we provide venue maps and GPS aligned scene graphs of the test environments. We discuss the potential use cases for this dataset.

</details>


### [196] [Sparse Feature Policy Unlearning Mitigates State Hallucination in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09496)
*Jiho Lee,Jeongeun Park,Heayoun Choi,Taekyung Kim,Eunwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出用稀疏自编码器定位VLA"状态幻觉"相关稀疏特征，并据此做选择性遗忘（SOUL）来降低幻觉失败、提升操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型泛化性强，但实际部署中反复出现"状态幻觉"——机器人把未达成的机器人-物体状态当作已达成并继续执行。需要可解释地定位并修正这类不良行为知识。

Method: 先做注意力分析与稀疏自编码器的机制性解释，发现状态幻觉伴随对任务相关视觉区域注意力减弱，且幻觉对应稀疏特征被激活；据此提出SOUL：以幻觉失败中识别出的稀疏特征作为遗忘目标、成功行为特征作为保持目标，对策略做选择性unlearning。

Result: 在多种VLA架构的仿真与真实环境中，方法显著减少幻觉失败并提升任务成功率，同时未明显损害已有操作能力。

Conclusion: 可解释的特征分析为选择性修改机器人策略中的不良知识提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong generalization in robotic manipulation by leveraging rich representations from pretrained vision-language models. However, their deployment in real-world environments remains limited by recurring unreliable behaviors. In this work, we study state hallucination, a recurring failure pattern in which a VLA continues acting as if an unrealized robot-object state had been achieved. Our analyses find that state hallucination coincides with weakened attention to task-relevant visual regions, and a mechanistic interpretation via sparse autoencoders reveals that hallucination-associated sparse features are activated when these failures occur. Based on this analysis, we propose SOUL (Sparse feature pOlicy UnLearning), which selectively unlearns policy knowledge associated with state hallucination behaviors, where sparse features identified from hallucination failures and successful behaviors serve as explicit forgetting and retention targets, respectively. Experiments across VLA architectures in simulated and real-world environments show that our method substantially reduces hallucinated failures and improves task success without substantially compromising the existing manipulation capabilities. These results suggest that interpretable feature analysis provides a practical basis for selectively modifying undesirable knowledge in robot policies.

</details>


### [197] [Not All Uncertainty Matters: Simulation-in-the-Loop Fast-Slow Reasoning for Decision-Critical Autonomous Driving System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09520)
*Jiayi Chen,Shuai Wang,Guangxu Zhu,Derrick Wing Kwan Ng,Chengzhong Xu,Kaibin Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SIGMA——仿真在环的任务导向快慢协同框架，将规划器嵌入不确定性评估，用"期望规划增益(EPG)"作为云端调用判据，在CARLA上减少50%冗余云交互，导航成功率提升>6%，动态场景完成时间最多缩短26.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大视觉语言模型(VLM)具备开放世界感知与推理能力，但算力开销大、推理延迟高，无法在车端持续运行，因此需要"快慢协同"：车端高效模块负责实时感知与控制，云端模型只在必要时提供高层推理。核心难题在于何时让云端推理介入时间敏感的驾驶决策。现有方法依赖感知不确定性、启发式触发或资源驱动策略，均未评估"解决该不确定性是否真能改善规划"——即缺少任务导向的判断依据。

Method: SIGMA为仿真在环(simulation-in-the-loop)框架：把规划器直接嵌入不确定性评估环节，在语义与几何不确定性下采样多种合理的场景实现(plausible scene realizations)，观察其对可行轨迹与规划代价的影响，据此估计"消除不确定性所能带来的规划代价期望下降量"。进一步提出期望规划增益(EPG)这一决策级指标，统一用于云端调用触发、云引导信息整合以及截止时间与资源约束下的请求优先级排序。

Result: CARLA实验显示，在静态与动态障碍场景中，SIGMA在提升规划质量、效率与导航成功率的同时减少了不必要的云交互。与固定周期协同相比，冗余云交互减少50%，导航成功率提升6%以上，动态场景完成时间最多缩短26.2%。

Conclusion: SIGMA表明，以"规划收益"而非"不确定性大小"作为云端VLM调用判据，可在有限通信与算力预算下实现更高效的快慢协同；EPG提供了可复用的决策级接口，为自动驾驶中VLM的按需调用与引导整合提供了范式。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (VLMs) provide powerful open-world perception and reasoning for autonomous driving, but their high computational cost and inference latency make continuous cloud-side use impractical. This motivates fast--slow collaboration, where efficient onboard modules handle real-time perception and control while cloud models provide high-level reasoning only when needed. The key challenge is deciding when cloud reasoning should influence time-critical driving decisions. Existing methods often rely on perception uncertainty, heuristic triggers, or resource-driven policies, without assessing whether resolving an uncertainty will improve planning. We propose \textbf{SIGMA}, a simulation-in-the-loop framework for task-oriented fast--slow collaboration. SIGMA embeds the planner into uncertainty assessment and evaluates how plausible scene realizations under semantic and geometric uncertainty affect feasible trajectories and planning cost. Based on these outcomes, it estimates the expected reduction in planning cost from resolving uncertainty. We further introduce expected planning gain (EPG), a decision-level metric for cloud invocation, cloud-guidance integration, and request prioritization under deadline and resource constraints. Experiments in CARLA show that SIGMA reduces unnecessary cloud interactions while improving planning, efficiency, and navigation success in static and dynamic obstacle scenarios. Compared with fixed-period collaboration, SIGMA reduces unnecessary cloud interactions by 50\%, improves navigation success by more than 6\%, and cuts finish time by up to 26.2\% in dynamic scenarios.

</details>


### [198] [Contact-Aware Imitation Learning Through Contact Factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09533)
*Jiho Hong,Daeun Song,Sanghyun Kim,Mingyo Seo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FACE 将力观测分解为任务行为与接触因素（接触法向、摩擦尺度），在归一化接触相对坐标下编码/解码策略输入输出，无需更新策略参数即可适应未知表面属性与几何变化，并在真机接触丰富操作上验证泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富操作中，相互作用力对表面几何、姿态和摩擦的微小变化极其敏感，直接用原始力信号训练的策略难以迁移到演示条件之外。因此需要一种能把"意图任务行为"与"环境相关接触因素"解耦的表征，实现执行时自适应而不需重训策略。

Method: 提出接触因子化模仿学习框架 FACE：(1) 把交互力表示为归一化、接触相对坐标系下的量；(2) 学习一个接触法向估计器推断局部接触法向；(3) 在线摩擦估计器推断有效摩擦尺度；(4) 两者共同用于编码力观测、解码策略输出为实际运动与力指令，在执行时适应接触条件而保持任务意图，且不更新策略参数。实验为真机接触丰富操作，并在未见过的表面属性与几何变化下，与适配到该设定下的既有方法变体做受控对比。

Result: 在真实机器人接触丰富操作任务上，面对未见的表面属性和几何变化，FACE 相比适配后的先前方法变体表现出更稳健的跨接触条件泛化能力（摘要未给出具体量化指标，细节见项目主页视频与材料）。

Conclusion: 通过将力表征分解为任务意图与接触因素，并借助接触法向/摩擦在线估计，可在不更新策略参数的前提下把模仿学习策略泛化到变化的接触条件；该思路为接触丰富操作的泛化提供了一条可执行的分解式路径，作者亦提供视频与附加材料。

Abstract: Generalizable contact-rich manipulation requires robots to preserve intended task behavior while adapting its physical realization to changing contact conditions. However, interaction forces can vary substantially with small changes in surface geometry, orientation, and friction, making policies trained directly on raw force measurements difficult to transfer beyond demonstrated conditions. We introduce FACE, a contact-factorized imitation learning framework that separates intended task behavior from environment-dependent contact factors. Our representation expresses interaction forces in normalized, contact-relative coordinates, while a learned contact-normal estimator and an online friction estimator infer the local contact normal and effective friction scale. Together, these estimators enable force observations to be encoded and policy outputs to be decoded into physical motion and force commands during execution. In this way, FACE adapts execution to current contact conditions while preserving the intended task behavior, without updating the policy parameters. We evaluate FACE on real-robot contact-rich manipulation under unseen variations in surface properties and geometry, demonstrating robust generalization across contact conditions through controlled comparisons with variants that adapt prior approaches to our setting. Videos and additional materials can be found on the project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [199] [MagCilia: A Compact Magnetociliary Tactile Sensor with 3D Force Sensing for Robotic Contact Perception and Grasping Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09536)
*Yu Feng,Hao Wu,Haotian Guo,Haoming Liu,William Su,Jingxiang Guo,Jiankun Li,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Wen Jung Li,Jianshu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出紧凑型磁纤毛触觉传感器MagCilia（磁纤毛+霍尔传感器）实现3D力感知，并用因果历史融合回归（CHFR）从耦合磁场通道重建三维力，完成抓取反馈与六类表面识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人抓取与表面探索需要同时测量法向/切向力并获取接触表面信息，而现有触觉单元往往结构复杂、体积大，难以兼顾三维力测量与表面感知。因此需要一种紧凑、单一单元的触觉传感器，并解决多轴磁场通道耦合下的力重建问题。

Method: 1) 硬件：柔性磁纤毛结构结合霍尔传感器构成紧凑触觉单元；2) 建模：准静态有限元分析研究多向加载下的结构变形与磁场响应；3) 算法：提出因果历史融合回归（CHFR），将当前磁场测量与近期变化量结合以解耦并重建力；4) 评估：按标定记录分组的五折交叉验证、按采集会话分组的三折交叉验证、机器人抓取与在线表面识别实验。

Result: Fx/Fy/Fz的RMSE分别为0.40/0.57/0.69 N，R²为0.93/0.90/0.92；机器人实验实现切向力引导的夹爪调整与外部扰动下的多轴载荷监测；重建力的频域特征在按采集会话分组的三折交叉验证中以99.39%准确率区分六类表面，在线演示可识别全部六种表面。

Conclusion: 单一紧凑触觉单元即可同时实现3D力重建、抓取反馈与表面识别，验证了磁纤毛式触觉设计结合历史融合回归的有效性，为机器人触觉感知提供了可行的集成方案。

Abstract: Robotic grasping and surface exploration benefit from simultaneous measurement of normal and tangential forces and from surface information obtained through contact. Here, we present a compact magnetociliary tactile sensor (MagCilia) that combines a flexible magnetic-cilia structure with a Hall sensor for 3D force sensing. Quasi-static finite element analysis is used to investigate structural deformation and magnetic responses under multidirectional loading. To reconstruct forces from the coupled magnetic channels, we propose causal history fusion regression (CHFR), which combines current magnetic-field measurements with their recent changes. Five-fold cross-validation grouped by calibration record yields root-mean-square errors of 0.40, 0.57, and 0.69 N for Fx, Fy, and Fz, respectively, with corresponding coefficients of determination of 0.93, 0.90, and 0.92. Robotic experiments demonstrate tangential-force-guided gripper adjustment and multi-axis load monitoring under external perturbations. Frequency-domain features of the reconstructed forces distinguish six surface categories with 99.39% accuracy in three-fold cross-validation grouped by acquisition session. An online robotic demonstration additionally identifies all six tested surfaces. These results demonstrate 3D force reconstruction, grasping feedback, and surface recognition using a single compact tactile unit.

</details>


### [200] [Targeted Modality Dropout for Real-Robot Manipulation Robust to Intermittent Vision Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09566)
*Genki Shikada,Kazuki Osamura,Masaru Ide,Tetsuya Ogata,Kanata Suzuki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出目标模态丢弃(TMD)方法，利用注意力估计各模态依赖度并选择性地丢弃主导模态，结合熵正则化，缓解模仿学习策略对视觉等单一模态的过度依赖，实现视觉缺失下的鲁棒任务执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态模仿学习策略在训练中易过度依赖某一主导模态（如视觉），当该模态在推理时缺失会导致策略执行失败，需要一种机制平衡模态依赖。

Method: TMD：用注意力估计每个模态的依赖程度，选择性地丢弃最主导模态；并在依赖分布上施加熵正则化以平衡各模态的利用率。与随机选择丢弃模态且无熵正则的传统dropout作对比。

Result: 真实双臂机器人实验：视觉缺失时基线策略成功率大幅下降，而TMD仍能维持任务执行；传统随机dropout无熵正则的方法即便无视觉缺失也在许多任务上失败。

Conclusion: 针对性地丢弃主导模态并结合熵正则化，可有效提升多模态模仿学习策略在模态缺失下的鲁棒性，优于传统随机dropout。

Abstract: Imitation learning policies that integrate multiple sensory modalities are prone to overreliance on a dominant modality, such as vision, during training, which can disrupt policy execution when that modality is lost at inference time. In this paper, we introduce Targeted Modality Dropout (TMD), in which the dependence on each modality is estimated using attention and the most dominant modality is selectively dropped. This is combined with entropy regularization over the dependence distribution. Through real-robot evaluation using a bimanual manipulator, we show that under vision loss the success rate of the baseline policy drops substantially, whereas TMD sustains task execution. In contrast, a conventional dropout that selects the dropped modality at random, without the entropy regularization, fails on many tasks even without vision loss.

</details>


### [201] [Point It, Strike It: Direction-Conditioned Dynamic Manipulation of Deformable Linear Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09573)
*Yi Yang,Xiang Fei,Lehong Wang,Zilin Dai,Ruogu Li,Jiting Cai,Liyao Chang,Xinyi Yang,Henry Kou,Ruijie Fu,Lu Li,Howie Choset*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DeformX2.0（GPU加速可变形线性物体仿真器）+ TRACE数据生成方法 + 条件流匹配策略 + RECAP残差标定，实现单摆绳索打击任务中位置与到达方向的双重目标，实机成功率显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可变形线性物体（DLO）的动态操作研究多将目标仅设定为绳尖到达位置，但许多任务实际依赖绳尖"如何到达"（即到达方向）。本文研究单次摆动绳索打击任务，要求同时满足绳尖3D位置与到达方向，且需跨工作空间、跨绳索泛化。难点在于：绳索动力学难以建模、缺乏示教数据、同一目标可由不同摆动以不同可靠性达到、sim-to-real差距不仅来自绳索本身还包括机械臂与台架。

Method: 1) 扩展SOTA DLO仿真器DeformX，加入GPU加速、稳定的Cosserat杆求解器与横流气动模型，得到DeformX2.0（提速超20000倍）；2) 提出TRACE（Trace-rooted Adaptive Cross-Entropy），以存储的"绳尖路径最接近新目标"的既有摆动作为热启动生成打击数据，代价函数惩罚绳索弯曲与绳尖突变运动以偏好可重复的摆动；3) 在生成数据上训练条件流匹配（flow-matching）策略；4) 提出RECAP（Residual Calibration Policy），用少量标定摆动拟合仿真器的绳索与台架参数，并用仿真中训练的修正策略在线适应动作。

Result: 仿真中条件流匹配策略达到92.1%准确率。实机实验中，在三条绳索上，RECAP将位置目标5cm内成功率从72%提升至87%；对同时指定到达方向的目标，在10cm与10°容差下成功率从50%提升至79%。

Conclusion: 通过高保真高速DLO仿真、轨迹引导的自适应交叉熵数据生成、流匹配策略学习与残差标定，可有效解决绳索打击的任务目标（位置+方向）并缩小sim-to-real差距，实机验证了方法的泛化性与有效性。

Abstract: Goal-conditioned dynamic manipulation of deformable linear objects has mainly specified goals as positions for a rope tip to reach. Many tasks, however, depend on how the tip arrives. We therefore study single-swing rope striking with goals that specify the tip's 3D position and arrival direction, across the workspace and on different ropes. This is challenging because rope dynamics are hard to model, no demonstrations exist, distinct swings reach the same goal with different reliability, and the sim-to-real gap extends beyond the rope. To address these challenges, we extend the state-of-the-art DLO simulator DeformX with GPU acceleration, a stable Cosserat rod solver, and a cross-flow aerodynamic model, yielding DeformX2.0, which is more than $20{,}000\times$ faster. We then propose TRACE (Trace-rooted Adaptive Cross-Entropy), which generates striking data by warm-starting each new target from the stored swing whose tip path passes closest to it. Its cost penalizes rope bending and abrupt tip motion to favor repeatable swings. A conditional flow-matching policy trained on this data reaches 92.1% accuracy in simulation. Finally, we propose RECAP (Residual Calibration Policy), which fits the simulator's rope and rig parameters to a few calibration swings and adapts actions with a correction policy trained in simulation. On a real robot, across three ropes, RECAP raises success within 5cm from 72% to 87% for position goals, and within 10cm and 10° from 50% to 79% for goals that also specify the arrival direction.

</details>


### [202] [Adaptive Code Generation for Controlling Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09588)
*Justus Flerlage,Thorsten Wittkopp,Alexander Acker,Odej Kao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个双AI架构的机器人控制框架，用LLM把自然语言意图转译为受限机器人库的可执行代码，用VLM做语义接地，并加入环境触发的重规划与运行时监控闭环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未知动态环境中，机器人需从刚性指令库转向意图级自主；自然语言是表达超出有限指令集复杂目标的唯一介质，但LLM接入面临形式化鸿沟、分类学鸿沟与时间状态/进度感知三大挑战。

Method: 架构框架：LLM将高层意图翻译为受形式化机器人库约束、语法可验证的程序代码；VLM通过蒸馏提供语义接地；基于几何与语义阈值的环境驱动重规划触发；持续运行时监控与自适应规划循环。

Result: 在多个前沿模型上基准测试，表明将生成式AI嵌入反应式受限闭环可在动态未知环境中稳健完成复杂意图（摘要未给出量化指标与实机部署细节）。

Conclusion: 以受限、可验证、反应式闭环约束生成式AI，是弥合意图到执行差距的可行架构路径。

Abstract: Deploying robots as Complex Adaptive Systems (CAS) in unknown and dynamic environments necessitates a transition from rigid command libraries toward intention-based autonomy, as natural language represents the only medium capable of articulating complex goals beyond the capacity of finite instruction sets. While Large Language Models (LLMs) offer a path toward natural language goal description, their integration introduces significant challenges: the formalization gap between imprecise intentions and executable actions, the taxonomy gap induced by unpredictable environments, and the challenge of maintaining temporal state and progress awareness. This work introduces an architectural framework that enables robotic control by leveraging generative AI. The system follows a dual-AI design: an LLM translates high-level intentions into executable program code restricted to a formal robotic library and constrained by verifiable syntax, while a Vision-Language Model (VLM) provides semantic grounding via a distillation process. To ensure robustness, the framework incorporates environment-driven replanning triggers based on geometric and semantic thresholds, complemented by continuous runtime monitoring and an adaptive planning loop. Benchmarked across frontier models, our framework architecture demonstrates that grounding generative AI in a reactive, constrained loop enables robust fulfillment of complex intentions in dynamic and unknown environments.

</details>


### [203] [Fast and Robust Teach-and-Repeat Navigation Using MixVPR Visual Place Recognition*](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09631)
*Václav Truhlařík,Tomáš Pivoňka,Libor Přeučil*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于MixVPR视觉位置识别的轻量级teach-and-repeat导航系统，实车验证室内外鲁棒性与精度可比SOTA，且硬件需求低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的teach-and-repeat导航方案计算量大，限制了在长时移动机器人导航中的实际部署。

Method: 以现代视觉位置识别方法MixVPR为核心构建teach-and-repeat系统，降低算力需求，并进行室内外真实环境测试。

Result: 实机测试表明系统可室内外运行，鲁棒性与导航精度与其他SOTA系统相当，同时硬件门槛更低。

Conclusion: 该高效teach-and-repeat方案适用于广泛的机器人平台与实际应用场景。

Abstract: Teach-and-repeat navigation systems employing advanced visual place recognition techniques for localization exhibit key attributes for long-term mobile robot navigation, such as the ability to operate in unstructured and dynamic environments. However, existing solutions based on deep-learning techniques are computationally demanding, limiting their applicability. This work introduces a novel and efficient teach-and-repeat system built on the modern visual place recognition method MixVPR. Real-world testing demonstrated its ability to operate both indoors and outdoors, achieving robustness and navigation precision comparable to other state-of-the-art systems. In addition, its lower hardware requirements make it suitable for a wide range of robotic platforms and practical applications.

</details>


### [204] [Design optimization of tendon-driven robots considering tendon wrapping and shortcut](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09645)
*Yuta Sahara,Kento Kawaharazuka,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出腱驱动机械臂的多目标优化设计方法，用NSGA-II同时最大化关节力矩、最小化臂厚，优化腱路径、滑轮配置与附着点，得到通过"捷径"效应扩大有效力臂的Pareto最优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腱驱动臂设计中，关节力矩与臂厚存在固有矛盾：腱绕行（wrapping）和捷径（shortcut）效应会改变有效力臂，但相关权衡缺乏系统量化。作者希望用多目标优化同时兼顾力矩与紧凑性。

Method: 将力矩最大化与厚度最小化建模为双目标优化问题，采用NSGA-II求解；设计变量包括腱走线路径、滑轮配置和腱附着点；通过Pareto前沿分析捷径效应带来的力臂扩展。

Result: 得到一组Pareto最优构型，显示可通过策略性捷径实现有效力臂扩展，其设计模式超出常规设计直觉，为紧凑高力矩臂提供实用准则。

Conclusion: 多目标优化可有效刻画腱驱动臂的力矩-厚度权衡，Pareto解为紧凑高力矩机械臂设计提供指导；研究以计算优化与设计分析为主，未见实物样机或实验验证。

Abstract: This study proposes a multi-objective optimization method for tendon-driven arm design, addressing the inherent trade-off between joint torque and arm thickness through tendon wrapping and shortcut effects. We formulate the problem to simultaneously maximize torque and minimize thickness, solved using NSGA-II. The algorithm optimizes tendon routing, pulley configurations, and attachment points. Results reveal Pareto-optimal designs with effective moment arm expansion via strategic shortcuts, providing practical guidelines for compact high-torque arms. The optimized configurations demonstrate non-trivial patterns beyond conventional design intuition, offering new insights for engineering efficient robotic systems.

</details>


### [205] [NAViLoss: An Underwater Navigation-Aware Dual-Residual Objective for Physics-Consistent Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09690)
*Arup Kumar Sahoo,Itzik Klein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 NAViLoss——一种导航感知、不确定性自适应的损失函数，联合约束导航状态域的速度残差与 DVL 波束一致性残差，并结合 DeepONet 构成 NAVi-DeepONet 模型用于 AUV 速度估计，在约 10,000 m 实测海试半合成数据上相对基线提升 44%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AUV 依赖 INS+DVL 组合导航，DVL 测速精度直接影响导航可靠性。现有学习方法在退化测量条件下虽有改善，但训练目标多为常规回归损失：对大残差与受损观测高度敏感，且未显式建模传感过程的物理一致性与测量不确定性。

Method: 提出 NAViLoss：在导航状态域惩罚速度估计残差，在 DVL 测量域惩罚波束一致性残差；采用有界形式抑制大残差影响，并引入自适应机制调节波束几何不确定性。将其与 DeepONet 架构集成，形成 NAVi-DeepONet 速度估计模型。

Result: 在多次真实海试采集的约 10,000 m 半合成 AUV 实验数据上评测，速度估计精度较传统方法与学习型基线提升 44%。

Conclusion: 面向导航的、不确定性自适应的损失设计可有效提升学习型水下速度估计的鲁棒性与精度，为 AUV 组合导航提供实用改进路径。

Abstract: Autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) commonly rely on inertial navigation systems (INS) aided by Doppler velocity logs (DVLs) for reliable underwater navigation. Accurate DVL velocity estimation is therefore essential for successful operation. Recent learning-based methods have demonstrated improved DVL velocity estimation, particularly under degraded measurement conditions. However, their training objectives typically rely on conventional regression losses that are highly sensitive to large residuals and corrupted observations. Additionally, they do not explicitly account for the physical consistency and measurement uncertainty associated with the underlying sensing process. To address these limitations, this paper introduces navigation-aware loss (NAViLoss), a robust and uncertainty-aware objective function for learning-based AUV velocity estimation. NAViLoss jointly penalizes the velocity-estimation residual in the navigation-state domain and the beam-consistency residual in the DVL measurement domain. Its bounded formulation limits the influence of large residuals, while an adaptive mechanism regulates the uncertainty in beam geometry. Furthermore, NAViLoss is integrated with a DeepONet architecture to form a novel NAVi-DeepONet model for seamless estimation of an underwater vehicle's velocity. Lastly, our model is evaluated using approximately 10,000m of semi-synthetic AUV experimental data collected during multiple real-world sea trials. Experimental results demonstrate a 44% improvement in velocity-estimation accuracy compared with conventional and learning-based baselines. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of navigation-aware and uncertainty-adaptive loss design for robust learning-based underwater velocity estimation.

</details>


### [206] [Do Better Visual Representations Always Lead to Better End-to-End Autonomous Driving?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09695)
*Zihao Zhang,Haochen Tian,Tianyu Li,Changhui Jing,Jingliang He,Naisheng Ye,Ziyuan Pu,Zhenjie Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ViRA：一种与规划器无关的视觉表征对齐框架，揭示VFM引导表征在端到端自动驾驶中的三大规律，并据此提出ViRA-Diffusion，在NAVSIM v2上取得92.3 EPDMS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉基础模型（VFM）已被广泛用于端到端自动驾驶，但其表征何时真正提升驾驶性能尚不清楚，因此需要一个保持规划器结构与推理成本不变的分析性框架来系统研究该问题。

Method: 提出规划器无关的视觉表征对齐框架ViRA：不改变规划器架构与推理开销，将规划器视觉表征对齐到不同VFM目标；在此框架下比较多种端到端规划器、五个VFM对齐目标，并考察辅助感知监督的影响；基于结论构建ViRA-Diffusion（无辅助感知监督的扩散式规划器）。

Result: 三点发现：(1) VFM引导的视觉表征在多种端到端规划器上稳定提升性能，收益可延伸至零样本闭环评测；(2) VFM目标选择会影响规划性能，对齐到不同VFM甚至可改善已用预训练VFM编码器的规划器；(3) 辅助感知监督降低对VFM目标选择的敏感性，将五个目标的EPDMS差距从2.7分收窄到0.5分。ViRA-Diffusion在NAVSIM v2 navtest上达92.3 EPDMS，优于对比方法至少1.9分。

Conclusion: 将VFM集成到端到端自动驾驶时，应联合考虑对齐目标选择与规划器监督方式；ViRA可作为规划器无关的通用对齐分析工具。

Abstract: Visual foundation models (VFMs) are increasingly integrated into end-to-end autonomous driving for their powerful representations, yet it remains unclear when these representations improve driving performance. To investigate this question, we introduce ViRA, a planner-agnostic visual representation alignment framework that keeps the planner architecture and inference cost unchanged. Our study reveals three findings: (1) VFM-guided visual representations consistently improve driving performance across diverse end-to-end planners, with gains extending to zero-shot closed-loop evaluation. (2) The choice of VFM target matters for planning performance, and alignment to a different VFM can further benefit planners with pre-trained VFM encoders. (3) Auxiliary perception supervision reduces sensitivity to VFM target selection, narrowing the EPDMS spread across five targets from 2.7 to 0.5 points and potentially compensating for less effective VFM targets. Guided by these findings, we develop ViRA-Diffusion, a diffusion-based planner trained without auxiliary perception supervision, which achieves 92.3 EPDMS on NAVSIM v2 navtest, outperforming recent methods in our comparison by at least 1.9 points. The results motivate jointly considering target selection and planner supervision when integrating VFMs into end-to-end autonomous driving. The results and demo are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [207] [RoboPace: Contact-Aware Time-Optimal Retiming for Action-Chunk Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09696)
*Mimo Shirasaka,Takehiko Ohkawa,Takuya Okubo,Nicola Scianca,Tatsuya Matsushima,Kei Ota*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RoboPace，一个无需重训策略的在线重定时层，在保持 VLA 策略几何路径的前提下，依据预测接触与机器人运动学/动力学约束自适应调整执行速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无机器人演示（UMI、人手采集等）正成为操作数据采集主流，但 VLA 策略会继承演示者的时间节奏。人类时序无法直接迁移到机器人：柔性手可容忍快速接触，机器人却可能因执行器与跟踪限制而超调；反之机器人在自由空间可更快。因此需要一种能协调执行速度与接触安全性的统一方案。

Method: RoboPace 作为在线重定时层，保留策略的几何路径、仅调整其时间参数。它基于对接触的预测来调整执行速度，同时联合考虑接触相关的速度上限与机器人自身运动约束，无需策略重训练，可实时运行。

Result: 在双臂机器人三项接触密集型任务上，更快的匀速执行以及仅考虑物理极限的重定时基本失败；RoboPace 相比慢速匀速执行取得更高的总体成功率，且能在约一半时间内完成五条指令中的四条，既保留了慢速执行的可靠性又去除了其时间代价。
