Conclusion: 在保留策略几何路径的同时在线重定时，可在不重训的前提下兼顾接触安全与执行效率，显著提升接触密集型操作的成功率-时间权衡。

Abstract: Robot manipulation data collection has been shifting from teleoperation toward robot-free demonstrations, through interfaces such as the Universal Manipulation Interface (UMI) or directly from human hands. Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies trained on such data inherit the demonstrator's timing. Yet human timing does not directly transfer to robots: compliant hands tolerate fast contact, whereas robots may overshoot due to actuator and tracking limitations; conversely, robots can move faster in free space. This motivates a unified approach that reconciles execution speed with contact safety. We present RoboPace, an online retiming layer that preserves the policy's geometric path while adapting its timing, respecting the target robot's kinematic and dynamic constraints. It adapts execution speed based on predicted contact, jointly accounting for contact-dependent speed limits and the robot's motion constraints. The method requires no policy retraining and operates in real time. Across three contact-rich tasks on a dual-arm robot, faster uniform execution and physical-limit-only retiming largely fail. RoboPace instead achieves higher overall success than slow uniform execution while completing four of five commands in approximately half the time, retaining the reliability of slow execution without its time cost.

</details>


### [208] [YUBI-STAG: Contact and Semantic-Rich Alignment for VLAs via Automated Video-Language Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09718)
*Masatoshi Tateno,Takehiko Ohkawa,Yueh-Hua Wu,Hanlong Li,Tatsuya Matsushima,Yoichi Sato,Kei Ota*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: YUBI-STAG 自动为操作演示生成交互丰富的时空语义标注，并蒸馏为轻量的 YUBI-VLM；用这些标注后训练 VLA 可提升双臂精细语言对齐与指令跟随能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人演示通常只有粗粒度任务描述，缺失"如何执行"的细粒度信息（哪只夹爪动作、接触哪个物体、如何抓取与移动），导致预训练 VLA 难以通过语言被精细地激发能力。

Method: 提出 YUBI-STAG 框架：结合接触物体分割与视觉语言模型，自动标注物体身份、属性与状态、单夹爪动作、双臂协调及空间接地的交互语义；为降低其对局部序列和多阶段 VLM 推理的依赖，蒸馏出 YUBI-VLM，可直接从原始未分割视频、少数推理调用、仅凭腕部视角恢复动作结构与标注。最后用这些标注后训练 VLA 策略。

Result: 在 YUBI-STAG-Bench 上评测时间、语义与空间接地任务：YUBI-VLM 以更少推理调用和更短运行时间保留了大部分标注精度，并可泛化到未见操作。双臂实验显示，用标注后训练的 VLA 策略性能与指令跟随提升，可控制物体身份、动作夹爪、目标位置和空间关系，这些在原始标签中不存在。

Conclusion: 自动化交互语义标注可将预训练 VLA 与细粒度操作语言对齐，轻量化蒸馏版本兼顾效率与泛化，为演示增强与 VLA 后训练提供有效路径。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models acquire broad manipulation capabilities via large-scale pretraining, yet eliciting them through language requires fine-grained alignment between instructions and physical interactions. Existing robot demonstrations typically provide only coarse task descriptions, omitting how actions are executed, including which gripper acts, which object is contacted, and how it is grasped and moved. We introduce YUBI-STAG, a framework for Spatio-Temporal Annotation and Grounding that automatically enriches manipulation demonstrations with interaction-rich semantics to align pretrained VLAs with fine-grained manipulation language. Combining contact-object segmentation with vision-language models, YUBI-STAG annotates object identities, attributes and states, per-gripper actions, bimanual coordination, and spatially grounded interactions. To address YUBI-STAG's reliance on localized sequences and multi-stage VLM inference, we distill it into YUBI-VLM. YUBI-VLM directly recovers action structure and annotations from raw, unsegmented video in few inference calls and operates from wrist views alone. We evaluate both frameworks on YUBI-STAG-Bench across temporal, semantic, and spatial grounding tasks. YUBI-VLM retains much of YUBI-STAG's annotation accuracy with fewer inference calls and shorter runtime while generalizing to unseen manipulations. Finally, post-training VLA policies on these annotations aligns them with fine-grained language and contact-aware structure. Bimanual experiments demonstrate improved performance and instruction following, including control over object identity, acting gripper, target location, and spatial relations absent from original labels.

</details>


### [209] [ΔWAM: Distilling Action Tangent Fields into World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09734)
*Ke Wu,Hanwen Huang,Bo Gu,Kaizhao Zhang,Xiangting Meng,Yupeng Zheng,Zijun Xu,Jieru Zhao,Wenchao Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Action Tangent Fields，通过对动作引起的未来动态做局部一阶泰勒展开来重构造型世界监督，把动作相关的变化方向蒸馏进 WAM 的去噪监督中，在多个机器人基准上提升分布偏移鲁棒性，并实现单步推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: World Action Models 用稠密未来预测增强稀疏动作监督，但可预测的未来大多由外观与场景持续性主导，而非动作相关动态。作者观察到光流、运动中心表示、latent action 等近期 WAM 设计都可用同一视角解释：世界监督中动作相关变化占比越高，监督越高效。因此需要一种能过滤外观冗余、聚焦动作致变方向的世界监督形式。

Method: 将未来动态表示在 Residual-VAE 潜空间中，未来潜变量可由当前潜变量与其残差恢复；用强动作条件世界模型（ACWM）探测动作变化与残差世界变化之间的局部对应关系，得到局部一阶结构（Action Tangent Fields）；再将该一阶结构蒸馏进 WAM，引导其去噪监督朝向与动作更紧密耦合的动力学方向。此外将多步 VideoDiT 去噪蒸馏为单步以加速推理。

Result: 在 LIBERO-Plus、RoboTwin、RoboTwin2.0-Plus 上，对光照、背景、相机、布局等环境扰动的一致性鲁棒性均获提升；在不使用大规模具身预训练的情况下，在若干分布偏移下鲁棒性强于预训练策略；单步蒸馏实现高效推理。

Conclusion: 有效的世界动作模型监督应保持信息丰富，同时把预测能力集中到动作改变未来的那些方向上；通过一阶动作切线场蒸馏可显著提升鲁棒性并降低推理成本。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAM) improve robot policies by augmenting sparse action supervision with dense future prediction. However, much of the predictable future is dominated by appearance and scene persistence rather than action-dependent dynamics. We observe that several recent WAM designs, including optical flow, motion-centric representations, and latent actions, can be understood from a common perspective in which world supervision becomes more efficient as it contains a higher proportion of action-relevant variation. Based on this insight, we introduce Action Tangent Fields, which reformulate world supervision through a local Taylor expansion of how actions induce changes in future dynamics. We represent future dynamics in Residual-VAE space, where the future latent remains recoverable from the current latent and its residual, and use a strong action-conditioned world model (ACWM) to probe the local correspondence between action variations and residual-world variations. This local first-order structure is distilled into the WAM to guide its denoising supervision toward dynamics that are more tightly coupled to action, rather than merely predictable from appearance. Across LIBERO-Plus, RoboTwin, and RoboTwin2.0-Plus, our method consistently improves robustness to lighting, background, camera, layout, and other environmental perturbations. Despite using no large-scale embodied pretraining, it achieves stronger robustness under several distribution shifts than pretrained policies. We further distill multi-step VideoDiT denoising into a single step for efficient inference. Our results suggest that effective WAM supervision should remain information-rich while concentrating its predictive capacity on the directions along which actions change the future.

</details>


### [210] [AeroEval: Staged Program and Execution Validation for AI-Generated Drone Missions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09764)
*Kautuk Astu,Naina Rabha,Yogesh Simmhan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AeroEval：面向AI生成无人机任务的智能体辅助分阶段验证中间件，结合程序分析与LLM智能体，将导航成功率从55%提升至95%，分析任务运行级成功率从34%提升至88%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM可从自然语言生成无人机程序，但语法合法的程序仍可能违背用户意图、环境约束与任务级行为。现有系统依赖提示词护栏或仿真器结果，对失败定位能力有限，且物理系统中正确性取决于代码、移动感知、环境几何、事件驱动分析与物理执行的交互。

Method: AeroEval采用智能体辅助中间件实现分阶段验证：先做确定性程序分析（语法、平台API用法、任务意图），再结合执行轨迹、任务需求与环境上下文评估实际行为；每阶段输出结构化失败信息以支持迭代重生成。评估使用20个导航任务与5类分析任务，覆盖AirSim与Gazebo仿真器。

Result: 导航成功率由55%提升至95%；分阶段消融中，仅Code Validator与Trajectory Validator的平均运行级成功率分别为44%和56%，完整管线达88%；各阶段可检测程序结构、API使用、任务意图、避障、高度、覆盖度与事件驱动转换中的互补性失败。主分析任务中，运行级成功率由一次生成AeroGen的34%提升至88%。

Conclusion: 结果证明，在评估环境下，将程序级验证与执行接地的智能体验证相结合，可有效提升AI生成无人机应用的正确性与可靠性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) can generate drone programs from natural-language mission descriptions, but syntactically valid programs may still violate user intent, environmental constraints, and mission-level behavior. This problem is pronounced in cyber-physical applications, where correctness depends on the interaction among generated code, mobile sensing, environmental geometry, event-driven analytics, and physical execution. Existing drone code-generation systems primarily use prompt guardrails or simulator outcomes and provide limited failure localization. We present AeroEval, an agent-assisted middleware for staged validation of AI-generated drone missions. AeroEval combines deterministic program analysis with context-grounded LLM agents. It first validates program syntax, platform API usage, and mission intent, and then evaluates the realized behavior using execution trajectories, mission requirements, and environmental context. Each stage returns structured failure information for iterative regeneration. In our evaluation using 20 navigation tasks and five analytical mission types over AirSim and Gazebo simulators, AeroEval improves navigation success from 55% to 95%. In a stagewise ablation study, our Code and Trajectory Validators by themselves achieve mean run-level success rates of 44% and 56%, respectively, while the full AeroEval pipeline achieves 88%; the stages detect complementary failures in program structure, API usage, mission intent, obstacle avoidance, altitude, coverage, and event-driven transitions and the guided regeneration corrects for them. Across the main analytics missions, AeroEval increases aggregate run-level success from 34% for one-shot AeroGen to 88% within the regeneration budget. These results demonstrate the benefit of combining program-level and execution-grounded agentic validation for AI-generated drone applications in the evaluated environment.

</details>


### [211] [On-Demand Robotic Assembly via Differentiable Geometric Part Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09777)
*Millicent Schlafly,Fabio Schaub,Diogo Costa Pais,Luca Lelli,Janne Dvorak,Claire Colmont,Sven Marti,Mark D. Fuge*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出端到端自主流水线：生成式AI代理将用户文本提示转为3D几何，再通过基于图注意力网络代理模型的梯度修复阶段调整零件几何，使设计可被机器人装配，无需数月人工调参。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从数字设计到机器人装配目前需数月专家手工调参，以调和零件几何与机器人约束，阻碍定制化、小批量木制品的按需快速制造。

Method: 生成式智能体由用户提示生成初始3D几何，兼顾视觉保真与部分物理约束；随后引入基于梯度的修复阶段，通过图注意力网络（GAT）代理模型反向传播调整构件几何，修正不连通与重叠部件，并做面向硬件（机器人拧螺丝）的可微几何优化。

Result: 在60个新颖自然语言输入中，86.7%的设计可实现机器人拧螺丝装配，较先前工作提升约十倍；其中10个结构用两台UR5e机器人完成物理装配演示。

Conclusion: 该工作向按需机器人制造迈出实质一步，支持定制化、小批量商品的快速生产，将设计生成、可装配性修复与机器人装配打通为自主流水线。

Abstract: Transitioning from a digital design to a robotic assembly process currently requires months of expert manual tuning to reconcile part geometries with robotic constraints. This paper presents an end-to-end, autonomous pipeline for the design and physical construction of bespoke wooden assemblies. A generative AI agent translates user prompts into initial 3D geometries, balancing the visual fidelity of the design with select physical constraints. The assemblability of the design is further improved by a gradient-based repair stage that backpropagates through a graph attention network surrogate to adjust component geometries. In addition to correcting for disjointed and overlapping components, we demonstrate hardware-specific corrections, differentiably optimizing the geometry of components to enable robot screwdriving for 86.7% of 60 novel natural language inputs, significantly outperforming prior work by a factor of ten. For ten of the structures, we physically demonstrate assemblability with two UR5e robots. This work marks a meaningful step toward on-demand robotic manufacturing, enabling the rapid production of customized, low-volume goods.

</details>


### [212] [End-to-End Autonomous Generation of Human Assembly Plans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09781)
*Faustin Arion von Arx,Millicent Schlafly,Mark D. Fuge*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 仅需输入网格装配体，系统即可自动生成包含拆解序列、子装配、工具清单、装配手册与可装配性设计反馈的完整装配计划，无需关节或紧固件标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从CAD设计到装配计划目前仍主要依赖人工，工程师需手工推理几何可行性、工具可达性、稳定性与人因工效学。作者希望把长期积累的面向装配设计（DfA）原则编码为可自动执行的端到端流程，降低人工成本。

Method: 方法分四部分：1）在物理仿真器中系统性地对物体做拆解（disassembly）搜索，结合编码DfA原则的代价函数（如装配时间代理、稳定性、工具可达性）生成序列与子装配；2）用多模态大语言模型完成手册生成、工具标注与设计反馈；3）以机械臂装配时间代理评估序列优劣；4）用视觉-语言模型作为评判者对生成手册与消融版本做对比，定位关键页面元素。输入仅需mesh，无需joint metadata或紧固件标注。

Result: 在136个、含5–30个零件的装配体上，DfA感知的序列规划相较 Tian et al. 的“始终先移除最外层零件”基线，将仿真装配时间（机械臂装配时间代理）降低35%；工具选择在88.6%的装配步骤中正确。VLM评判者识别出承载关键信息的页面元素。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将DfA原则嵌入物理仿真拆解搜索并结合多模态大模型，可在无任何关节/紧固件元数据条件下自动生成可用的装配序列、手册与设计反馈，代码开源，可供工程师或AI代理快速生成制造计划。

Abstract: Turning a CAD design into an assembly plan is still largely done by hand, requiring engineers to reason about geometric feasibility, tool access, stability, and the ergonomics of human assembly. In this work, we encode long-established design for assembly (DfA) principles into a contained, end-to-end approach for generating assembly plans. Our approach takes only a mesh assembly and produces either a step-by-step assembly manual or a structured failure report, requiring no joint metadata, fastener annotations, or additional information. Four major components of a manufacturing plan are addressed autonomously: an assembly tool list, the assembly sequence and subassemblies, an assembly manual, and design feedback for improving assemblability. For determining the sequence plan, we systematically disassemble the object in a physics simulator and apply a cost function that encodes DfA principles. Manual generation, tool labelling, and assembly feedback rely primarily on multimodal large language models. Compared with a baseline that always removes the outermost part first from Tian et al., DfA-aware sequence planning reduces simulated assembly time, measured with a robot-arm assembly-time proxy, by 35% on 136 assemblies of 5 to 30 parts. The correct tool is selected for 88.6% of assembly steps. A vision-language model judge compares the generated manuals against ablated variants, identifying which page elements carry the information a reader needs. The presented approach and open-source code are available for use by engineers or AI agents looking to rapidly accelerate the creation of manufacturing plans for a given product design.

</details>


### [213] [Enhancing Robotic Perception and Adaptability through Sensor Fusion and Origami-Inspired Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09828)
*Namai Chandra,Jaison Jose,Kavi Arya,Shivaram Kalyanakrishnan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 一款低于2 kg、低于300美元的紧凑移动机器人，利用折纸轮驱动机身俯仰，从而扫描2D LiDAR的观察几何，并将LiDAR点云与RGB-D融合，显著降低无效深度比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑移动机器人在负载与成本受限条件下，需要在光照变化和弱纹理表面上稳定恢复场景几何。单一传感器存在盲区：2D LiDAR只有单仰角切片，RGB-D在弱纹理和强光下深度缺失严重。

Method: 以折纸启发式轮子实现运动并主动调整传感几何：轮子在适应地形的构型间变化时，底盘俯仰随之扫动2D LiDAR至中间仰角，保持轮位即锁定选定视角；IMU提供底盘姿态补偿，融合节点将LiDAR回波投影进RGB-D深度流，再送入RTAB-Map建图。

Result: 在无纹理室内走廊与户外阳光区域各做3次测试：整帧无效深度比例均值室内由21%降至11%，户外由48%降至18%。未增加额外执行器，复用轮子驱动即实现连续可调LiDAR视角。

Conclusion: 复用已有轮子驱动进行主动传感几何调节，是一种低成本、低负载的深度覆盖增强方案，适合紧凑移动机器人在复杂光照与纹理条件下的建图与导航。

Abstract: Compact mobile robots must recover scene geometry under changing lighting and surface texture while working within tight payload and cost limits. We present a compact mobile robot that uses origami-inspired wheels for locomotion and active control of its sensing geometry. As the wheels move between terrain-adaptive configurations, the changing chassis pitch sweeps a 2D LiDAR through intermediate elevations; held wheel positions provide a chosen viewing angle. An IMU accounts for chassis attitude, and a fusion node projects LiDAR returns into the RGB-D depth stream supplied to RTAB-Map. The arrangement uses the wheel actuation already present on a sub-300 USD, sub-2 kg prototype to extend the scanner's viewing geometry. We assess depth fusion in a textureless indoor corridor and an outdoor sunlit area, with three runs per sensor configuration in each setting. Mean full-frame invalid-depth fractions fell from 21% to 11% indoors and from 48% to 18% outdoors. The prototype combines improved depth coverage with a continuously adjustable LiDAR viewpoint using the same actuation that reconfigures its wheels.

</details>


### [214] [Towards Accurate End-Effector Localization for UMI-Style Robotic Manipulation Teaching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09857)
*Junjie Zhang,Deteng Zhang,Zhisong Xu,Bo Sun,Liuyang Li,Yihong Tian,Jie Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MILD：面向近距操作与遮挡场景的机械臂末端定位数据集（Insta360 X5/Insight9 真实序列86条、Isaac Sim仿真子集），并给出AprilVINS——将鱼眼视觉惯性估计与序列局部AprilTag几何联合优化，在统一协议下达毫米级SE(3)对齐TCP相对APE RMSE，鱼眼VIO仅厘米级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SLAM基准偏重导航运动，而操作类数据集偏重策略学习，缺少对操作场景下末端执行器定位精度与时间覆盖度的评测；UMI式演示采集在近距离操作与相机遮挡时难以获得准确、时间连续的末端定位，且教学工位常无预先测绘的标定板地图。

Method: 构建MILD数据集：真实子集含Insta360 X5与Insight9的86条传感器序列、15类重复桌面任务、标定资产与每次执行的末端参考轨迹；MILD-Sim在Isaac Sim中扩展任务覆盖用于受控重放。提出AprilVINS，将鱼眼视觉惯性里程计与序列局部AprilTag几何结合，并将先验接纳与受保护导出分离，对联合优化状态进行受约束输出。

Result: 在仪器化真实记录上评测视觉惯性与标定板辅助系统，同一名义任务下TCP相对轨迹误差与时间覆盖度差异显著。Insta360 AprilTag4记录上，AprilVINS(full)在统一协议与序列特定配置下实现毫米级SE(3)对齐TCP相对APE RMSE、高时间完成度，且报告误差低于各对比路线在各自协议下的结果；无tag因子的鱼眼VIO仍停留厘米级。消融区分了精度与可导出性，MILD-Sim重放研究给出任务特定的误差容差参考。

Conclusion: MILD与AprilVINS共同构成面向UMI式演示采集的诊断性基准框架，代码、数据集与评测清单将在录用后释放。

Abstract: Robot demonstration learning requires accurate and temporally complete end-effector localization during close-range manipulation and camera occlusion. Existing SLAM benchmarks emphasize navigation motions, whereas manipulation datasets prioritize policy learning over localization evaluation. We introduce MILD, a Manipulation-Interface Localization Dataset with real-world and simulation sequences. The real-world subset provides 86 sensor sequences from Insta360 X5 and Insight9 across 15 repeated tabletop tasks, calibration assets, and a per-execution robot end-effector reference trajectory. The simulation subset, MILD-Sim, extends task coverage in Isaac Sim for controlled manipulation-replay studies. Benchmarking visual-inertial and fiducial-aided systems on instrumented real-world recordings reveals large differences in both TCP-relative trajectory error and temporal coverage, even under the same nominal task. To support marker-augmented teaching workspaces without a pre-surveyed fiducial map, we present AprilVINS, which combines fisheye visual-inertial estimation with sequence-local AprilTag geometry and separates prior admission from guarded export of the jointly optimized state. On Insta360 AprilTag4 recordings, AprilVINS(full) under a unified protocol with sequence-specific profiles reaches millimeter-level SE(3)-aligned TCP-relative APE RMSE with high time completion and lower reported error than the tested routes under their respective protocols, whereas fisheye VIO without tag factors remains at centimeter scale. Ablations separate accuracy from exportability, and a MILD-Sim replay study provides task-specific tolerance references for interpreting those error magnitudes. Together, MILD and AprilVINS provide a diagnostic benchmarking framework for UMI-style demonstration collection. Code, datasets, and evaluation manifests will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [215] [Juno: Taming Predictive Latents for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09940)
*Yuchen Zhu,Chenyi Xu,Yulin Zhang,Gang Xu,Wentao Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Juno：以单一 action-conditioned JEPA 作为控制对齐表征骨干、预测教师与可适应动力学模型，统一预训练、策略学习与部署三期，在 SimplerEnv 与真机上显著提升成功率与分布漂移鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA 在表征空间预测掩码/未来观测，可为 VLA 提供预测性潜变量，但直接使用存在三类失败：与具身控制不匹配、干扰动作学习、分布漂移下教师失准。作者希望用统一框架同时解决这三点，使预测潜变量在预训练、策略学习与部署全流程可用。

Method: 核心是单一 action-conditioned JEPA，承担三重角色：(1) 表征骨干（控制对齐）；(2) 预测教师；(3) 可适应动力学模型。预训练阶段在具身匹配轨迹上训练，用 dynamic CLS loss 将运动加权 patch 动力学迁移到紧凑全局状态；策略学习阶段把当前帧 JEPA patch 融入 VLA 感知，并采用解耦推理分支（独立变换参数）蒸馏未来潜状态以生成动作；部署阶段在全部观测转移（含失败 rollout）上适应世界模型，冻结适应后的教师，用 LoRA 适配器与可训练 action head 在已验证执行上重新对齐策略，无需专家纠正或任务奖励。

Result: SimplerEnv 上平均成功率由最强基线 Qwen3GR00T 的 60.9% 提升至 68.5%，测试时适应进一步达 72.7%；真机上在背景、高度、物体分布偏移下保持 70%–75% 成功率，而基线策略崩溃至 0%。

Conclusion: 用一个 action-conditioned JEPA 统一预测表征、教师蒸馏与部署期世界模型适应，可有效对齐具身控制并抵抗分布漂移，验证了预测潜变量在全流程中的复用价值。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) predict masked or future observations in representation space, offering a natural source of predictive latents for vision-language-action (VLA) models. Yet making these latents useful across pretraining, policy learning, and deployment requires addressing three failures: mismatch with embodiment-specific control, interference with action learning, and teacher miscalibration under distribution shifts. We introduce Juno, a unified framework built around one action-conditioned JEPA that serves as a control-aligned representation backbone, a predictive teacher, and an adaptable dynamics model. During pretraining, we train it on embodiment-matched trajectories and use a dynamic CLS loss to transfer motion-weighted patch dynamics to a compact global state. During policy learning, we fuse current-frame JEPA patches into VLA perception and use a decoupled reasoning branch with separate transformation parameters to distill future latent states for action generation. During deployment, we adapt the world model on all observed transitions, including failed rollouts, freeze the adapted teacher, and re-align the policy on verified executions using LoRA adapters and a trainable action head, without expert corrections or task rewards. On SimplerEnv, Juno raises average success from $60.9\%$ to $68.5\%$ over Qwen3GR00T, the strongest baseline, and test-time adaptation further reaches $72.7\%$; on a real robot, it retains $70\%$--$75\%$ success under background, height, and object shifts where the base policy collapses to $0\%$.

</details>


### [216] [Many Ways to Succeed: Diversity-Driven RL Fine-Tuning for VLA Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09943)
*Haoru Li,Jinmei Liu,Zhiyong Wang,Xiaoming Li,Zhenhong Sun,Daoyi Dong,Chunlin Chen,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DRIVE 提出将成功行为多样性作为显式 RL 微调目标，通过分组对齐轨迹并构造成功条件的内在奖励，提升 VLA 策略的 OOD 泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 策略经 RL 微调可借闭环经验获益，但泛化到微调分布之外仍受限。作者分析发现 RL 会全局收缩探索行为，却同时多样化成功轨迹、以更少 rollout 取得成功并覆盖更大潜在任务有效解空间，这种更广的成功模式覆盖可能提供分布偏移下的替代策略。

Method: DRIVE 将成功行为多样性转为显式 RL 目标：在匹配任务条件下对 rollout 分组，做时间对齐后比较轨迹，并从相对行为多样性导出成功条件的内在奖励；该设计鼓励覆盖更广可行解，同时不奖励多样化的失败或表层的时序差异。

Result: 在 LIBERO-Plus、ManiSkill3、RoboTwin 2.0 上，DRIVE 相对普通 RL 微调的 OOD 平均性能在 π0 上提升 5.3 分、π0.5 上提升 2.0 分；在双臂 AgileX PiPER-X 真机平台，平均 OOD 成功率由 64.1% 提升至 73.3%（+9.2 分），表明收益在物理部署下依然保持。

Conclusion: 显式优化成功行为多样性是提升 VLA 泛化的一种有效 RL 微调范式，且在多仿真基准与真机双臂平台上均得到验证。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) fine-tuning improves vision-language-action (VLA) policies through closed-loop experience, yet generalization beyond the fine-tuning distribution remains limited. Our analysis reveals a selective reshaping of exploration: RL contracts behavior globally, yet diversifies successful trajectories, elicits success with fewer rollouts, and covers more of the latent task-valid solution space than supervised fine-tuning. Broader successful-mode coverage may provide alternative strategies under distribution shifts. Inspired by this, we introduce DRIVE (Diversity-driven RL fIne-tuning for VLA gEneralization), which turns successful-behavior diversity into an explicit RL objective. DRIVE groups rollouts under matched task conditions, compares their trajectories with temporal alignment, and derives a success-conditioned intrinsic reward from relative behavioral diversity. This design encourages broader coverage of feasible solutions without rewarding diverse failures or superficial timing differences. Across LIBERO-Plus, ManiSkill3, and RoboTwin 2.0, DRIVE improves the average out-of-domain (OOD) performance over vanilla RL fine-tuning by 5.3 points on $\pi_0$ and 2.0 points on $\pi_{0.5}$. On a dual-arm AgileX PiPER-X platform, DRIVE further increases average OOD success from 64.1% to 73.3% (+9.2 points), demonstrating gains that persist under physical deployment.

</details>


### [217] [Borrowed Eyes: Markerless Nano-UAV Flight with an Active Quadruped Observer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09967)
*Alejandro Lorite Mora,Dimitrios Arapis,Andrés Faíña*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出四足机器人搭载臂载相机对微型无人机进行视觉定位的系统，通过感知感知NMPC动态调整机身与机械臂以保持无人机可见性，仅靠外部估计实现微型无人机起降全过程定位（中位3D误差59 mm）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微型无人机载荷受限，在GNSS拒止环境中难以搭载足够的传感器与算力实现自定位，但可进入大型机器人无法通过的狭窄空间；因此需要外部平台分担定位负担。

Method: 四足机器人用分割掩码与深度估计无人机在本体坐标系中的位置，并通过实时无线链路发送给无人机；设计感知感知非线性模型预测控制器（NMPC），将观测可靠性作为主要控制目标，动态调整机身与机械臂姿态以最大化无人机可见性；视觉短暂遮挡时无人机平滑切换至机载惯性飞行。

Result: 仅依赖外部估计，微型无人机完成从起飞到降落的预设轨迹，3D定位误差中位数59 mm；系统对短时视觉遮挡具备鲁棒性，可平滑过渡到机载惯性飞行。

Conclusion: 该框架使四足机器人可为微型无人机卸载定位负担，实现单个机器人无法独立完成的复杂空间巡检。

Abstract: Nano unmanned aerial vehicles (nano-UAVs) can navigate confined spaces that larger robots cannot, but their payload capacity severely limits the sensors and compute available for self-localization in global navigation satellite system (GNSS)-denied environments. We present a vision-based system that localizes a nano-UAV from a quadruped robot with an arm-mounted camera. The quadruped tracks the drone, estimates its position in its own coordinate system using segmentation masks and depth, and transmits that position over a real-time radio link. To ensure continuous tracking, we developed a perception-aware nonlinear Model Predictive Controller (NMPC) that dynamically adjusts the quadruped's body and arm to maximize the drone's visibility, treating observation reliability as a primary control objective. Relying solely on this external estimate, the nano-UAV executed predefined trajectories from takeoff to landing with a median 3D localization error of 59 mm. The system demonstrates robustness against short visual occlusions by smoothly transitioning to onboard inertial flight when line-of-sight is temporarily lost. Ultimately, this framework allows a quadruped to offload the localization burden of a nano-UAV, enabling inspection of complex spaces that neither robot could navigate alone.

</details>


### [218] [Decoding Neural Population Dynamics through Robotic Analog](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09977)
*Wenhui Chen,Jiyue Tao,Yitao Cheng,Yutong Shi,Feitian Zhang,Xitong Liang,Ke Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究构建了带人工肌肉、多模态传感器和强化学习神经控制器的机器人"类比系统"，复现了动物运动皮层中旋转型神经群体动力学，发现神经旋转产生与伸手方向正交的振荡运动以优化轨迹调整，并获得灵长类神经数据验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动物实验已表明精确自主运动源自运动皮层中的旋转神经群体动力学，但其物理效应（即动力学如何因果地转化为运动结果）尚不清楚，缺乏可控、可介入的实验平台。

Method: 构建具身化机器人类比系统：人工肌肉驱动 + 多模态传感 + 通过强化学习训练的神经网络控制器；使控制器自发形成与生物相似的旋转型神经群体动力学，并与灵长类神经记录数据做交叉验证，辅以神经能量原理与学习过程分析。

Result: 机器人类比系统实现精确运动、对损伤的鲁棒性及类动物神经群体动力学；神经旋转生成与伸手方向正交的振荡机动以优化轨迹调整，该现象被灵长类神经数据证实；还揭示传感器与执行器冗余下的反直觉神经能量原理，以及运动学习中的"顿悟"现象。

Conclusion: 任务驱动的具身模型揭示了神经群体动力学与运动结果之间的因果联系，为理解神经动力学如何支撑精确灵活运动提供新视角，并可启发具备动物式运动能力的智能机器人。

Abstract: Animal evidence shows that precise voluntary movements arise from rotational neural population dynamics in motor cortex, but their physical effects remain unknown. We developed a robotic analog of biological motor systems with artificial muscles, multimodal sensors, and a neural network controller trained via reinforcement learning. The robotic analog exhibited accurate movements, robustness to damage, and neural population dynamics akin to animals. This task-driven, embodied model illuminates the causal link between neural population dynamics and motor outcomes. We discovered that neural rotations generate oscillatory maneuvers orthogonal to the reaching direction, optimizing trajectory adjustments, which is confirmed by primate neural data. The model also revealed counterintuitive neural energy principles under sensor and motor redundancies, and striking Eureka moments during motor learning, bridging biological and artificial systems. These findings provide new perspectives on how neural dynamics contribute to accurate and flexible movement, inspiring future intelligent robots with animal-like mobility.

</details>


### [219] [Tactile Reconstruction of Contact Task Frames and Forces for Hybrid Force/Motion Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10020)
*Antonio Rapuano,Simone Orelli,Barbara Bazzana,Antonio Franchi,Alessandro De Luca*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 仅用软光学触觉图像在线估计接触力与时变任务坐标系，通过自标注学习+EKF融合本体感受实现闭环力调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合力/位控制需要接触力与任务坐标系（力控/位控方向）的先验知识，传统方法依赖力/力矩传感或模型残差，且常需名义环境模型。作者希望摆脱F/T传感与模型假设，仅用软光学触觉传感在线获取这些量。

Method: 假设局部平面接触且接触力矩可忽略，将软光学触觉传感器形变弹体单帧图像映射到可观测接触变量：压入深度、两个表面-传感器倾角、3D接触力，并给出逐样本不确定性。该映射通过自标注采集流程学习：机械臂施加受控接触，辅助F/T传感器离线提供真值标签。触觉测量与机器人本体感受在扩展卡尔曼滤波中融合，持续更新接触任务坐标系与交互力估计。

Result: 在UR10机械臂上搭载DigiTac传感器，对人类操作者引起的平板刚体（作线性与角运动）进行闭环接触力调节实验，仅以触觉作为唯一外感受反馈，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 表明仅靠软光学触觉即可在线估计接触力与时变任务坐标系，并在仅触觉反馈条件下实现闭环力调节，为无F/T传感的混合力/位控制提供可行路径。

Abstract: Hybrid force/motion control requires knowledge of the interaction force and of a task frame defining the force- and motion-controlled directions. These quantities are usually obtained from force/torque sensing or model-based residuals, often assuming also a nominal environment model. This work addresses the online estimation of the contact force and a possibly time-varying task frame using only soft optical tactile sensing, under the assumption of locally planar contact with a negligible contact moment. The proposed method maps a single image of the deformed elastomer of a soft optical tactile sensor to observable contact variables: indentation depth, two surface-to-sensor tilt angles, and 3D contact force, each with a per-sample uncertainty estimate. The mapping is learned through a self-labeling acquisition procedure, in which a manipulator imposes controlled contacts while an auxiliary Force/Torque sensor is used offline to provide ground-truth labels. The tactile measurement is then fused with robot proprioceptive data in an Extended Kalman Filter, producing a continuously updated estimate of the contact task frame and of the interaction force. Control experiments with a DigiTac sensor mounted on a UR10 manipulator demonstrate closed-loop contact force regulation against a flat rigid board in linear and angular motion by a human operator, with touch as the only exteroceptive feedback.

</details>


### [220] [Video-to-Model: Automatic Modeling of Deformable Linear Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10039)
*Akshun Sharma,Kimia Forghani,Yancy Diaz-Mercado*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 从单目视频中自动估计可变形缝合线运动的CBF–CLF–QP结构化模型参数，实现视频到模型的可变形线性物体建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可变形线性物体（如缝合线）的动力学建模通常依赖繁琐的手动参数调节，且缺乏从真实视频自动获取模型参数的框架。作者希望减少人工调参，推动基于视频的自动建模。

Method: 框架分三步：1）感知模块在视频中定位并跟踪缝合线，输出有序的线节点序列；2）用时空CNN处理观测到的线运动，估计CBF–CLF–QP结构化模型的有效参数；3）在用户给定的针速度输入下用估计参数仿真线体运动。

Result: 在未见过的线几何与运动配置上，框架能可靠地从视频重建线体行为，自动配置结构化模型，并以较低跟踪误差复现预期线体运动。

Conclusion: 该工作将视频感知与结构化物理仿真结合，减少手动调参，是朝向可变形线性物体自动视频建模的一步，但仅在缝合线这一窄域验证。

Abstract: This paper presents a video-to-model framework for automatically modeling the motion of a deformable suture thread from an input video. We utilize a recently developed CBF--CLF--QP numerical model that simplifies the characterization of deformable string motion through the selection of a small number of parameters. A perception module first localizes and tracks the thread in video, producing an ordered sequence of thread nodes. The observed thread motion is then processed by a spatio-temporal CNN network that estimates the effective parameters of a structured CBF--CLF--QP model. These parameters are used to simulate the thread under a user-defined needle velocity input. Experiments using unseen thread configurations and motion demonstrate that the framework can reliably reconstruct the thread behavior from video, automatically configure the structured model, and reproduce the expected thread motion with low tracking error. The proposed approach reduces the need for manual parameter tuning and provides a step toward automatic video-based modeling of deformable linear objects.

</details>


### [221] [Distributed Motion Planning for Multi-Robot Systems under Topological Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10065)
*Gianpietro Battocletti,Dimitris Boskos,Dimos V. Dimarogonas,Bart De Schutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于MPC的分布式控制器，用绕数（winding number）作为辫群拓扑规范的代理，通过代价函数项连续跟踪，并将全局辫规范解耦为成对局部MPC问题，结合一致性进度估计实现多机器人协调，仿真与实机验证了更快执行速度与更低控制代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑辫（braid）能以紧凑、抽象的形式编码多机器人复杂时空协调模式，但现有分布式执行方法通常一次只执行一个辫生成元，导致轨迹执行缓慢且次优，缺乏高效分布式执行辫约束的手段。

Method: 以绕数（辫的拓扑不变量）替代直接跟踪辫规范：1）将离散辫规范转化为连续函数，便于在MPC代价函数中加入相应项跟踪；2）将全局辫规范解耦为一组成对规范，仅需解局部MPC问题即可分布式跟踪；3）采用基于一致性的进度估计使机器人同步运动以维持全局协调。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界实验中验证了方法有效性，在执行速度和控制代价两方面均优于现有基于逐生成元执行的方法。

Conclusion: 以绕数为代理的MPC分布式框架可高效执行辫拓扑规范，兼顾连续可跟踪性与分布式解耦，并通过一致性进度估计保持全局协调，为多机器人拓扑协调提供可实机部署的方案。

Abstract: Efficient and distributed coordination of mobile robots is one of the main challenges in multi-robot systems. Topological constraints, often expressed as topological braids, are a popular tool to encode complex coordination patterns between multiple mobile robots, as they offer a compact and abstract representation of the desired qualitative relation between the space-time trajectories of the robots. However, execution of joint motion plans encoded as braid-based topological constraints via distributed controllers is challenging, with existing approaches, generally based on the execution of one braid generator at a time, producing slow and suboptimal trajectories. We propose a distributed controller based on Model Predictive Control (MPC) to efficiently execute braid-based topological specifications. Rather than directly tracking the braid specification, we propose to use winding numbers, which are topological invariants for braids, as a proxy. This has the twofold benefit of converting braids into a continuous function, which can be easily tracked by an MPC controller through an appropriate term in the cost function, and of decoupling the global braid specification into a set of pairwise specifications, which can be tracked distributedly through the solution of only local MPC problems. To maintain global coordination, we propose a consensus-based progress estimation approach, which allows the robots to synchronize their motion toward the desired specification. We validate the proposed approach in simulation and in real-world experiments, where we demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach and the improvement over existing approaches in terms of execution speed and control effort.

</details>


### [222] [RealtimeWAM: How Fast Can I Run My World Action Model?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10079)
*Huanan Liu,Ye Li,Kangye Ji,Xiaoyu Chen,Hanyun Cui,Yutian Shen,Yuan Meng,Chenglei Wu,Jingyan Jiang,Bo Li,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RealtimeWAM：一个免训练、通用的推理加速框架，通过并行执行与自适应计算协调，对World Action Model实现低延迟闭环控制推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: World Action Models结合视觉动力学建模与动作生成，但推理延迟高，限制了机器人闭环响应的实时性。已有方案如FastWAM依赖定制化架构，通用缓存策略又只利用特征冗余，无法捕捉闭环控制中动态变化的计算需求。

Method: 提出RealtimeWAM，免训练框架：利用层级依赖将观测处理与预测重叠并行；针对并发分支争抢GPU资源的问题，在整条流水线上做自适应计算——在视觉稳定区域缓存观测特征做选择性复用，复用Transformer残差，同时为小幅预测调整保留额外精化计算。在FastWAM与OpenWAM上跨RoboTwin、LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus评测。

Result: RTX 4090上平均推理延迟24.09ms与63.09ms，平均加速8.90×与10.67×；平均成功率82.75%与87.41%，与原生推理相差仅0.02与0.53个百分点。五项真实世界任务中，成功率较原生推理分别提升17.2与37.2个百分点。

Conclusion: RealtimeWAM以免训练、架构无关的方式显著降低WAM推理延迟，并在仿真与真实机器人任务上保持甚至提升成功率，兼具通用性与实用性。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) combine visual dynamics modeling with action generation, but their high inference latency limits responsive robot control. Recent efforts accelerate inference by removing explicit future-video generation at test time, as in FastWAM, an approach that requires a specially tailored architectural design. More general caching strategies exploit feature redundancy, but redundancy alone does not capture the changing computational demands of closed-loop control. To address these challenges, we present RealtimeWAM, a general, training-free framework that coordinates parallel execution with adaptive computation for low-latency inference across diverse WAM architectures. We exploit layerwise dependencies to overlap observation processing with prediction. However, concurrent branches still compete for GPU resources, limiting the benefit of parallel execution. We therefore adapt computation throughout the pipeline through selective reuse, caching observation features in visually stable regions and reusing Transformer residuals while reserving additional refinement for small predicted adjustments. We evaluate RealtimeWAM on FastWAM and OpenWAM across RoboTwin, LIBERO, and LIBERO-Plus. On an RTX 4090, measured mean inference latencies are 24.09 and 63.09 ms, corresponding to average speedups of 8.90$\times$ and 10.67$\times$. Average success rates are 82.75% and 87.41%, respectively, within 0.02 and 0.53 percentage points of native inference. Across five real-world tasks, RealtimeWAM improves average success rates over native inference by 17.2 and 37.2 percentage points on FastWAM and OpenWAM, respectively.

</details>


### [223] [Lifelong small-object navigation in changing object layouts: a benchmark and method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10125)
*Jiagan Huang,Zikun Zhou,Zijian Ni,Hongpeng Wang,Guangming Lu,Jun Yu,Wenjie Pei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出“终身小物体导航于变化布局”(LiSoNav-COL)任务，构建28个室内场景、45类小物体的基准LiSoNav-Eval，并提出基于多视角检视与视点锚定记忆的导航方法IVAM-Nav。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭机器人需反复导航至同一环境中的小物体（工具、玩具等），这类物体视觉足迹小、易被遮挡，且可能被人移动而机器人未察觉；已有方法缺乏针对“无先验建图、物体被重定位”设定下的基准与方法。

Method: 提出 LiSoNav-COL 任务形式化：智能体以空场景记忆起步，需主动寻找合适视点可靠观测、累积并复用场景知识、并在物体搬移后更新过时记忆。方法 IVAM-Nav 从互补视角主动观察承载表面以提升小物体感知，并将记忆锚定于观测视点，从而支持相似视角条件下的关系记忆复用与再验证。

Result: 在 LiSoNav-Eval 上，IVAM-Nav 相较代表性方法取得较好表现；基准分析显示较小物体、较大环境与更长搬移距离带来更大挑战。

Conclusion: 该工作补足了小物体终身导航在变化布局下的基准空白，并以视点锚定记忆机制应对感知困难与记忆失效问题，为具身导航中的长期场景记忆提供参考。

Abstract: Household robots need to continually navigate to different objects in the same environment, many of which are small and portable, such as tools and toys. Their small visual footprint and frequent occlusion make reliable observation difficult, and they may be moved by people without the robot observing the changes. We formulate this challenging task as Lifelong Small-object Navigation in Changing Object Layouts (LiSoNav-COL). Agents must seek suitable viewpoints for reliable observation, accumulate and reuse scene knowledge to efficiently locate subsequent targets, and update outdated memory after object relocation. To eliminate the need for prior scene scanning, we also require agents to start navigation with empty scene memory. Although practical, this task still lacks benchmarks designed around its defining assumptions. To bridge this gap, we introduce LiSoNav-Eval, a dedicated benchmark spanning 28 indoor scenes with 45 small-object categories. Its lifelong navigation sequences include both unchanged and relocated targets to evaluate memory reuse and adaptation to object relocation. To address this challenging task, we propose a navigation method based on multi-view Inspection with Viewpoint-Anchored Memory, dubbed IVAM-Nav. IVAM-Nav actively observes supporting surfaces from complementary viewpoints for reliable small-object perception and anchors the resulting memory to their observation viewpoints, supporting relational memory reuse and revalidation under similar viewing conditions. Extensive experiments on LiSoNav-Eval demonstrate favorable performance of IVAM-Nav against representative methods. Benchmark analyses also show that smaller objects, larger environments, and longer relocation distances pose greater challenges. The dataset and code are available here.

</details>


### [224] [Do Vision-Language-Action Models Understand Instructions? A Mechanistic Interpretability Study on Language Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10178)
*Theodor Wulff,Angelo Cangelosi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文用激活/归因修补(activation/attribution patching)对π0.5与GR00T N1.7两个VLA模型做机制可解释性分析，考察其动作生成对语言指令的依赖程度。五种指令扰动策略显示：模型对抽象改写和虚构物体不敏感，但对空指令和方向性语言高度敏感；敏感性位点模型间差异明显（GR00T集中于早期周期性交叉注意力层，π0.5分散于最早层与部分后层）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型号称跨环境、跨任务描述泛化，但其动作生成究竟依赖语言指令，还是主要靠视觉线索与表层相关性，尚不清楚。VLAs缺少显式语言接地模块，仅依赖VLM骨干的隐式接地能力，因此需要对语言接地能力做可控的机制性研究。

Method: 在LIBERO基准上，对π0.5与GR00T N1.7的动作生成模块残差流施加激活修补与归因修补；用五种策略系统性破坏任务指令：同义词替换、语义缩放、方向性破坏、随机物体替换、空字符串；并对比两模型敏感性的层位分布。

Result: 两模型对抽象改写、指向不存在物体均相对不敏感，但对空任务描述、尤其对方向性语言反应强烈。敏感性位点：GR00T N1.7主要在早期周期性交叉注意力层，π0.5则分散在最早层与部分后层。GR00T N1.7中方向扰动产生部分最大因果效应，但内部表征几何几乎不变（解离现象），π0.5中不明显。归因修补可靠性依模型而异：对GR00T N1.7与激活修补高度一致，对π0.5则不然。

Conclusion: VLA的语言接地是部分且风格化的：抽象语义与指代无关紧要，而方向性与任务是否存在才是关键。接地机制在两模型间位置不同，因果效应与表征几何可解离，且归因修补作为代理指标的可靠性因模型而异，提示此类可解释性结论需跨模型验证。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models are designed to generalise across environments and task descriptions, raising the question of whether their action generation actually depends on the language instruction, or whether they largely rely on visual cues and superficial correlations. Robustness to variance in the visual and linguistic observation space is critical for real-world deployment, yet VLAs lack explicit grounding modules and instead rely on the intrinsic language grounding capabilities of their Vision-Language model backbones. For this reason, we conduct a controlled mechanistic interpretability study on the language grounding capabilities of two state-of-the-art Vision-Language-Action models, $\pi_{0.5}$ and GR00T N1.7, by applying activation and attribution patching to the residual stream of the action generation modules. We systematically corrupt the task instruction of input samples of the LIBERO benchmark following five strategies: synonym replacement, semantic scaling, directional corruption, random object substitution, and empty string. Our experiments find that both models are comparatively insensitive to abstract rephrasing and to referencing non-existent objects, but react strongly to empty task descriptions and, especially, to directional language. During action generation, this sensitivity is concentrated in different loci for each model: mainly in the early, periodic cross-attention layers for GR00T N1.7, versus distributed across the earliest and selected later layers for $\pi_{0.5}$. For GR00T N1.7, directional perturbations drive some of the largest causal effects while leaving the internal representational geometry comparatively unchanged, a dissociation we do not observe clearly for $\pi_{0.5}$. Finally, the reliability of attribution patching is model-dependent: it closely tracks activation patching for GR00T N1.7 but not for $\pi_{0.5}$.

</details>


### [225] [Design of a Fully Actuated 4-DOF Robotic Finger With Joint-Specific Hybrid Remote Actuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10180)
*Hyojae Kang,Hyun-mok Jung,Joonho Lee,Dongil Park,Hyunmin Do,Jongwoo Park,Jeongdo Ahn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出全驱动4自由度机械手指，采用关节特异的混合远程驱动架构：MCP由双协调刚性连杆组驱动，PIP与DIP通过含圆弧滚动接触关节(RCJ)的闭环线传动独立驱动，实现远端传动的机械解耦，并完成解析建模与定量实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多自由度机械手指的驱动单元难以全部集成在指内，远程驱动会带来关节间耦合，尤其是PIP运动干扰DIP差动驱动；因此需要一种既能保持全驱动能力、又能实现远端传动解耦的混合架构。

Method: MCP关节用两组协调刚性连杆传动；PIP、DIP用闭环线传动并引入圆弧滚动接触关节，PIP传动半径大于DIP；RCJ线几何保证关节转动时线环总长不变；DIP走线设计使MCP构型固定时PIP运动不影响其差动驱动。对远端线传动、MCP连杆与指尖运动学进行解析建模；实验用ArUco标记跟踪测量滚珠丝杠位移量化传动行为，并测试孤立驱动下的指尖力与抓取姿态。

Result: MCP驱动会引起PIP、DIP传动单元的可测位移；在MCP固定时，孤立驱动PIP与DIP验证了远端传动间的预期机械解耦。孤立MCP、PIP、DIP驱动的平均峰值指尖力分别为21.28 N、9.22 N、5.75 N；并用不同几何与尺寸的物体考察了手指姿态。

Conclusion: 该关节特异混合远程驱动架构在实现4自由度全驱动的同时，达成了远端传动的机械解耦，并提供了可量化、可解析建模的传动性能，适用于多指灵巧手的手指单元设计。

Abstract: This paper presents a fully actuated 4-DOF robotic finger using a joint-specific hybrid remote-actuation architecture. The metacarpophalangeal (MCP) joint is driven by two coordinated rigid-link transmission sets, whereas the proximal interphalangeal (PIP) and distal interphalangeal (DIP) joints are independently actuated by closed-loop wire transmissions incorporating circular rolling-contact joints (RCJs). A larger transmission radius is used at the PIP joint than at the DIP joint. The RCJ wire geometry maintains the total wire-loop length during joint rotation, and the DIP wire routing is designed so that PIP motion does not affect its differential actuation for a fixed MCP configuration. The distal wire transmission, MCP linkage, and fingertip kinematics are analytically modeled. In experiments, the transmission behavior was quantitatively evaluated from the ball-screw displacements measured using ArUco-marker tracking. MCP actuation produced measurable displacements of the PIP and DIP transmission units, whereas isolated PIP and DIP actuation with the MCP fixed supported the intended mechanical decoupling between the distal transmissions. The mean peak fingertip forces under isolated MCP, PIP, and DIP actuation were 21.28 N, 9.22 N, and 5.75 N, respectively. The resulting finger postures were also examined using objects of different geometries and sizes.

</details>


### [226] [Benchmarking Behavioral Steerability in Behavior Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10198)
*Minghe Gao,Zhanxi Yan,Jiahui Liu,Wendong Bu,Xiaoting Chen,Qizhou Wang,Yi Su,Siliang Tang,Jun Xiao,Yueting Zhuang,Tat-Seng Chua,Juncheng Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出"行为可操控性"(behavioral steerability)概念，并构建RoboSteer——首个针对行为基础模型(BFM)可操控性的基准，含三级层次(条件、约束、组合操控)与多模态运动语料，对9个现有BFM进行了首次大规模实证评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为基础模型(BFM)正从单纯动作生成走向通用行为系统，但能否可靠地按用户意图被"操控"这一根本问题尚缺研究。作者提出behavioral steerability概念，填补该能力评估的空白。

Method: 提出RoboSteer基准：将可操控性分为Conditional、Constraint、Compositional三级，建立统一评测框架，并配套大规模多模态运动语料；在此基础上对9个现有BFM做首次大规模实证研究。

Result: 对9个现有BFM进行了首个大规模行为可操控性实证评测，给出三级操控下的能力对比与问题揭示（摘要未给出具体量化指标）。

Conclusion: 作者将行为可操控性视为超越运动控制的能力，关乎具身系统如何把人类意图转化为有目的的行动，希望RoboSteer推动"意图实现"作为通用具身智能基础的研究。

Abstract: Behavior Foundation Models (BFMs) are emerging as a paradigm for translating human intentions into executable humanoid behaviors. As these models evolve beyond behavior generation toward general-purpose behavioral systems, a fundamental question arises: can they be reliably steered according to user intentions? In this paper, we introduce the concept of behavioral steerability, defined as the ability of BFMs to faithfully generate behaviors that satisfy user-specified intentions. To study this capability, we present RoboSteer, the first benchmark for behavioral steerability in BFMs. RoboSteer organizes behavioral steerability into a three-level hierarchy-Conditional Steering, Constraint Steering, and Compositional Steering-and establishes a unified evaluation framework supported by a large-scale multimodal motion corpus. Using RoboSteer, we conduct the first large-scale empirical study of behavioral steerability across 9 existing BFMs. We view behavioral steerability as more than a capability for controlling motion: it concerns how embodied systems translate human intentions into purposeful actions. We hope RoboSteer will advance research on intention realization as a foundation for general-purpose embodied intelligence.

</details>


### [227] [Making Task Abstractions Executable: Control-Aware Layout Repair for a Fixed Controller](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10281)
*Chiyoung Kim,Seungyeon Back,Sumin Shim,Doyoung Heo,Rita Singh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将任务抽象编译为可审计的仿射布局约束，仅修复声明的连续坐标，实现整任务跟踪、间隙与驱动要求的可验证布局修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务抽象可规定期望事件，但其空间布局可能导致固定智能体与控制器无法完成这些事件，需要在不改变事件顺序、时序、拓扑与控制器的前提下修复布局。

Method: 从结构化任务记录出发，将整任务跟踪、间隙（clearance）与驱动（actuation）需求编译为可审计的仿射布局约束；仅修复已声明的连续坐标；采用"最大违反行"更新，提供条件有限认证与净位移界；用同一编译器的二次投影把表示与优化器解耦。

Result: 在三个研究者自建任务抽象上，两种后端均认证全部三个布局，并完成每种后端300次全新的成对 rollout；风险目标扫描还暴露出所选证书仅靠布局编辑无法满足的固定事件测试。

Conclusion: 该工作给出可审计、可认证的布局修复流程，保持事件序、时序、拓扑与控制器不变，并将表示层与优化器分离；同时揭示认证能力对固定事件测试的局限。

Abstract: A task abstraction can specify the intended events while its spatial layout prevents a fixed agent and controller from completing them. Starting from a supplied structured task record, we compile whole-task tracking, clearance, and actuation requirements into auditable affine layout constraints. We repair only declared continuous coordinates, preserving event order, timing, topology, and the controller. A most-violated-row update admits conditional finite-certification and net-displacement bounds; a same-compiler quadratic projection separates the representation from the optimizer. On three researcher-authored task abstractions, both backends certify all three layouts and complete all 300 fresh paired rollouts per backend. A risk-target sweep also exposes fixed event tests that the chosen certificate cannot satisfy through layout edits alone.

</details>


### [228] [Temporal Visuo-Tactile Learning for Dexterous Grasp Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10283)
*Ken Nakahara,Aleksei Buvailik,Prokhor Kotov,Roberto Calandra*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 利用四指Digit 360触觉传感器采集1万次抓取试验，训练时序多模态模型预测提离后稳定性，并作为在线门控实现10.5个百分点成功率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人抓取研究多依赖视觉与平行夹爪，忽视了指尖高分辨率动态触觉在稳定性判断中的作用；作者希望验证触觉（尤其是动态触觉）对多指灵巧手抓取稳定性预测与模型引导重抓取的贡献，并避免显式接触/力建模。

Method: 搭建装备4个Digit 360触觉传感器的多指灵巧手平台，跨200个物体采集10,000次抓取试验，同步记录外部视觉、本体感觉与触觉时序流；训练端到端时序多模态模型，以提离前观测预测提离后稳定性，并对比不同传感模态与编码骨干网络，配合受控输入消融；将预测器部署为真实机器人上的在线稳定性门控，引导重抓取。

Result: 消融显示引入触觉、特别是高分辨率动态触觉，可提升抓取稳定性预测；在线部署中，视触觉模型引导的重抓取使实际执行提离的成功率相对非触觉门控提升10.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 丰富的指尖传感配合捕捉触觉动态的时序模型，可在不显式建模接触或力的情况下支撑多指手的学习式抓取，为从触觉经验走向稳定灵巧操作提供了可扩展的数据驱动路径；数据集已公开。

Abstract: Humans can grasp everyday objects with almost perfect success rates using fingertip tactile feedback, yet much of the robotic grasping literature emphasizes vision-based grasp selection with parallel grippers. In this work, we systematically investigate how high-resolution, dynamic tactile sensing contributes to grasp stability prediction and model-guided grasping in dexterous robotic hands. To this end, we collected a dataset of 10,000 grasp trials across 200 objects using a multi-fingered robotic hand equipped with four Digit 360 tactile sensors, recording external vision, proprioception, and tactile streams throughout each grasp. With this dataset, we trained end-to-end temporal multimodal models to predict post-lift stability from pre-lift grasp observations and compared sensing modalities and encoding backbones. Experimental results and controlled input ablations show that incorporating touch, and particularly high-resolution, dynamic touch, improves grasp stability prediction. Finally, we deployed the learned predictor as an online stability gate on the real robot, where visuo-tactile model-guided regrasping improved the success rate among executed lifts by 10.5 percentage points over a non-tactile gate. These results show how rich fingertip sensing and expressive temporal models that capture the dynamics of touch can support learned grasping with multi-fingered hands without explicit contact or force modeling, providing a scalable data-driven path from tactile experience toward stable dexterous manipulation. The dataset is publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [229] [Energy-Efficient Gait Adaptation via Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning for Quadrupedal Locomotion Across Diverse Terrains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10297)
*Ammar Issa,Anubhav Singh,Anton Tsaritsin,Sergey Kolyubin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出分层强化学习框架，高层低频策略显式优化运输代价(CoT)并做步态自适应，低层高频策略负责关节级稳定执行，三阶段Isaac训练实现零样本sim-to-real，在Unitree AlienGo上验证能耗与鲁棒性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端RL步态控制中步态生成、运动执行与能耗优化高度耦合，对奖励设计极敏感，难以在宽速度范围与多地形下同时保持低能耗与鲁棒性。

Method: 分层RL：高频策略负责稳定鲁棒的关节级运动执行，低频策略进行步态自适应并显式最小化运输代价CoT；采用三阶段Isaac仿真训练流程实现零样本sim-to-real。

Result: 相比代表性单策略与分层locomotion基线，在宽指令速度范围内降低CoT，在平地、崎岖不平与斜坡地形保持鲁棒行走；学习到速度相关步态自适应（低速pace、高速trot）；在Unitree AlienGo四足上完成零样本部署。

Conclusion: 将频率分离与显式能耗优化结合，可在不牺牲鲁棒性的前提下显著提升四足locomotion能效，并具备零样本实机可行性与自动步态切换能力。

Abstract: While energy efficiency is a critical objective for legged-robot locomotion control, achieving low energy consumption while maintaining robust performance across different velocity ranges and terrain conditions remains a key challenge. This is particularly true for end-to-end RL policies, where gait generation, motion execution, and energy optimization are tightly coupled, leading to high sensitivity to reward design. In this work, we propose a hierarchical reinforcement learning (HRL) framework that separates a high-frequency policy for stable and robust joint-level motion execution from low-frequency gait adaptation that explicitly minimizes the cost of transport (CoT). The three-stage Isaac-based training procedure enables zero-shot sim-to-real transfer with improved tracking accuracy, robustness, and energy efficiency. The learned hierarchy exhibits automatic speed-dependent gait adaptation, transitioning from pacing at low speeds to trotting at higher speeds. We validate the proposed approach in simulation against representative single-policy and hierarchical locomotion baselines, demonstrating reduced CoT over a broad range of commanded velocities, while maintaining robust locomotion across flat, uneven rough, and inclined terrains. We further demonstrate its practical feasibility through zero-shot deployment on a physical Unitree AlienGo quadruped.

</details>


### [230] [Hall Effect-Based Tactile Force Detection Sensor for Robot-Assisted Minimally Invasive Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10346)
*Charles DeLorey,Michael Brockdorff,Adam Metcalf,Benjamin Calmé,Pietro Valdastri,Dominic Jones*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 一种可贴附于机器人手术器械夹持面的紧凑型三轴霍尔效应触觉传感器（3个毫米级taxel），经6-DoF并联机器人标定后可检测法向与剪切力，并在体模与离体猪组织上实现牵拉与滑动感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人微创手术中外科医生与器械机械分离，丧失触觉反馈，限制了力敏感操作的安全性，因此需要能在狭小器械表面集成的小型化力/触觉传感器。

Method: 设计紧凑型触觉传感器：将霍尔效应传感器与磁体嵌入可变形弹性体接触层，形成3个毫米级taxel；利用6自由度并联机器人进行标定，使每个taxel可解耦检测法向力与剪切力。

Result: 每个taxel法向与剪切力检测平均RMSE为2.133 kPa；在体模与离体猪组织上验证，成功实现牵拉过程中的触觉感知与滑动（slip）检测。

Conclusion: 该工作提出一种小型化霍尔效应触觉传感器，可为力敏感的机器人手术应用提供触觉感知能力，但验证限于离体组织，尚缺乏活体或真实手术场景证据。

Abstract: Robotic minimally invasive surgery has revolutionized surgical practice. However, as the surgeons are mechanically separated from the surgical tools, loss of tactile feedback occurs. We present a compact force sensor with three, millimeter scale sensing elements (taxels) that can be affixed to the grasping face of a robotic surgical tool. The sensor features a miniaturized design using Hall effect sensors and magnets embedded in a deformable elastomer contact layer. Sensor calibration is achieved with a 6 degree of freedom (DoF) parallel robot. Each taxel can detect applied normal and shear forces with average RMSE of 2.133 kPa. The calibrated sensor was validated on phantom and ex vivo porcine tissue, demonstrating tactile sensing during retraction as well as slip. This work introduces a small-scale Hall effect based tactile sensor, enabling sensing for force-sensitive robotic surgical applications.

</details>


### [231] [MultiFly: A Real-World Multimodal Aerial Dataset with Annotation-Efficient Label Transfer and Cross-Modal Semantic Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10359)
*Markus Gross,Andreas Greiner,Taehyoung Kim,Sivasubiramaniam Subbiah,Tomaž Cotič,Sai Bharadwaj Matha,Conrad Christoph,Oussema Dhaouadi,Simon Zieher,Surya Vijaya Kumar,Gordon Elger,Henri Meeß,Olaf Wysocki,Paul Spannaus,Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MultiFly 是一个真实低空无人机多模态语义感知数据集，涵盖 RGB、热成像、LiDAR 与雷达四种模态，通过几何标签传播实现跨模态语义标注，并建立四模态语义分割基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态航空感知缺乏一致的帧级语义标注数据，且逐模态人工标注成本高、一致性差。

Method: 采集四郊区场景 17,272 个同步样本，配准与 GNSS-RTK/IMU 数据；仅用 115 张人工标注 RGB 图，经共享几何表示将标签传播至四种模态，生成 17,157 张 RGB、17,272 张热成像、8.4 亿 LiDAR 点与 340 万雷达点的语义标签；并建立各模态分割基准。

Result: 传播标注与留出人工标注平均一致率 89.93%，六组模态对平均语义一致性 90.94%；基准显示稠密 LiDAR 与稀疏雷达呈现不同架构行为。

Conclusion: MultiFly 为多模态空中感知提供可扩展基础，是首个同时具备 RGB、热成像、LiDAR 与雷达一致帧级语义标注的公开真实低空航空基准。

Abstract: We introduce MultiFly, a real-world, low-altitude UAV dataset for semantic perception across RGB, thermal, LiDAR, and radar modalities. MultiFly provides 17,272 synchronized samples from four suburban scenes with frame-wise annotations for 15 semantic classes, together with calibration and GNSS-RTK/IMU measurements. To avoid costly and inconsistent modality-specific annotation, we propagate labels from only 115 manually annotated RGB images through shared geometric representations to all four modalities. This approach generates semantic labels for 17,157 additional RGB images, 17,272 thermal images, 840M LiDAR points, and 3.4M radar points. Transferred annotations achieve 89.93% average agreement with held-out manual annotations, and 90.94% average semantic consistency across all six modality pairs. We further establish semantic segmentation benchmarks for all four modalities, revealing distinct architectural behavior for dense LiDAR and sparse radar data. Taken together, MultiFly provides a scalable foundation for multimodal aerial perception and, to the best of our knowledge, the first public real-world low-altitude aerial benchmark that combines consistent frame-wise semantic annotations for RGB, thermal, LiDAR, and radar. Data at this https URL .

</details>


### [232] [Semantic-Aware Predictive Mapping for Exploration and Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10382)
*Kenneth J.K. Ong,William W.J. Teo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在CogniPlan数据集中注入"门"引起的占用歧义，比较纯几何模型与加入语义门通道的预测式占用补全模型；全局指标相近，但门区域局部L1由0.004342降至0.000025、F1与IoU升至1.000000。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人探索与导航依赖从部分占用观测预测未观测区域几何，但纯占用表示无法区分几何相似而语义不同的结构（如门与墙）。作者试图验证语义门线索能否改善此类歧义区域的预测式几何占用建图。

Method: 在CogniPlan数据集子集上人工注入门导致的歧义（部分占用图中加入歧义，真值布局不变）；训练两个同数据同架构模型：仅几何的对照模型与额外输入一个门语义通道的语义提示模型；用全图与10像素门区域掩码上的L1、F1、IoU评估。

Result: 全图性能两模型大体相当；门区域局部结果显著定性改善：L1从0.004342降至0.000025，F1从0.031311升至1.000000，IoU从0.015905升至1.000000。

Conclusion: 在几何观测本身存在歧义的区域，语义线索可有效提升预测式占用补全质量，为语义-几何联合的预测建图提供初步证据。

Abstract: Predictive mapping can support robotic exploration and navigation by estimating unseen geometric layouts from partial occupancy observations. However, occupancy-only representations may fail to distinguish semantically different structures with similar geometry. This is particularly relevant for indoor doors, which may appear as occupied cells like walls but indicate possible connected rooms or corridors beyond the observed region. This work investigates whether semantic door cues improve predictive geometric occupancy mapping around such ambiguous regions. We modify a subset of the CogniPlan dataset by inserting door-induced ambiguities into partial occupancy maps while keeping the ground-truth layouts unchanged. We compare a geometry-only control model with a semantic-cued model trained on the same modified dataset, where the semantic-cued model receives an additional door channel. Evaluation uses L1 error, F1 score, and Intersection over Union (IoU) over both the full map and a 10-pixel door-region mask. Full-map performance remains broadly similar between models, but localized door-region results show a clear qualitative improvement: L1 decreases from 0.004342 to 0.000025, while F1 and IoU improve from 0.031311 and 0.015905 to 1.000000 and 1.000000, respectively. These results suggest that semantic cues can improve predictive occupancy completion in regions where geometric observations alone are ambiguous.

</details>


### [233] [OpenViTac: Learning and Benchmarking Visuo-Tactile Policies in a Unified Sim-and-Real Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10384)
*Yifan Wu,Qin Li,Nan Min,Guojin Zhong,Haoyu Zhao,Zhiyuan Li,Houze Xu,Shengqi Xu,Xingyao Lin,Zijie Diao,Zhaoxiang Liu,Shiguo Lian,Shunlin Lu,Shihao Zhao,Ziyi Ye,Zuxuan Wu,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 OpenViTac 视触觉操作基准（仿真-真实配对、四个触觉能力维度），并基于此提出 OpenVTLA 触觉增强框架探究触觉表征与融合策略，同时研究 sim-real 协同训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VTLA 策略进展迅速，但缺少统一基准来评估触觉使能的机器人在仿真与真实世界中的操作能力，且触觉表征与融合方式、跨域学习因素尚不明确。

Method: 构建配对的仿真-真实基准，将接触密集操作归为四类触觉相关能力维度；系统比较不同触觉表征与集成策略对预训练 VLA 模型的影响，据此提出 OpenVTLA 触觉增强框架；利用配对设定研究仿真-真实协同训练。

Result: 摘要未给出定量结果，仅说明基准可对 VLA、WAM、VTLA 策略进行一致性评估，并支持跨域策略学习因素分析。

Conclusion: OpenViTac 为视触觉机器人操作提供统一评估与推进平台，OpenVTLA 给出较优表征与融合方案，配对设定有利于 sim-real 迁移研究。

Abstract: Tactile feedback provides embodied agents with physical information beyond visual observations, enabling more reliable interaction with the real world. However, despite the rapid progress of vision-tactile-language-action (VTLA) policies, there remains a lack of unified benchmarks for evaluating tactile-enabled robot manipulation across simulation and the real world. To address this gap, we introduce OpenViTac, a visuo-tactile manipulation benchmark for evaluating robot policies across simulation and the real world. OpenViTac organizes contact-rich manipulation into four tactile-relevant capability dimensions and provides paired simulation-real-world settings for consistent evaluation of VLA, WAM, and VTLA policies. Building upon this benchmark, we investigate how different tactile representations and integration strategies affect the performance of pretrained VLA models. Correspondingly, we introduce OpenVTLA, a tactile augmentation framework that combines the best-performing representation and integration strategy. Furthermore, we leverage the paired benchmark setting to study sim-real co-training and analyze factors affecting cross-domain policy learning. Together, OpenViTac provides a unified platform for evaluating and advancing visuo-tactile robot manipulation.

</details>


### [234] [NeRFifyMesh: Optimizing Neural Radiance Fields from Textured Meshes for Robotics Scene Building](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10387)
*Nillan Nimal,Mahboubeh Asadi,Sajad Saeedi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出将现有3D网格模型转换为基于点的辐射场（NeRF）表示的新流程：通过对网格几何与纹理采样人工生成真值点基辐射场，无需多视角渲染或相机采样即可训练NeRF，渲染质量与基线相当，并用于构建统一NeRF场景与碰撞仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人场景表示中NeRF被广泛用于语义建图与仿真，研究者常需用多个NeRF模型（每对象一个）构建场景；而现有大量3D网格资产缺乏便捷转换工具，需避免依赖多视角图像训练的高成本流程，以加速算法开发与测试。

Method: 直接在网格表面采样几何与纹理，构造基于点的真值辐射场（point-based radiance field），以此为监督信号训练NeRF模型，替代传统的相机采样/多视角渲染管线。

Result: 大量基准测试显示渲染质量与基线方法相当；并展示将转换后的表示组合为统一NeRF场景，利用提取几何进行碰撞仿真。

Conclusion: 该流程为网格资产向NeRF表示的转换提供了免渲染的可行路径，服务于机器人仿真与场景构建，但收益主要体现在工程便利性而非泛化性或实机部署验证。

Abstract: In robotics, scene representation plays a pivotal role in understanding and interacting with the environment. The advent of Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF) and its variants, as a novel representation, has opened a new frontier of research. In applications such as semantic mapping and simulation, roboticists aim to build scenes using multiple NeRF models, each representing an object. While extensive datasets of 3D mesh models already exist, there is an urgent need to develop tools to convert these assets to NeRF models for rapid algorithm development and testing. This paper presents a new pipeline for converting existing mesh models to NeRF representations by artificially generating a ground truth point-based radiance field through sampling mesh geometry and texture. This approach alleviates the need for camera-based sampling or rendering multi-view images of the original mesh to train the NeRF model. Extensive benchmarking demonstrates that our method yields comparable rendering quality to the baselines. Additionally, the application of this representation is shown by constructing unified NeRF scenes and performing collision simulations with extracted geometry.

</details>


### [235] [RoboQuest: Generalist Physical Agents that Search, Inspect and Test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10388)
*Liu Renhang,Navonil Majumder,Tej Deep Pala,Soujanya Poria*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboQuest：面向目标导向具身探索的基准，覆盖搜索、操作式探查与交互测试三类不确定性；五个前沿多模态智能体最高成功率仅23%，微调策略几乎全败，失败主因是过早停止探索与不修复探索扰动，而非执行能力不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态基础模型已可作为通用物理智能体完成多种操作任务，但在陌生环境中，任务相关信息常缺失于观测之中：需要定位相关物体、检查未观测属性、弄清陌工具的作用。现有基准未系统考察"通过物理交互主动获取任务相关信息并据此调整动作、自主决定何时提交"这一能力，因此作者提出 RoboQuest 来量化该缺口。

Method: 构建 RoboQuest 基准，包含十项移动操作任务，围绕三类不确定性组织：搜索（search）、基于操作的探查（manipulation-based inspection）、交互式测试（interactive testing）。在统一视运动（visuomotor）接口下评测五个前沿多模态智能体，另评测在全轨迹演示上微调的 π₀.₅ 策略；同时发布完整回合演示数据。此外进行隔离测试：将隐藏信息直接提供，仅考察任务所依赖的执行技能；并做失败分析归因。

Result: 最佳智能体仅 23% 回合成功，微调 π₀.₅ 策略几乎从不成功。隔离技能测试（补充隐藏信息）显示智能体能够完成大部分所需动作，失败分析中仅少数归因于执行层面。主要失败模式：智能体在观察到完成任务所需证据之前就做出决定、过早停止探索；很少预防或修复自身探索造成的扰动；对多数模型而言试错学习仍然困难。

Conclusion: 当前前沿多模态智能体的瓶颈不在低层动作执行，而在主动信息获取、证据驱动的行动适应与自主提交时机判断，以及探索扰动的预防/修复和试错学习。RoboQuest 提供了诊断该类能力缺口的标准任务与演示数据，指向具身探索与不确定性消解方向的研究重点。

Abstract: Recent advances in multimodal foundation models have made them capable generalist physical agents for a range of manipulation tasks. However, successful operation in an unfamiliar environment may require an agent to seek task-relevant information through interaction when it is absent from the observations: it may need to determine where a relevant object is, inspect an unobserved property, or discover the effect of an unfamiliar tool. We thus introduce RoboQuest, a benchmark for goal-directed embodied exploration, where agents must actively acquire task-relevant information through physical interaction, use the resulting evidence to adapt subsequent actions, and autonomously decide when to commit to task completion. RoboQuest comprises ten mobile manipulation tasks centered on three forms of uncertainty: search, manipulation-based inspection, and interactive testing. We evaluate five frontier multimodal agents through a common visuomotor interface, as well as a $\pi_{0.5}$ policy fine-tuned on the full-episode demonstrations we release. The best agent succeeds in only 23\% of the episodes, and the fine-tuned policy almost never succeeds. Isolated tests of the execution skills the tasks are built from, with the hidden information supplied, show that the agents can carry out most of the required actions, and our failure analysis attributes only a minority of the failures to execution. Our failure analysis further finds that the agents often stop exploring too early as they make decisions before observing the required evidence for task completion. We also find that agents rarely prevent or repair the disturbances caused by their exploration. Moreover, learning by trial and error remains difficult for most models.

</details>


### [236] [RobotWorld: Benchmarking Multimodal Agents for Robot Use Across Diverse Tasks and Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10409)
*Zhiqin Yang,Chenxin Li,Xiaomeng Hu,Yibin Liu,Weidong Huang,Jiankai Sun,Haitao Li,Zijian Wu,Yuzhi Huang,Fanding Huang,Hanwen Sun,Jiashun Liu,Jingqi Tong,Mingxin Huang,Shaoli Hu,Shijue Huang,Tianyi Bai,Xinyuan Wang,Yunlong Lin,Zhengyang Tang,Zhexin Zhang,Zhuo Chen,Xierui Song,Juntao Dai,Boyuan Chen,Jiaming Ji,Fangneng Zhan,Mengkang Hu,Wei Xue,Yonggang Zhang,Han Hu,Tsung-Yi Ho,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RobotWorld仿真测试平台，用84个跨操作/移动/驾驶/飞行的任务评估通用智能体在物理世界的表现，发现智能体能构建复杂感知-控制流程但难以稳定组合成成功行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用智能体在代码、工具使用等数字任务上能力快速提升，但其能力在物理世界中的可迁移程度尚不明确，缺少带显式交互预算和可执行成功判据的严格评测环境。

Method: 构建RobotWorld仿真测试床，将指令与观测通过机器人接口转化为物理任务执行；涵盖操作、移动作业、运动、驾驶与空中控制共84个任务，设置显式交互预算与可执行成功校验，并结合任务结果与执行轨迹做细粒度分析。

Result: 智能体能搭建图像分割、相机标定、空间估计、动力学计算等感知与控制流程，但无法稳定组合为成功行为：到达目标位姿却丢失任务相关物体状态、无法纠正无效动作、恢复过晚、误判任务完成；Astra在空间与受限接触目标上表现更好，Opus 5.5在连续平衡与定时交互目标上表现更好。

Conclusion: RobotWorld既提供严格的评测基准，也给出对剩余能力缺口的实证刻画，为训练与设计更可靠的物理世界智能体提供了具体目标。

Abstract: General-purpose agents increasingly write code, use tools, and complete complex digital tasks, raising the question of how far these capabilities carry into the physical world. To investigate this, we introduce RobotWorld, a challenging simulation testbed for robot use: turning instructions and observations into physical task execution through robot interfaces. Its 84 tasks span manipulation, mobile manipulation, locomotion, driving, and aerial control, with explicit interaction budgets and executable success checks. By analysing task outcomes alongside execution traces, we identify both the capabilities that transfer and the gaps that prevent reliable completion. Furthermore, we find that current agents can construct sophisticated perception and control workflows, including image segmentation, camera calibration, spatial estimation, and dynamics-based computation. These capabilities, however, do not consistently compose into successful behaviour: agents lose task-relevant object states despite reaching commanded poses, fail to correct ineffective actions, recover too late, or mistake unfinished tasks for completion. This uneven transfer also differs across models: Astra succeeds more often on spatial and constrained-contact goals, whereas Opus 5.5 succeeds more often on continuous-balance and timed-interaction goals. By linking these outcomes to execution behaviour, RobotWorld provides both a rigorous proving ground and an empirical account of the remaining capability gaps, thereby establishing concrete targets for training and designing more reliable physical-world agents.

</details>


### [237] [AirGroundVLN: A Large-Scale Benchmark for Goal-Oriented Air-Ground Collaborative Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10421)
*Zhenxuan Zeng,Qingle Wu,Wei Suo,Maojia Wu,Bairong Zhang,Hangzheng Yu,Peng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出空中-地面协同的目标导向VLN基准AirGroundVLN（19个UE环境，10281条导航情节，955个目标实例，含seen/unseen划分与空中可见性协议），并提出AG-CoNAV参考框架，含时空锚定协同记忆（SACM）与空中引导区域到局部规划（AGRLP）两个模块，实现跨平台空间一致的历史上下文维护与粗到细协同规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标导向空中-地面协同VLN缺乏大规模多样化基准，且存在两大挑战：一是空中与地面视角差异大、有用观测随导航推进而失效，难以维持跨平台、跨时间的空间一致上下文；二是空间可观测性不对称——地面感知局部精细但范围受限，空中感知范围广但局部粗糙，单平台规划可靠性不足。

Method: 构建AirGroundVLN基准：19个Unreal Engine环境、10281条导航情节、955个目标实例，提供seen/unseen划分及空中可见性评估协议。提出AG-CoNAV框架，包含两个组件：SACM（Spatiotemporally Anchored Collaborative Memory）跨空中与地面观测维护并检索空间一致的历史上下文；AGRLP（Aerial-Guided Regional-to-Local Planning）将空中区域级引导与地面细粒度导航相结合。

Result: 大量实验验证了AG-CoNAV的有效性，并确立AirGroundVLN作为面向未来研究的综合基准。摘要未给出具体量化指标或真实平台部署结果。

Conclusion: 空中-地面协同的目标导向VLN在基准与协同记忆、跨平台规划方法上取得初步进展，AirGroundVLN可作为该方向的系统评测平台。

Abstract: Goal-oriented Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) requires agents to locate and reach targets described in natural language without prescribed routes. Air--ground collaboration is valuable for tasks requiring both wide-area search and fine-grained localization. However, systematic study of goal-oriented air--ground collaborative VLN remains limited by the lack of large-scale, diverse benchmarks and two core challenges: 1) substantial differences between aerial and ground views, together with useful observations becoming unavailable as navigation proceeds, make it difficult to maintain spatially consistent context across platforms and over time; and 2) asymmetric spatial observability makes ground perception locally detailed but spatially limited and aerial perception broad but locally coarse, limiting the reliability of single-platform planning. To address these limitations, we introduce AirGroundVLN, a benchmark containing 10,281 navigation episodes and 955 target instances across 19 Unreal Engine environments, with seen/unseen splits and an aerial-visibility protocol for systematic evaluation. Alongside the benchmark, we propose AG-CoNAV, a trainable reference framework comprising two key components: Spatiotemporally Anchored Collaborative Memory (SACM) and Aerial-Guided Regional-to-Local Planning (AGRLP). SACM maintains and retrieves spatially consistent historical context across aerial and ground observations. Meanwhile, AGRLP combines regional aerial guidance with fine-grained ground navigation. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of AG-CoNAV and establish AirGroundVLN as a comprehensive benchmark for future exploration.

</details>


### [238] [RFPO: Rectified Flow Policy Optimization for Embodied Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10453)
*Ting Huang,Lisiyu Pan,Haoyu Wang,Zeyu Zhang,Siyuan Qian,Yanjun Li,Yandong Guo,Boxin Shi,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RFPO 框架，通过奖励感知在线 Reflow 与冻结高斯 PPO 控制器监督，消除流策略少步离散化差距，实现单步欧拉推理保持全步控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配策略表达力强，但迭代 ODE 积分推理代价高；直接削减步数会因"少步离散化差距"（few-step discretization gap）——即在全步执行下优化的策略并未约束其在粗积分下可靠——导致控制性能严重下降。

Method: RFPO：在 on-policy 学习中引入奖励感知在线 Reflow，修正学生策略诱导的传输路径，使其对粗积分更鲁棒；同时用冻结的高斯 PPO 控制器在全步与中间步预算下提供动作空间辅助监督；部署时仅保留单个流学生策略并以一步欧拉积分执行。

Result: 在 Unitree Go2、Spot、Unitree H1、Unitree G1 上，单步执行的回报保持在对应 64 步值的 2.4% 以内（零初始化与随机初始化均成立）。Go2 上单步保留 64 步回报的 98.5%，机载平均推理延迟由 4.39 ms 降至 0.08 ms，加速 54.9 倍；真机实验验证了一步步态稳定行走。

Conclusion: 该工作表明通过奖励感知 Reflow 与辅助监督可有效弥合流策略的少步离散化差距，在保持控制性能的同时大幅降低推理延迟，具备真机可部署性，且已开源代码与网站。

Abstract: Flow-based policies provide an expressive framework for continuous robot control, but their iterative ODE integration incurs substantial inference cost. Naively reducing the integration budget can severely degrade control, since policies optimized under full-step execution are not explicitly constrained to remain reliable under coarse numerical integration. We refer to this mismatch as the few-step discretization gap. To address this problem, we introduce RFPO, a flow-policy optimization framework for reliable few-step execution. Reward-aware online Reflow rectifies student-induced transport paths during on-policy learning, making the resulting policy more robust to coarse integration. A frozen Gaussian PPO controller supplies complementary action-space supervision at full and intermediate integration budgets, while the deployed policy remains a single flow student executed with one Euler step. Across Unitree Go2, Boston Dynamics Spot, Unitree H1, and Unitree G1, RFPO consistently preserves full-step control performance under one-step execution, with one-step returns remaining within 2.4% of their corresponding 64-step values across both zero and random initialization. On Unitree Go2, one-step execution retains 98.5% of the 64-step reward while reducing onboard mean inference latency from 4.39 ms to 0.08 ms, yielding a 54.9x speedup. Real-robot experiments further validate stable one-step locomotion. Code: this https URL . Website: this https URL .

</details>


### [239] [FoldBack: Self-Correcting Masked Generative Policy for Long-Horizon Garment Folding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10462)
*Lipeng Zhuang,Shiyu Fan,Yingdong Ru,Zhuo He,Florent P. Audonnet,Paul Henderson,Gerardo Aragon Camarasa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FoldBack 是一种可自我纠正的掩码生成式策略，在长时程叠衣任务中实现推理时的"精炼—回滚—重试"闭环，无需恢复示范或基策略重训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程操作策略在抓取漏抓或滑脱后仍会继续执行，导致衣物未达到目标构型却无法察觉，因此需要在推理时引入可编辑的失败检测与修复机制。

Method: 围绕三个推理时决策构建恢复机制：(1) 何时精炼与验证——将精炼和抓取验证对齐到 pick-and-place 事件；(2) 如何回滚——回到可重试的预抓取构型同时保留已成功的抓取；(3) 何处如何重试——选择性重生成失败片段及部分未来动作，并规避此前失败的抓取位置。

Result: 在 6 类、33 件真实衣物上，最终折叠成功率 75.2%、最终掩码 IoU 0.837，显著优于最强基线（45.7% / 0.689）。

Conclusion: FoldBack 是首个统一上述三类决策的可编辑全轨迹策略，能够在执行继续前检测、撤销并修复失败交互，且不需要恢复示范或基策略重训练。

Abstract: We present FoldBack, a self-correcting masked generative policy for long-horizon garment folding. Existing long-trajectory policies may continue after a missed or slipped grasp even when the garment has not reached the intended configuration. We structure FoldBack's recovery mechanisms around three inference-time decisions: when to refine and verify, how to roll back, and where and how to retry. FoldBack aligns refinement and grasp verification with pick-and-place events, returns the robot to a retryable pre-grasp configuration while preserving successful grasps, and selectively regenerates the failed segment and selected future actions while avoiding previous failed grasp locations. To our knowledge, FoldBack is the first editable full-trajectory policy to unify these decisions, enabling failed interactions to be detected, undone, and repaired before execution continues, without recovery demonstrations or base-policy retraining. Across 33 real garments from six categories, FoldBack achieves 75.2% final folding success and 0.837 final-mask IoU, versus 45.7% and 0.689 for the strongest prior baseline.

</details>


### [240] [LLA-MPPI: Rapidly Adaptive Whole-body Control of Legged Robots with GPU-Accelerated Parallel Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10465)
*Sebin Jung,Maitham F. AL-Sunni,Juan Alvarez-Padilla,Zachary Manchester,Changliu Liu,John M. Dolan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LLA-MPPI：将全身自适应转化为对一组GPU并行接触仿真器（不同物理/结构参数）的选择，用滑动窗口预测误差挑选最解释近期运动的模型，再由MPPI在该模型上优化控制；无需离线训练，假设可解释。四个仿真任务成功率97.5%（最强基线74%，真模型oracle 98.5%），并在Unitree Go2上验证了中途加载、单腿失效、推箱同时增重等场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腿足机器人全身控制器通常基于固定名义模型规划，实际动力学变化时性能退化；自适应方法要么依赖接触动力学难以提供的模型结构，要么需针对每种预期工况离线训练。

Method: LLA-MPPI：维护一组参数（物理或结构）不同的接触仿真器，GPU批量并行前向；通过滑动窗口的预测误差选择最能解释近期运动的模型（look-back），再由全身MPPI规划器在所选模型上优化控制（look-ahead）。不需要离线训练，被选假设具有物理可解释性。

Result: 四个仿真任务平均成功率97.5%，最强基线74%，使用真实模型的oracle为98.5%；Unitree Go2真机验证：运行中加载载荷后继续行走、单腿禁用后行走、推箱入目标同时在线增加箱体质量。

Conclusion: 无需离线训练、可解释的在线模型选择与MPPI结合，可在动力学/结构突变下实现鲁棒的全身腿足控制，仿真接近oracle水平且有真机演示支撑。

Abstract: Real-time whole-body controllers for legged robots typically plan through a fixed nominal model and degrade when the deployed dynamics change. Adaptive methods typically require a model structure that contact dynamics do not provide, or they need offline training for each anticipated condition. We present Look-back and Look-ahead Adaptive Model Predictive Path Integral control (LLA-MPPI). The method converts whole-body adaptation into selection over a bank of GPU-batched contact simulators with different physical or structural parameters. Windowed prediction errors select the simulator that best explains recent motion. A whole-body MPPI planner optimizes controls through the selected model. The framework requires no offline training, and its selected hypotheses are physically interpretable. Across four simulated tasks, it achieves 97.5% success while the strongest baseline reaches 74% and an oracle with the true model reaches 98.5%. Hardware validation on a Unitree Go2 shows the robot walking under a payload added mid-run, walking after one leg is disabled, and pushing a box to its goal while increasing its mass on the fly. Code, videos, and project details are available at: this https URL

</details>


### [241] [CMP-IRRT*: A Perception-Assisted Height-Adaptive Planner for Quadruped Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10470)
*Mingfan Zhao,Wendong Mao,Zhongfeng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 CMP-IRRT* 感知辅助高度自适应规划框架，用 Channel Mamba PointNet 引导 Informed RRT*，实现四足机器人对低障碍的可通行穿越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 2D 规划流水线多将障碍建模为二值占据区域，无法利用可通行的低矮障碍，且在有限采样预算下采样搜索效率低。作者希望借助俯视 RGB 感知获得地面相对高度图，并引入神经引导加速采样，从而在保守障碍假设之外挖掘更短路径。

Method: 顶层为标定后的俯视 RGB 输入，感知模块估计障碍区域并将深度预测转换为地面相对高度图；规划器执行高度条件化碰撞检测（高障碍视为阻塞、低障碍允许穿越），用 CMP（Channel Mamba PointNet）引导采样偏向可行区域，同时保留标准自由空间与 informed sampling 兜底策略。

Result: 在 2D 规划基准上，相比经典与神经引导基线减少探索节点数与迭代次数；受控消融验证 Mamba 引导模块的贡献；在构造的可通行场景中，低障碍可穿越时路径长度最多缩短 16.3%；Unitree Go2 实机演示了绕行与穿越行为；代码开源。

Conclusion: 高度条件化规划结合神经引导可在采样预算受限条件下兼顾效率与路径质量；但评测以 2D 基准与单一实机示范为主，缺少大规模真实环境的定量部署数据与地形泛化评估。

Abstract: Quadruped robots can traverse low obstacles, but many 2D planning pipelines still model obstacles as binary occupied regions and rely on sampling-based search that can be inefficient under a limited budget. We propose a perception-assisted height-adaptive planning framework based on CMP-IRRT*, a Channel Mamba PointNet-guided Informed RRT* planner. Given a calibrated top-view RGB observation, the perception module estimates obstacle regions and converts depth predictions into a ground-relative height map. The planner then performs height-conditioned collision checking, treating high obstacles as blocked while allowing low obstacles to be traversed, and uses the CMP guide to bias sampling toward promising regions while retaining standard free-space and informed sampling fallbacks. Experiments on 2D planning benchmarks show that CMP-IRRT* reduces explored nodes and iterations compared with classical and neural-guided baselines, and a controlled ablation supports the contribution of the Mamba-based guide. In constructed traversability-aware scenarios, the proposed planner reduces path length by up to 16.3% when low obstacles are traversable, and a Unitree Go2 demonstration further shows executable bypassing and traversal behaviors.
